Trained on RSOC Building and evaluated directly on DOTA Large Vehicle, Small Vehicle, and Ship, COBICount achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) averaged over the target domains among the compared methods, 174.132. It uses 5.07 million parameters and 17.41 billion floating point operations for a 512x512 input. COBICount improves transfer without target data or training for each target. The code will be available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [105] [EgoTools: Towards Tool-Centric Reasoning in Real-World Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39378)
*Shulin Tian,Junsu Kim,Shuai Liu,Hao Li,Yujiao Shen,Sihan Li,Zhe Yang,Yeongon Kim,Feiyu Li,Jialin Wu,Yichi Zhang,Wenhui Wang,Runmao Yao,Yuhao Dong,Zhaoxi Chen,Fangzhou Hong,Antonino Furnari,Jingkang Yang,Hongyuan Zhu,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTools：首个以工具使用为中心的第一人称具身理解套件，含100小时多模态数据（EgoTools-Data）与1000题四轨诊断基准（EgoTools-Bench），揭示当前多模态视频模型在工具可供性与几何、程序、因果推理上的显著短板，并验证该数据可用于训练提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实具身任务高度依赖工具中介行为，需推理可供性、手-工具-物几何、程序进展与因果效应；但现有视频多模态模型在感知类任务上表现虽好，工具中心的具身推理仍受限，主因是缺乏真实第一人称数据与诊断基准。

Method: 构建EgoTools套件：EgoTools-Data为100小时工具中心第一人称录制，含同步音频、密集描述、推理型叙述与补充3D信息；EgoTools-Bench为1000对问答，覆盖感知与定位、几何、程序、因果四条赛道。并在严格源视频分离下用该数据对Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct做全监督微调以验证训练价值。

Result: Gemini-3.1-Pro总体准确率66.9%，但感知与定位仅51.7%，说明模型难以将工具使用扎根于视觉证据；全监督微调使Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在完整1000题上从50.0%提升至60.9%。

Conclusion: EgoTools既是训练资源也是诊断评估基准，统一支撑真实第一人称工具使用理解的训练与评测，并指出现有模型在工具中心具身推理上的关键差距。

Abstract: Real-world embodied tasks, from everyday activities to professional procedures, require agents to act under physical constraints while tracking evolving object and task states. Tool use sits at the heart of such tasks, as many everyday and professional activities are tool-mediated. Understanding them requires reasoning about affordances, hand-tool-object geometry, procedural progress, and causal effects on target objects. Yet despite strong performance on perception-oriented video tasks such as captioning and general video QA, current multimodal video models remain limited in this form of tool-centric embodied reasoning. Progress in this direction has been limited by the lack of real-world egocentric data and diagnostic benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce EgoTools, the first comprehensive suite for egocentric tool-use understanding. It consists of two complementary components: EgoTools-Data, a large-scale corpus of 100 hours of tool-centric egocentric recordings with synchronized audio, dense captions, reasoning-heavy narrations, and supplementary 3D information; and EgoTools-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,000 QA pairs across four tracks that cover tool-use understanding from perception and geometry to procedure and causal reasoning. Experimental results show that current models still struggle to ground tool use in visual evidence: Gemini-3.1-Pro achieves 66.9% overall accuracy but only 51.7% on Perception & Grounding. Beyond evaluation, we validate EgoTools-Data as a training resource. On the full 1,000-question benchmark, full supervised fine-tuning improves Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 50.0% to 60.9%, under strict source-video separation. Together, these results establish EgoTools as a unified resource for both training and diagnostic evaluation of real-world egocentric tool-use understanding.

</details>


### [106] [InfoAgent: Traceable Generation and Repair of Evidence-Grounded Infographics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39380)
*Yifan Li,Tong Li,Qi Zeng,Lishuai Gao,Ruwei Pan,Cong Wei,Shaohua Kevin Zhou,Zhuoliang Kang,Xiaoming Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 InfoAgent 框架，将信息图生成建模为"证据绑定的视觉-符号程序合成"，通过带类型的依赖图（IVD）记录事实、证据来源、执行路径与验证义务，支持分层执行与依赖感知的局部修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息图生成需要事实、符号与视觉关系在渲染和修订过程中保持一致；修改单个元素时还需追踪其支撑证据与受影响的依赖关系，现有方法缺乏这种一致性保障与可定位修复能力。

Method: 免训练框架 InfoAgent：1）Infographic Visual Description（IVD）以带类型依赖图记录事实载荷、证据出处、执行路线与验证义务；2）检索设计先验引导编译；3）分层执行，结合栅格合成与可编辑的符号/绑定对象并保留其轨迹；4）依赖感知修复：定位修改点、复检受影响依赖，并要求受保护义务在指定检查器下保持满足，未解决的义务显式暴露。

Result: 在 IGenBench 上达到 93.0 Q-ACC、59.0 I-ACC；新提出 InfoGraphicBench-Evidence，200 条测试请求的完整清单通过率由 Same-IVD Prompt 的 21.5% 提升至初始分层输出的 23.5%，修复后达 28.5%（使用相同证据与初始 IVD）；在 120 个审计修复案例中，局部修复平均仅修改画布 12.4%，而全局重生成修改 67.3%。

Conclusion: 以证据绑定 + 符号依赖图为核心的免训练框架可同时提升信息图生成的正确率与可修订性，局部依赖感知修复在保证验证义务的前提下显著降低编辑代价。

Abstract: Reliable infographic generation requires facts, symbols, and visual relations to remain consistent through rendering and revision. Correcting one element also requires tracking its supporting evidence and the dependencies affected by the change. We present \textbf{InfoAgent}, a training-free framework for \emph{evidence-bound visual-symbolic program synthesis}. Its Infographic Visual Description (IVD) records factual payloads, evidence provenance, execution routes, and verification obligations in a typed dependency graph. Retrieved design priors guide compilation, and layered execution combines raster synthesis with editable symbolic and binding objects while retaining their traces. Dependency-aware repair localizes corrections, rechecks affected dependencies, and requires protected obligations to remain satisfied under the declared checkers. Unresolved obligations remain explicit. On IGenBench, InfoAgent achieves 93.0 Q-ACC and 59.0 I-ACC. We also introduce InfoGraphicBench-Evidence, where complete-checklist pass rates on 200 test requests increase from 21.5\% for Same-IVD Prompt to 23.5\% for the initial layered output and 28.5\% after repair, using the same evidence and initial IVD. On 120 audited repair cases, localized repair edits 12.4\% of the canvas on average, compared with 67.3\% for global regeneration.

</details>


### [107] [PCB-MC: Missing Component Analysis in Printed Circuit Boards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39427)
*Betsy Villa Brochero,Ian Gibson,Estefania Talavera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PCB-MC数据集，用于印刷电路板缺件检测，采用板级类型感知交叉验证防止布局泄漏，并给出监督与无监督方法的基准结果，表明该任务仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺件检测与常规目标检测本质不同：模型需定位“不存在”的元件。现有评价协议未考虑同一PCB设计在多张图像中的布局重复，导致训练-测试间布局泄漏，指标虚高，缺少能揭示真实泛化能力的基准。

Method: 基于RF100构建PCB-MC数据集，含197种不同板型设计，每种板型配多张增强样本，标注粒度到footprint（封装）级别；提出板型感知交叉验证划分，按板型隔离以避免布局泄漏；在统一协议下评测多类监督检测方法与无监督异常检测方法。

Result: 监督模型在未见过的板型设计上假负率（漏检率）很高；无监督异常检测方法因缺乏与板型专属参考图的空间对齐而完全失效。两类方法在跨板型泛化上均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 跨多样PCB布局的缺件检测仍是未解决的问题，尤其是“结构性缺失”检测的泛化能力不足。作者公开PCB-MC数据集与全部训练协议以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Detecting missing components on printed circuit boards (PCBs) differs fundamentally from conventional object detection, as the model must localize components that are not present. We introduce PCB-MC, a curated dataset for missing component detection with footprint level annotations built on top of the RF100 dataset. The dataset contains 197 distinct board types, each corresponding to a unique PCB design, with multiple augmented samples per type. We also provide benchmark results on PCB-MC by evaluating a diverse set of supervised and unsupervised methods. To ensure fair evaluation, we propose board type aware cross validation splits that prevent layout leakage between training and test sets. Supervised models showcase high false negative rates on unseen board designs, and unsupervised anomaly detection methods fail entirely due to the lack of spatial alignment with a board specific reference. These results confirm that missing component detection on diverse PCB layouts remains an open challenge. We release PCB-MC and all training protocols to support reproducible research on structural absence detection in industrial inspection.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Trustworthy AI for Glioma Diagnosis: A Task-Aware Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39429)
*Gonzalo Esteban Mosquera Rojas,Sebastian R. van der Voort,Carolin M. Pirkl,Sandeep Kaushik,Marion Smits,Stefan Klein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估多任务深度学习框架（脑胶质瘤分割+IDH/1p19q分级预测）中的不确定性量化，对比MCD、DE、MCDE并分析校准、错误检测与选择性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险医学影像分析需要可信AI，而多任务框架下的不确定性量化缺乏任务感知的系统评估，尤其是分割与分类不确定性之间的相互作用尚不明确。

Method: 在MRI脑胶质瘤多任务网络中采用Monte Carlo Dropout进行预测/偶然/认知不确定性的分解分析，评估MC采样收敛、校准、错误检测、选择性预测及体素级不确定性聚合对病例级可靠性的影响，并与Deep Ensembles、MC Deep Ensembles对比，同时检验分割质量与分类的交互及复合信任分数。

Result: 不确定性估计在各任务上有助于错误检测；校准依赖dropout率，中等速率最可靠；不确定性分解提供任务相关的可解释性，但未稳定优于单一预测不确定性；DE与MCDE操作效用相当，无方法全面占优；复合信任分数未稳定优于分类不确定性。

Conclusion: 提出任务感知的不确定性评估策略，为可信脑胶质瘤诊断AI提供实践指导，但未提出新的不确定性建模方法。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) is a key requirement for trustworthy AI in high-stakes medical image analysis. In this work, we evaluate UQ in a multi-task Deep Learning framework for MRI-based glioma diagnosis that performs tumor segmentation and predicts IDH mutation status, 1p/19q co-deletion status, and tumor grade. Monte Carlo Dropout (MCD) is used for a detailed task-aware analysis of predictive, aleatoric, and epistemic uncertainty. We assess MC sample convergence, calibration, error detection, selective prediction, associations with segmentation performance, and the effect of voxel-wise uncertainty aggregation on case-level reliability. We also compare MCD with Deep Ensembles (DE) and Monte Carlo Deep Ensembles (MCDE), examine interactions between segmentation quality and classification, and evaluate a composite trust score integrating segmentation and classification uncertainty. Across tasks, uncertainty estimates supported meaningful error detection, while calibration depended on the dropout rate, with moderate rates yielding the most reliable probabilities. Uncertainty decomposition provided task-dependent interpretability but did not consistently improve error detection over predictive uncertainty alone. DE and MCDE showed comparable operational utility, with no method consistently dominating across tasks and metrics. The composite trust score did not consistently outperform classification uncertainty for selective prediction. Overall, our results provide a task-aware evaluation strategy and practical guidance for the development of trustworthy AI for glioma diagnosis.

</details>


### [109] [CAST: Causal Advantage-Structured Training with Spatially Grounded Compositional Rewards for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39441)
*Shu Yu,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAST：面向预训练扩散模型的在线RL微调框架，用去噪轨迹自动确定SDE窗口、以因果场景图分解原子奖励并投影到像素空间加权策略目标，在FLUX.2-dev与Qwen-Image-2512上提升组合生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线RL+flow matching的DM生成范式存在三点不足：采样窗口需人工设定；基于人类标注训练的奖励模型在最新SOTA模型上分数极高、难以区分，优势估计失效；单一标量奖励把不同失败模式压成近似相同分数，梯度引导微弱。

Method: 提出CAST（Causal Advantage-Structured Training）：(1) 从各模型去噪轨迹中识别其固定物体及其空间排布的步骤，据此设定SDE注入窗口；(2) 用因果场景图（CSG）将提示分解为可独立校验的原子（物体、数量、属性、空间关系）并分别给奖励；(3) 通过teacher-forced attention把带符号的原子级优势投影到像素空间，对SDE策略目标做空间加权。

Result: 在FLUX.2-dev与Qwen-Image-2512上微调，于组合基准GenEval 2和整体质量基准Qwen-Image-Bench评测：几乎相同训练预算下，在最具挑战的GenEval 2提示上相对基座模型的提升最高达Flow-GRPO的3.07倍，整体生成质量亦提升。

Conclusion: 面向扩散模型在线RL的窗口选择、奖励饱和、样本低效可通过轨迹驱动窗口、原子级因果奖励分解与优势像素投射协同缓解，组合式文生图能力与整体质量可同时改善。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning has been extended to flow matching for diffusion model (DM) image generation. However, this paradigm faces three limitations: (1) Window selection. Existing methods manually set the stochastic differential equation (SDE) sampling window, i.e., the denoising steps where exploration noise is injected. We instead determine it from each model's denoising trajectory. (2) Reward saturation. Current methods rely on scoring models trained on human annotations; we find that such scores are extremely high and nearly indistinguishable on the latest SOTA open-source DMs, making advantage estimation largely ineffective. (3) Sample inefficiency. A single scalar reward collapses different failure modes into almost identical scores, leaving minimal gradient guidance for targeted improvement. To address these issues, we propose CAST (Causal Advantage-Structured Training), an RL fine-tuning method for pretrained DMs, which (1) identifies the denoising step at which each model fixes the objects and their spatial arrangement in the image and uses that timing to set the SDE window, (2) decomposes each prompt via Causal Scene Graphs (CSG) into verifiable-atoms, i.e., minimal semantic units such as an object, count, attribute, or spatial relation that can each be checked independently, and rewards each atom separately, and (3) projects the signed atom-level advantages into pixel space through teacher-forced attention and uses them to spatially weight the SDE policy objective. We fine-tune two of the strongest open-source DMs, FLUX.2-dev and Qwen-Image-2512, with CAST, and evaluate them on GenEval 2, a compositional benchmark, and on Qwen-Image-Bench for overall quality. Within almost the same training budget, CAST's improvement over the base model on the most challenging GenEval 2 prompts is up to 3.07x that of Flow-GRPO, while overall generation quality also improves.

</details>


### [110] [ResARC: Residual-Aware AutoRegressive Coding for Ultra-Low Bitrate Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39451)
*Qin Yan,Ruixiao Dong,Yutao Xie,Li Li,Ying Chen,Kai Li,Daowen Li,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResARC：残差感知自回归图像编解码器，在解码端显式补偿量化残差与生成残差，实现超低码率下的高分布保真生成式压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进式自回归图像编解码器在量化离散token与自回归生成后缀token两条路径上分别引入量化残差与生成残差，二者叠加从根本上限制了重建质量，现有方法未显式建模与补偿这两类残差。

Method: 在解码端显式补偿两类残差：(1) 以自回归解码上下文为条件，用扩散Transformer生成量化残差，无需额外边信息；(2) 在编码端计算生成残差，并通过可学习的Generation Residual Codec压缩传输，用于解码端校正；两类残差与重建潜表示整合后经适配的VAE解码器解码。

Result: 在超低码率区间，相比主流生成式编解码器显著提升分布保真度（分布相似性），同时保持有竞争力的感知相似度。

Conclusion: 通过残差分解与显式补偿可有效突破渐进式自回归编解码的质量瓶颈，为生成式压缩提供新的残差感知范式。

Abstract: Progressive autoregressive image codecs provide an appealing paradigm for generative compression by quantizing continuous latents into discrete tokens, transmitting coarse-to-fine prefix tokens and generating the remaining suffix tokens at the decoder. However, their reconstruction quality is fundamentally limited by two residuals introduced along this pipeline: the quantization residual, arising from information loss during discrete tokenization, and the generation residual, resulting from imperfect autoregressive generation of the suffix tokens. To address these limitations, we introduce ResARC, a residual-aware autoregressive codec that explicitly compensates for both residuals at the decoder. Specifically, we generate the quantization residual with a diffusion transformer conditioned on the autoregressive decoding context, while requiring no additional side information. In parallel, we compute the generation residual at the encoder and employ a learned Generation Residual Codec to efficiently compress and transmit it for decoder-side correction. The recovered residuals are then integrated with the reconstructed latent representation and decoded through an adapted VAE decoder. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ResARC achieves competitive perceptual similarity while substantially improving distributional fidelity over leading generative codecs across the ultra-low bitrate regime. Code and models will be released soon.

</details>


### [111] [DensePed-Lite: Quality-Aware Adaptive Detection for Dense Pedestrians under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39467)
*ZiAn Wang,MingZhe Liu,Chaoyi Guo,ChangChun Li,Fangming Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DensePed-Lite 轻量行人检测框架，以"遮挡下网络应依据观测质量自适应"为核心原则，通过 UQE、MPSC、CTDM 三个机制分别解决置信度不可靠、空间覆盖碎片化与多尺度融合不一致问题，在 CityPersons 与 CrowdHuman 上取得更优的精度-效率折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集场景下的行人检测面临严重遮挡、剧烈尺度变化和严苛实时性要求；现有轻量检测器难以兼顾精度与效率，且普遍忽视质量感知的特征建模以及分类与定位的一致性，导致拥挤场景性能不稳定。

Method: 以单一原则"遮挡时网络应依据其观测内容的质量自适应，而非假设信息完整"构建统一框架，并在遮挡危害最大的三个环节落地为对应模块：不可靠置信度评分（UQE）、碎片化空间覆盖（MPSC）、不一致的多尺度融合（CTDM）；三机制相互增强且不显著增加模型复杂度。

Result: 在 CityPersons 与 CrowdHuman 数据集上，DensePed-Lite 相较近期最先进的轻量方法实现了更优的精度-效率权衡，适合密集行人场景的实时部署。

Conclusion: DensePed-Lite 表明围绕"观测质量自适应"统一设计轻量检测器，可在不牺牲效率的前提下提升遮挡密集场景的检测稳定性与综合性能。

Abstract: Pedestrian detection plays a crucial role in computer vision with applications in autonomous driving, surveillance, and public safety. However, real-world dense scenes bring severe challenges, including heavy occlusion, drastic scale variations, and strict real-time requirements. Existing lightweight detectors struggle to balance accuracy and efficiency while often neglecting quality-aware feature modeling and consistency between classification and localization, leading to unstable performance under crowded conditions. To address these issues, we propose DensePed-Lite, a unified framework built on a single principle: under occlusion the network should adapt its behavior to the quality of what it observes rather than assume complete information. This principle is realized at three points where occlusion does the most damage: unreliable confidence scoring (UQE), fragmented spatial coverage (MPSC), and incoherent multi-scale fusion (CTDM). The three mechanisms reinforce one another instead of acting in isolation, all without significantly increasing complexity. Experiments on CityPersons and CrowdHuman validate that DensePed-Lite achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off compared with recent state-of-the-art lightweight methods, making it suitable for real-time deployment in dense pedestrian scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [From Wrecks to Wisdom: Recovering Crash Mechanics from Real-World Multi-View Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39486)
*Ondřej Valach,Václav Diviš,Ivan Gruber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用事故后车辆多视角照片，通过共享视觉骨干与多视角特征融合，监督预测CDC碰撞变形描述符与ΔV纵向/横向分量，建立基于不完整多视角证据的视觉碰撞力学估计评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实事故记录中碰撞构型、主受力方向、速度变化ΔV等关键元数据常缺失、延迟或损坏，而事故后照片广泛可得且包含变形视觉证据，因此需要研究在无结构化信号时能从照片中恢复多少碰撞力学信息。

Method: 将碰撞理解建模为逐案例多视角照片集合的监督预测；每张照片经共享视觉骨干编码，视角级特征融合为案例级表示，再由目标专用头预测六个CDC描述符及ΔV纵/横向分量；数据来自Crash Investigation Sampling System，15.2k训练案例（过滤前约150万张照片）、1.15k验证、1.15k测试，并定义评测协议。

Result: 仅靠事故后图像即可为多个非平凡碰撞力学描述符提供可用信号，弱可观测与长尾目标仍具挑战；联合训练配方将主受力方向平均绝对角度误差从20.1°降至14.05°，纵向ΔV的MAE从8.04降至7.45 km/h。

Conclusion: 该工作为视觉碰撞描述符估计提供了参考基准，并为后续与结构化碰撞元数据的多模态融合奠定基础。

Abstract: Estimating accident mechanics from real-world crashes is important for vehicle-safety analysis, injury modeling, crash-severity prediction, and operational workflows such as insurance claim triage. In standard crash records, key metadata such as impact configuration, principal direction of force, and change in velocity ($\Delta V$) may be missing, delayed, or corrupted, while post-crash photographs are widely available and contain rich visual evidence of deformation. We study how much crash-mechanics information can be recovered directly from vehicle photos when structured signals are absent. We formulate crash understanding as supervised prediction from per-case multi-view photo sets. Targets include six Collision Deformation Classification (CDC) descriptors and the longitudinal and lateral components of reconstructed $\Delta V$. Each photo is encoded by a shared visual backbone, and the resulting view-level features are fused into a case-level representation from which target-specific heads predict crash descriptors. Using 15.2k training cases from the Crash Investigation Sampling System, drawn from about 1.5M photos before filtering, together with 1.15k validation and 1.15k test cases, we define an evaluation protocol for vision-based crash descriptor estimation from incomplete multi-view evidence. Post-crash imagery alone provides usable signal for several non-trivial crash-mechanics descriptors, while weakly observable and long-tailed targets remain challenging. Within the compared training regimes, the selected joint-training recipe reduces mean absolute angular error for principal direction of force from 20.1 to 14.05 degrees and longitudinal $\Delta V$ MAE from 8.04 to 7.45 km/h. Our work provides a reference point for future multimodal fusion with structured crash metadata.

</details>


### [113] [OmniReasoning: Pushing the Limits of Audio-Visual Joint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39490)
*Junming Lin,Yuxuan Wang,Zhenxin Lei,Yuxin Liu,Ruixun Liu,Yinsong Yan,Ling Wang,Minghao Han,Yunfei Chu,Shun Lei,Xueyao Zhang,Qize Yang,Jin Xu,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向全模态联合推理的基准OmniReasoningBench、数据引擎OmniQA与学习法MFSD，训练出OmniReasoning-30B-A3B并显著提升音视频联合推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态模型在训练数据、评测基准与学习方法上基本割裂处理音频、视觉与语言，音视频联合推理能力既缺乏评测也被低效激发，缺少"音频与视觉证据缺一不可"的任务设定与对应数据。

Method: 1) OmniReasoningBench：含1150道多选与开放题，覆盖"视频内推理"与"超越视频推理"两类任务，要求音视频证据同时必要；2) OmniQA数据引擎：自动构造证据锚定、必须依赖音视频联合推理的QA对及带时间戳的线索链，用于标注思维过程，产出OmniReasoning-SFT-112K与OmniReasoning-RL-19K；3) Modality-Factored Self-Distillation（MFSD）：在线自蒸馏，在模态特定线索上下文下评估每个采样响应，解耦各单模态线索贡献与跨模态交互，实现token级信用分配。

Result: OmniReasoning-30B-A3B在OmniVideoBench达50.0%、OmniReasoningBench达42.5%，较基座Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Thinking分别提升12.8与9.3个百分点；在通用与长视频基准（含Video-MME-v2）上亦有显著增益。

Conclusion: 以基准+数据引擎+自蒸馏学习方法的组合方式，系统性补齐全模态联合推理的评测与训练缺口，为该方向后续研究提供可复用基线与数据基础。

Abstract: Recent advances have enabled unified omni-modal models in understanding audio, vision, and language. However, existing benchmarks, training data, and learning methods largely treat the modalities independently, leaving the capability of audio-visual joint reasoning poorly evaluated and insufficiently elicited. We address this gap with a benchmark, data engine, and learning method. First, we introduce OmniReasoningBench, a benchmark where both audio and visual evidence are indispensable. It comprises 1,150 multiple-choice and open-ended questions across two tasks, reasoning over video and reasoning beyond video. Second, we develop a data engine OmniQA. It automatically constructs evidence-grounded QA pairs that explicitly necessitate audio-visual joint reasoning, together with time-stamped clue chains that guide the annotation of thinking process. Besides our benchmark, this engine produces training data OmniReasoning-SFT-112K and OmniReasoning-RL-19K. Finally, we propose an on-policy self-distillation method Modality-Factored Self-Distillation (MFSD). It evaluates each sampled response under modality-specific clue contexts, disentangling the contributions of individual clues and their cross-modal interactions for token-level credit assignment. With our training data and learning method, our model OmniReasoning-30B-A3B achieves 50.0% on OmniVideoBench and 42.5% on OmniReasoningBench, improving the base model Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Thinking by 12.8 and 9.3 percentage points, respectively. Moreover, it delivers substantial gains on general and long-video benchmarks, including Video-MME-v2. We hope our work offers a solid step for facilitating future research in omni-modal joint reasoning.

</details>


### [114] [Front-to-Back: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Asymmetric Cross-View Vehicle Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39492)
*Moseli Mots'oehli,Thulani Babeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Front2Back-ReID 基准（500 例人工校验的前后摄像头车辆交接），系统评测 7 个零样本 VLM、4 个图像检索基线与 25 名人类在跨视角车辆重识别上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前摄像头与后摄像头不共享视野，车辆外观在跨视角间变化显著，导致同一车辆匹配困难；现有工作缺乏针对该场景的评测基准，也不清楚通用 VLM 是否优于强视觉检索方法。

Method: 构建 Front2Back-ReID 基准，采集南非 20 段序列、人工校验 500 次车辆交接；每个样本要求模型将前摄像头中高亮的车辆与后摄像头图像中至少 3 个候选中的同一车辆匹配。对比 7 个零样本 VLM、4 个图像检索基线和 25 名人类被试；输入条件包括完整前视 RGB 图、裁剪目标车辆图和二值轮廓图，并对比开启推理与否两种模式。

Result: 最强 VLM 在目标裁剪上达 76.6% Rank-1，SigLIP2 冻结基线为 74.0%，差异不具统计显著性；人类在完整图像上达 94.0%，目标裁剪上 92.2%。开启推理在全部三种输入条件下均提升所有模型的准确率；VLM 在完整场景上普遍差于目标裁剪。

Conclusion: 通用 VLM 在前后摄像头车辆匹配上尚未稳定超越强视觉检索方法，人类可靠性仍显著更高；该基准揭示了跨视角车辆 ReID 的现有能力上限与推理机制的正向作用。

Abstract: Matching the same vehicle across front and rear cameras is difficult because the cameras do not share a view and the vehicle's appearance changes substantially. We introduce Front2Back-ReID, a benchmark of 500 manually verified vehicle handovers from 20 recording sequences in South Africa. Each example asks a model to match a vehicle highlighted in a front-camera image to the same vehicle among at least three candidates in a later rear-camera image. We evaluate seven zero-shot vision-language models, four image-retrieval baselines, and 25 human participants. Models are tested using full front RGB images, cropped target vehicles, and binary silhouettes. The strongest VLM achieved 76.6 percent Rank-1 accuracy on target crops, compared with 74.0 percent for the frozen SigLIP2 baseline; this difference was not statistically clear. Human participants achieved 94.0 percent accuracy with full images and 92.2 percent with target crops. Under our evaluation setup, enabling reasoning improved accuracy across all three input conditions for every model evaluated in both modes. We also found that VLMs generally performed worse on full scenes than on target crops. These results show that general-purpose VLMs do not yet consistently outperform strong visual retrieval for front-to-rear vehicle matching, while humans remain substantially more reliable.

</details>


### [115] [PartiCam: Camera Controlled Video Generation with Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39504)
*Amine Ouasfi,Runjia Li,Junlin Han,Eric Marchand,Philip H.S. Torr,Adnane Boukhayma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PartiCam，一种免训练、基于粒子滤波的采样引导方法，通过全局-局部细化框架（基于粒子滤波重采样的局部细化阶段）提升扩散视频生成中的相机轨迹控制精度与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频扩散模型难以严格遵循指定的相机轨迹。免训练引导方法虽不依赖相机标注数据集且与骨干模型无关，但现有采样引导存在轨迹不稳定问题：或探索过广而无法贴合目标相机运动，或过早坍缩而丧失视觉多样性。该类方法还能为后续训练相机条件视频扩散模型生成带相机控制的视频数据。

Method: 在 Sequential Monte-Carlo (SMC) 引导基础上引入全局-局部细化框架：全局阶段进行扩散奖励引导，局部阶段基于粒子滤波重采样进行细化，以纠正轨迹漂移；无需重新训练模型，与骨干无关。

Result: 实验显示在相机轨迹贴合度上大幅提升，漂移减少，视觉质量更好，且无需模型重训练。

Conclusion: PartiCam 通过粒子滤波重采样的局部细化增强了 SMC 奖励引导，实现更准确的相机控制视频生成，并可产出用于训练相机条件扩散模型的数据；属于免训练、与骨干无关的引导式视频生成方法。

Abstract: We present PartiCam, a training-free Particle filtering rooted method for improved Camera controlled video generation. Generating videos that follow a precisely specified camera trajectory remains challenging for large video diffusion models. Training-free approaches are backbone-agnostic and avoid the need to construct large camera-annotated datasets by steering pretrained models toward the desired camera motion at test time. This enables the generation of camera-controlled video data that can subsequently be used to train camera-conditioned video diffusion models. Existing sampling-based guidance approaches often suffer from unstable trajectories: they either explore too broadly and fail to respect the target camera motion or collapse early and lose visual diversity over time. We introduce a global-local refinement framework for diffusion reward guidance, enabling accurate and consistent camera control during video generation. Our method builds on Sequential Monte-Carlo (SMC) guidance, but introduces a local refinement stage based on particle filtered resampling. Experiments show large improvements in camera trajectory adherence, reduced drift, and better visual quality, without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [116] [Lens Flare Removal and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39527)
*Tarun Yenamandra,Jonathon Luiten,Daniel Cremers,Nathan Matsuda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个“大尺寸镜头光晕（lens flare）移除与显式重建”框架：构建真实数据+程序化生成的配对大光晕数据集，微调扩散模型实现大光晕去除；并提出基于相机主点对称性的显式光晕表示，与3DGS联合优化实现场景/光晕分解，所得光晕可重渲染、编辑并迁移到新视角与新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜头光晕是相机成像系统的固有属性而非场景内容，会严重损害3D场景重建等下游任务。现有方法仅能处理光源周围的小范围光晕，对充满整幅图像的大光晕失效；同时光晕在影视媒体中是重要的艺术表现手段，而跨多视角一致地表示与重建光晕尚未被探索。故论文同时解决“大光晕去除”与“光晕可分解、可编辑重建”两个问题。

Method: 1) 构建大光晕移除数据集：融合公开真实数据与程序化生成管线获得配对数据；在其上微调扩散模型完成复杂大光晕去除。2) 提出利用光晕关于相机主点对称性的显式光晕表示模型。3) 提出联合优化该光晕模型与3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的计算管线，借助光晕去除模型将3D场景分解为“光晕”与“纯净场景”两部分。4) 由于重建光晕是显式且可重渲染的，支持编辑并迁移至新图像与新的3D场景。5) 在既有基准与面向大反射光晕的新基准上评估，直接量化光晕/场景分解效果，并验证对自动光源定位误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在已有基准和新增的大反射光晕基准上完成了去除效果评估；直接量化了光晕/场景分解质量；实验表明该管线对自动光源定位中的误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 论文将镜头光晕从“需被消除的成像伪影”重新定位为“可显式表示、可重渲染、可编辑并可跨视角迁移的场景组成成分”，为大光晕去除与光晕-场景分解提供了数据集、显式表示与联合优化管线，并具备跨视角一致性与对光源定位噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The presence of lens flares in images can significantly reduce the quality of downstream application results for tasks such as 3D scene reconstruction. This is because lens flares are a property of the camera imaging system, and not a part of the underlying scene being modeled. There are previous methods that tackle the removal of small flares focused around a light source. However, existing methods struggle with large flares, such as those that fill the entire image. In this work, we compile a novel dataset for large-flare removal, combining publicly available real-world data with a procedural generation pipeline. We fine-tune a diffusion-based model on our dataset to remove complex, large lens flares. On the other hand, lens flares remain effective artistic tools, widely used in the media. While there are ways to simulate 2D flares, representing and reconstructing lens flares consistently across multiple views has not yet been explored. To achieve this, we introduce a flare representation model that leverages the symmetry of lens flares about the camera's principal point. We propose a computational pipeline to jointly optimize this flare model and a Gaussian splatting model (3DGS). This enables the decomposition of a 3D scene into lens flares and the scene itself, using our flare-removal model. Because the reconstructed flare is explicit and re-renderable, it can be edited and transferred to novel images and new 3D scenes. We evaluate removal on an established benchmark and a new one for large reflective flares, quantify the flare/scene decomposition directly, and show that the pipeline is robust to errors in automatic light-source localization.

</details>


### [117] [Comparative study of adapting pre-trained models for driving behavior video captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39542)
*Sayak Mallick,Philipp Geiger,Augustin Kelava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告在自动驾驶视频数据集上将LLM SpaceTimeGPT进行全量微调，并对比LoRA与提示工程（VideoLLaVA），以低维理解驾驶情境，在BDD-X的自动指标上取得与基线相当或更优的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已具备跨模态理解能力，但如何将其适配到自动驾驶这类需要低维情境表征的领域尚缺乏系统性比较，作者希望厘清不同微调与提示方法在该域的有效性与局限。

Method: 以SpaceTimeGPT为主测模型，在BDD-X自动驾驶视频数据集上执行全量微调；同时对VideoLLaVA尝试LoRA低秩适配与提示工程，比较各方法的表现并讨论其局限。

Result: 全量微调框架在部分自动指标上表现良好，甚至超过基线；LoRA与提示工程在VideoLLaVA上的效果受限，具体局限被讨论但未见显著提升。

Conclusion: 在自动驾驶视频理解中，全量微调目前优于LoRA与提示工程；后者在跨模态情境理解上仍有明显局限。

Abstract: This report examines and compares some of the many fine tuning and prompting methods existing, applying them within the domain of autonomous driving. The idea is to compare these methods by adapting a Large Language Model (LLM) on a video dataset. LLM's have become extremely good at achieving a good understanding of different forms of data and this study aims to induce a low dimensional understanding of driving situations into our primary test model SpaceTimeGPT. Experiments on BDD-X (Berkeley DeepDrive eXplanation) dataset demonstrate good performance of the full fine tuning framework on some automatic metrics, and in some metrics, it even surpasses the baseline. We also try Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and prompt engineering on VideoLLaVA model and discuss its limitations.

</details>


### [118] [Learning Normal Diffusion Dynamics for Backdoor Defense in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39548)
*Junjian Li,Xiaolong Liu,Peng Sun,Liantao Wu,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从扩散轨迹的转移动力学视角研究T2I扩散模型的后门防御，提出NDDL框架，仅用良性样本学习正常转移动力学，通过预测与观测轨迹的偏差检测后门并定位触发器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型后门防御依赖内部表征中的特定异常模式，随着攻击机制日益多样，这类方法的泛化性受限。

Method: 观察良性扩散轨迹在交叉注意力、潜空间与噪声空间中呈现结构化的、与时间步相关的转移模式，而后门攻击会破坏该演化。基于此，NDDL构造紧凑的多空间轨迹表示，训练时间步条件化的动力学模型预测扩散演化；推理阶段用观测与预测转移之间的偏差量化动力学不一致性以检测后门，并通过低语义词替换实现无需先验知识的触发器定位。

Result: 在多种后门攻击上的大量实验验证了NDDL的有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 从转移动力学角度建模正常扩散演化，为T2I扩散模型后门防御提供了一种通用且可定位触发器的新范式。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to the secure deployment of text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models. Existing defenses typically detect backdoors from specific abnormal patterns in internal representations, which may limit their generalizability with the emergence of increasingly diverse attack mechanisms. In this paper, we study backdoor defense of T2I diffusion models from a transition-dynamics perspective. We observe that benign diffusion trajectories exhibit structured and timestep-dependent transition patterns from cross-attention, latent and noise spaces, whereas backdoor attacks tend to induce deviations from such normal evolution. Motivated by these observations, we propose Normal Diffusion Dynamics Learning (NDDL), a novel backdoor defense framework that learns the normal transition dynamics of diffusion trajectories utilizing only benign samples. NDDL constructs compact multi-space trajectory representations and trains a timestep-conditioned dynamics model to predict the diffusion evolution. In the inference phase, deviations between the observed and predicted transitions are exploited to quantify dynamics inconsistency for backdoor detection. NDDL further enables trigger localization without any prior knowledge of the embedded backdoor by performing substitution with low-semantic words. Extensive experiments for diverse backdoor attacks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our proposed NDDL.

</details>


### [119] [EffGS: Efficient and High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39553)
*Changbai Li,Shuo Yang,Yichen Yang,Shuwei Shao,Huobin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EffGS 提出一种通用 3DGS 加速框架，结合频率感知引导、局部密度控制与自适应基元尺度调制，在保持甚至提升重建质量的同时改善训练与渲染效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用 3DGS 加速方法在复杂大规模场景中扩展时会出现严重的渲染质量下降，因此需要一个在受限场景与大尺度场景下均可保持质量、同时提升效率的加速框架。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 重要度评分机制，将逐像素重建误差与随训练调度的 Difference-of-Gaussians 掩码结合，提供分阶段的空间引导；(2) 局部稠密化与剪枝，仅在采样视图中具有有效投影足迹的高斯上执行密度修改；(3) 可学习的逐高斯基元尺度调制，在保持 Compact Box 光栅化规则的前提下调整有效基元范围。

Result: 在受限场景与大尺度场景数据集上的大量实验表明，在重建质量、训练时间与基元数量之间取得了良好平衡；组件消融与匹配基元预算的对比进一步验证了框架各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: EffGS 是一种更具通用性的 3DGS 加速方案，可在有界与大规模场景中同时改善训练/渲染效率并维持或超越原始 3DGS 的重建质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis, but existing general-purpose acceleration methods suffer severe rendering quality degradation when extended to more complex, large-scale scenes. To address this issue, we propose EffGS, a more general acceleration framework that improves training and rendering efficiency while maintaining reconstruction quality comparable to or better than vanilla 3DGS across bounded and large-scale scenes. EffGS combines frequency-aware guidance, localized density control, and adaptive primitive scale modulation. First, an importance scoring mechanism combines pixel-wise reconstruction errors with a difference-of-Gaussians mask scheduled over training to provide stage-dependent spatial guidance. Second, localized densification and pruning restricts density modifications to Gaussians with valid projected footprints in the sampled views. Third, learnable per-Gaussian scale modulation adjusts effective primitive extent during optimization while retaining the Compact Box rasterization rule. Extensive experiments on bounded and large-scale scene datasets demonstrate a favorable balance between reconstruction quality, training time, and primitive count. Component ablations and matched-primitive-budget comparisons further support the effectiveness of the framework.

</details>


### [120] [RESUME: Recurrent State Updates from Motion and Residual Signals for Efficient Video Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39563)
*Can Zhang,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu,Ruirui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RESUME 提出一种带状态的编码器前端表示：I 帧初始化紧凑隐状态，后续 P 帧作为状态更新被递归吸收，共享读出层输出与 VideoLM 兼容的 token，使视频语言模型接收的是变化轨迹而非独立帧 token 集合，在三个时序推理基准上稳定超越 RGB 与 codec 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语言模型独立编码采样 RGB 帧，长视频要么耗尽 token 预算，要么丢失采样帧之间的变化；codec-aware 前端虽利用运动矢量与残差，但部署时每个预测帧仍被单独 token 化，token 只是当前原语（primitive）的函数，而非当前原语加携带参考（carried reference）的函数。作者认为更自然的函数应同时依赖二者：同一片段与其时间反演共享相同帧、仅变化顺序不同，而对称池化在设计上就丢弃了顺序轴，且该顺序信息在 VideoLM 所用冻结视觉特征中非空。

Method: 提出 RESUME：一种有状态的 codec 表示。锚点 I 帧初始化一个紧凑隐状态；每个后续 P 帧被作为对该状态的更新予以消费；共享读出层从累积状态中暴露 VideoLM 兼容的 token。由此把 codec 预测保持在表示层面，并以"轨迹"而非"独立 token 组"的形式交给语言模型。在与先前 codec-aware 方法相同的每 P 帧 token 预算下，P 帧进入 LM 时是对前端已知内容的读出，而非仅对当前原语的编码。另做了冻结转移测试（frozen-transition）以检验锚点依赖性、顺序敏感性及超出训练时域的有效 rollout 行为。

Result: 在十个基准上，增益集中在时序推理：三个时序基准（TempCompass、TOMATO、MVBench）上均超过 RGB 帧基线 LLaVA-Video-7B（分别 +2.8、+5.1、+3.9 分）与 codec 基线 CoPE-7B，同时在通用与长视频 QA 上保持竞争力。冻结转移实验显示锚点依赖、顺序敏感性以及超出训练时域的有效 rollout 行为。

Conclusion: 将 codec 递归在表示层显式建模为携带参考的累积状态，是让 VideoLM 获得时序/顺序感知的一条自然且低 token 开销的路径；temporal 增益与冻结转移诊断共同支持"轨迹式 token"优于"独立 token 组"的论点。

Abstract: Existing video language models encode sampled RGB frames independently, so a long video must either exhaust the token budget or drop the changes between sampled frames. Codec-aware front-ends read the motion vectors and residuals that encoding produced, but in their deployed form each predictive frame is still tokenized on its own: the tokens are a function of the current primitives, not of a carried reference. We argue that a more natural function is of both---the current primitives and a carried reference. A clip and its time reversal share the same frames and differ only in the order of changes---an axis that symmetric pooling discards by construction, and that is non-empty in the frozen vision features VideoLMs use---and the codec recurrence already composes those changes in order against a reference state. We introduce RESUME, a stateful codec representation: an anchor I-frame initializes a compact latent state, each subsequent predictive frame is consumed as an update to that state, and a shared readout exposes VideoLM-compatible tokens from the accumulated state. Codec prediction is thereby kept at the representation level and handed to the language model as a trajectory, not as a set of independent token groups. At the same per-predictive-frame token budget as prior codec-aware methods, a predictive frame enters the language model as a readout of what the front-end already knows, not as an encoding of the current primitives alone. Across ten benchmarks, the gains concentrate on temporal reasoning: on all three temporal benchmarks RESUME improves over both the RGB-frame baseline LLaVA-Video-7B (by 2.8, 5.1, and 3.9 points on TempCompass, TOMATO, and MVBench) and the codec-based baseline CoPE-7B, while staying competitive on general and long-form QA. Frozen-transition tests further show anchor dependence, order sensitivity, and useful rollout behavior beyond the training horizon.

</details>


### [121] [From Given to Gathered Evidence: Agentic Learning for Longitudinal Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39566)
*Minye Shao,Chaohui Yu,Yixuan Wu,Fan Wang,Ling Shao,Yang Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CASE 提出面向临床证据检索的角色化智能体、工具调用框架与智能体后训练方法，并基于 UK Biobank 构建纵向多模态基准（50,401 个问题，4,739 名受试者），使紧凑 VLM 在 MRI 与病历环境中自主选择访视、器官、模态、切片与工具；8B 模型在匹配条件下准确率相对 GPT-5.4 与 Claude Opus 4.8 提升 16% 与 10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医学基准只评估对预选证据的推理，而非跨临床记录与纵向影像主动检索证据的能力，缺少可自主决策的工具使用环境与评估体系。

Method: 1) 角色化临床智能体 CASE + 工具调用 harness + 面向紧凑视觉语言策略模型的智能体后训练框架；2) 基于 UK Biobank 的纵向多模态基准：由 ICD-10 真实诊断生成 50,401 个问题，每题对应含临床上下文与基线与随访多序列 MRI 的患者环境，智能体自主选择访视、器官、模态、切片与专科工具；3) 先用 14,734 条前沿模型交互轨迹做监督微调，再在自身环境交互上做智能体强化学习；4) 采用特权 on-policy 自蒸馏与 rubric 式 LLM 反馈优化证据到结论的推理，而不规定工具调用顺序。

Result: Qwen3-VL-8B 智能体在匹配评估条件下，答案准确率相对 GPT-5.4 与 Claude Opus 4.8 分别提升超过 16% 与 10%；模型从问答模仿转向可迁移的调查策略，纵向证据落地推理增强。

Conclusion: 将工具使用、智能体强化学习与自蒸馏结合可显著提升紧凑模型在长时程多模态临床证据检索上的表现，为智能体式医学推理提供了基准与方法模板。

Abstract: Foundation models can serve as clinical agents through tool-use harnesses. However, conventional medical benchmarks assess reasoning over preselected evidence rather than the ability to seek it across clinical records and longitudinal imaging. We propose CASE: a series of role-specific Clinical Agents for Seeking Evidence, together with a tool-use harness and an agentic post-training framework for compact vision-language policy models. We further introduce a longitudinal multimodal benchmark built on UK Biobank, comprising 50,401 clinical questions derived from real-world ICD-10-coded diagnoses of 4,739 participants. Each question links to a patient-specific environment containing clinical context and multi-sequence MRI from baseline and follow-up visits, where agents autonomously select which visits, organs, modalities, slices, and specialist tools to inspect and compare. Supervised fine-tuning transfers evidence-seeking workflows from 14,734 frontier-model interaction trajectories, followed by agentic reinforcement learning on the learner's own environment interactions. Privileged on-policy self-distillation and rubric-based LLM feedback refine evidence-to-conclusion reasoning without prescribing tool sequences. Experiments show that CASE moves beyond question-answer imitation toward transferable investigation policies, strengthening evidence-grounded longitudinal reasoning. Under matched evaluation conditions, our Qwen3-VL-8B based agent achieves over 16% and 10% relative improvements in answer accuracy over GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.8. Code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [122] [Invariant Shape Analysis of Surfaces with Spherical Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39567)
*T. Shaska,M.-R. Siadat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于共形参数化与旋转群多项式不变量的球谐描述子，精确消除参数化、位姿与尺度依赖，并保留手性（镜像）信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统球谐描述子受参数化、位姿、尺度影响，常用旋转不变约简（功率谱、双谱）丢弃谐波带间相对朝向，且无法区分镜像，导致手性信息丢失。

Method: 用共形重心归一化的共形参数化消除参数化依赖，将每个谐波带对应为二元形式（binary form），把旋转商转化为经典不变量理论，构造旋转群多项式不变量；反射体现为不变量的符号，从而记录手性。

Result: 描述子对截断展开是完备的，在轨道距离下稳定，并提供数值诊断；基准实验验证理论保证，在双侧解剖结构上可区分镜像对与非对称对，而奇偶盲描述子无法做到。

Conclusion: 该不变描述子精确消除三类依赖且不损失其他信息，首次将手性纳入完备球谐表示，适用于需要区分镜像形状的几何分析任务。

Abstract: Spherical harmonic descriptors of closed 3D shapes depend on the parameterization, the pose and the scale of the surface, and the standard rotation-invariant reductions, the power spectrum and the bispectrum, discard the relative orientation of the harmonic bands and cannot distinguish a shape from its mirror image. We construct a descriptor that removes all three dependencies exactly and loses nothing else: a conformal parameterization normalized by its conformal barycenter, followed by polynomial invariants of the rotation group. Identifying each harmonic band with a binary form turns the rotation quotient into classical invariant theory and makes reflections visible as the sign of an invariant, so chirality is recorded. The descriptor is complete for the truncated expansion, stable in the orbit distance, and comes with numerical diagnostics. Benchmarks confirm the guarantees, and on bilateral anatomical structures the descriptor separates mirror-image pairs from asymmetric pairs, which parity-blind descriptors cannot.

</details>


### [123] [Steering Fields: Adaptive Vector Fields for Safe Image Generation and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39573)
*Simone Facchiano,Jan Eric Lenssen,Bernt Schiele,Wolfgang Stammer,Fabio Galasso,Jonas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Steering Fields：将固定的全局 steering vector 泛化为在生成轨迹每步自适应重估方向，实现流模型的连续可控、可组合概念引导，并可用于免反演的结构保持图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像流模型的质量已接近照片级，输出控制（抑制有害内容、引导安全替代）成为核心挑战。主流范式是在选定激活上加入全局、示例无关的固定 steering vector；该向量沿整条轨迹均匀施加，无法适应生成过程不断变化的状态，常带来非预期的全局改动。

Method: Steering Fields 在流模型的带噪状态上工作，将 steering 方向泛化为随生成步自适应重估的场：每一步重新估计 steering direction，而非固定向量。方法暴露 steering 强度与内容保持之间的连续权衡，并具组合性，可同时诱导与抑制多个概念；无需显式空间掩码或物体先验，轨迹自适应估计自然保持局部结构。

Result: 在安全 steering 基准上达到新的 state of the art；作为结构保持的图像编辑技术，取得 state-of-the-art 语义保真度（CLIP、VQAScore），同时模型无关且免反演，无需空间掩码或物体先验即可保持局部结构。

Conclusion: 将 steering vector 推广为轨迹自适应的 Steering Fields，可有效提升安全可控生成与语义保真编辑，兼具组合性、模型无关、免反演与结构保持等优势。

Abstract: As state-of-the-art text-to-image flow models achieve near-photorealistic quality, controlling their outputs, e.g., suppressing harmful content while promoting benign alternatives, has become a central challenge. The current steering paradigm consists of adding a global steering vector to selected activations. While functional, a fixed and example-agnostic vector applied uniformly along the entire trajectory cannot adapt to the changing state of the generation and often causes unintended global changes. We introduce Steering Fields, a generalization of steering vectors that adaptively re-estimates the steering direction at each step of the generative process. Steering Fields operate on the noisy states of flow models, expose a continuous trade-off between steering strength and content preservation, and are compositional, enabling the simultaneous induction and inhibition of concepts, setting a new state of the art on safety steering benchmarks. Despite using no explicit spatial masks or object priors, the trajectory-adaptive estimation naturally preserves local structure, in a manner reminiscent of image editing. In fact, Steering Fields can serve as a structure-preserving image-editing technique that achieves state-of-the-art semantic fidelity (CLIP, VQAScore), while remaining model-agnostic and inversion-free.

</details>


### [124] [A Generalizable and Explainable Framework for Synthetic Video Detection Using First-Digit Gradient Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39585)
*Sidharth Shanu,Gautam Kumar,Tej Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用Sobel梯度幅值结合首位数字定律（Benford定律）做AI生成视频检测，用LDA可视化判别信号、MLP分类，无需生成器或场景先验，在GenBuster-200K、GenVA、FaceForensics++ C23等五个基准上验证，并揭示零样本检测失败现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视频生成器越来越难检测，且生成场景高度多样（风景、街景、动物等）。现有CNN检测器虽有效但缺乏可解释性；取证类检测器往往针对特定场景预训练，或复杂度过高难以提供有效洞察。作者希望提出一种不依赖生成器/场景特定特征、也不依赖容器格式、编解码、码率或压缩伪影的通用检测方法，并在多基准上验证其泛化性。

Method: 对视频帧提取Sobel梯度值，分析其首位数字分布是否符合首位数字定律（Benford定律），以该统计特征作为判别信号；用线性判别分析（LDA）可视化判别方向，用多层感知机（MLP）分类。方法不含生成器或场景特定特征，模型不感知容器格式、编解码、码率与压缩伪影。训练与测试数据集：GenBuster-200K、GenBusterBench、GenVA、FaceForensics++ C23、CelebDF；并开展零样本检测实验。

Result: 模型在五个基准（GenBuster-200K、GenBusterBench、GenVA、FaceForensics++ C23、CelebDF）上完成训练与测试；LDA可视化显示出可解释的判别信号。同时发现：尽管特征集本身携带判别信号，零样本检测仍会失败。

Conclusion: 基于Sobel梯度与首位数字定律的检测框架提供了一种生成器无关、场景无关、且不依赖压缩/编码先验的AI视频检测思路，兼具一定可解释性；但特征中的判别信号不足以支撑零样本跨域检测，说明泛化仍是该方向的开放问题。

Abstract: AI video generators have not only become harder to detect but are used to generate a diverse set of scenarios from landscapes to street views to animal videos. This creates a problem where CNN-based detectors are effective but offer no insight into their inner workings, while forensics-based detectors are often pretrained for a set scenario or become too complex to derive meaningful insights. We present a novel approach to AI video detection using Sobel gradient values analysed with the first-digit law. Using linear discriminant analysis, we visualise the discriminatory signal, while a multi-layer perceptron is used for classification. The detection method has no generator- or scenespecific features, and the model has no knowledge of container formats, codec, bitrate, or compression artefacts. The model is trained and tested on GenBuster-200K, GenBusterBench, GenVA, FaceForensics++ C23, and CelebDF. We also show how zero-shot detection fails even though the feature set carries a discriminatory signal.

</details>


### [125] [KilometerVision: A New Frontier for Large-Scale Spatial Intelligence in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39588)
*Aravindh Mahendran,Michael King,Matthew Koichi Grimes,Antoine Yang,Tyler Zhu,Joseph Heyward,Tengda Han,Shiry Ginosar,Chen Sun,Dima Damen,Simon Osindero,Noah Snavely,Simon Lynen,João Carreira,Viorica Pătrăucean*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于真实视频、跨度达1km的地理布局理解基准，按"地标锚定—路径连接—全局心理地图"三阶段评估VLM空间智能，发现当前模型几乎完全依赖2D视觉识别与文本匹配，缺乏真正的路径积分与几何勘测知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模空间智能是VLM的前沿方向，但现有基准多基于静态图像或短程场景，无法评估真实视频中长距离（可达1km）的地理布局理解。受认知科学启发，作者希望以人类空间认知的层级阶段为框架，揭示模型在空间推理上的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建首个从真实世界视频中探测地理布局理解的基准，覆盖最长1km距离；按认知科学的三级层次设计评测：地标锚定（anchoring）、经由路线连接（route）、整合为全局心理地图（survey knowledge）。对现有VLM进行大规模实验对比分析。

Result: 实验发现当前AI模型在处理空间信息上存在根本性分歧：VLM几乎完全依赖2D视觉识别与文本匹配来"绕过"复杂空间推理，而非使用真正的路径积分或形成几何勘测知识，在各层级阶段均暴露出空间智能缺陷。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了VLM在长程真实视频地理布局理解上的能力上限，指出其空间推理是"识别+匹配"的伪推理，为后续发展真正的路径积分与全局地图建模提供了诊断基准与方向。

Abstract: We push the frontier of large-scale spatial intelligence in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and introduce the first benchmark that probes geographical layout understanding from real-world videos, spanning up to 1km distances. Inspired by the cognitive science literature, we evaluate models against the hierarchical stages of human spatial awareness: anchoring via landmarks, connecting them through routes, and integrating these into global mental maps. Extensive experiments reveal a fundamental divergence in how current AI models process spatial information. Instead of utilising true path integration or forming geometric survey knowledge, we find that VLMs rely almost entirely on 2D visual recognition and text-matching to bypass complex spatial reasoning. The benchmark is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [SPOON: Towards Coherent Compositional 3D Scene Generation from Uncalibrated Multi-view Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39590)
*Guibiao Liao,Mochu Xiang,Heng Li,Ken Deng,Zijie Wang,Guanbin Li,Ping Tan,Shenghua Gao,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPOON 将多视角组合式3D场景生成重构为场景级、几何锚定的位姿推理问题，用 Guide-Route-Reconcile 范式协调视角间物体位姿与相机配置，在 ARSG-110K 与 MIDI-3D-Front 上改善场景布局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角组合式3D生成的关键难点不在单个物体的几何质量（已有强先验），而在于如何把生成的资产组织成全局一致、且与多视角观测相符的场景布局。现有方法要么将布局与物体生成耦合，要么对每个视角独立估计位姿，导致位姿假设歧义且跨视角不一致，形成"物体-相机大杂烩"。

Method: SPOON 以场景级几何锚定的位姿推理替代逐视角独立位姿估计，通过 Guide-Route-Reconcile 范式，利用重建导出的多视角几何对视角间位姿假设进行协调，逐步将物体位姿与相机配置整理为一致的整体空间布局。

Result: 在 ARSG-110K 与 MIDI-3D-Front 上，随输入视角数量变化均取得一致的物体摆放与场景构图改进；在 ARSG-110K 上相较强基线，场景级与物体级 Chamfer 距离分别降低 12.7% 与 17.7%。

Conclusion: 将多视角组合式3D生成表述为场景级几何锚定位姿推理，并显式协调跨视角位姿假设，是提升场景级一致性的有效路径。摘要未给出开源信息、推理耗时与真实场景泛化证据。

Abstract: Compositional 3D scene generation aims to recover complete 3D object shapes and their spatial arrangement from visual observations. Recent image-conditioned 3D generators provide strong priors for producing high-quality object geometry, making the generation of complex scenes increasingly practical. A central challenge is therefore to spatially organize these generated assets into a globally coherent scene while remaining consistent with multi-view observations. Existing approaches either entangle scene layout with object generation or separately estimate spatial placement from view-specific observations, where pose hypotheses may remain ambiguous and inconsistent across views, often resulting in an incoherent object-camera soup. We introduce SPOON, a framework that reformulates multi-view compositional 3D generation as scene-level, geometry-grounded pose reasoning. Rather than treating view-specific object pose hypotheses independently, SPOON coordinates them using reconstruction-derived multi-view geometry through a Guide-Route-Reconcile paradigm. This progressively organizes object poses and camera configurations into a coherent scene-level spatial arrangement. Extensive experiments on ARSG-110K and MIDI-3D-Front demonstrate consistent improvements in object placement and scene composition across varying numbers of input views. On ARSG-110K, SPOON reduces scene-level and object-level Chamfer distances by 12.7% and 17.7%, respectively, compared with a strong baseline.

</details>


### [127] [Structural Limits of the Information-Theoretic Uncertainty Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39591)
*Jakob Lønborg Christensen,Christian F. Baumgartner,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl,Vedrana Andersen Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文从函数层面分析信息论不确定性分解框架，揭示在有限蒙特卡洛样本下大量AU/EU取值区域不可达（AU ≤ log(2)/N），并据此解释认知崩塌现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准信息论框架将不确定性分解为偶然不确定性(AU)与认知不确定性(EU)，但实践中存在两个关键问题：二者高度相关（纠缠）以及EU随模型容量增大而收缩（认知崩塌）。作者希望从根本机制上解释这些现象。

Method: 在函数层面对AU/EU分解框架进行理论分析，推导有限类概率空间下可达的AU/EU取值范围，刻画不可行区域如何随类别数K与蒙特卡洛样本数N变化，并分析该边界与模型置信度、集成规模之间的关系。

Result: 证明存在被结构性排除的AU/EU区域，其边界由 AU ≤ log(2)/N 界定；当模型置信度高时，该不可行边界保证 AU > EU 成立，从而解释了认知崩塌；增大集成规模可缩小不可行区域、缓解认知崩塌；并指出在低AU区间（AU ≤ log(2)/N）AU与EU耦合，不应视为独立量。

Conclusion: AU/EU分解在有限样本下存在结构性缺陷，二者并非独立；研究结果为认知崩塌提供了理论解释，并提示应以集成规模等手段缓解该问题，同时警示在低AU区间不可将AU与EU独立解读。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation in machine learning typically decomposes uncertainty into aleatoric uncertainty (AU) and epistemic uncertainty (EU) using the standard information-theoretic framework. However, in practice, two critical issues arise: entanglement (AU and EU are highly correlated) and epistemic collapse (EU magnitude shrinks with increasing model capacity). We analyze this framework on a functional level and discover that significant portions of the assumed AU, EU range are infeasible in finite settings, and cannot be attained with any class probabilities. We characterize how this infeasible region scales with the number of classes and Monte Carlo samples $N$ (e.g., from ensembles with $N$ members), revealing it is bounded by $\text{AU} \leq \log(2)/N$. Crucially, the infeasible region's boundary helps explain epistemic collapse: when model confidence is high, $\text{AU} > \text{EU}$ is guaranteed by this fundamental structural limitation. Our findings show that increasing ensemble size mitigates epistemic collapse by reducing the infeasible area. Lastly, we caution against interpreting AU and EU as independent quantities in low AU regimes, since we show they are coupled when $\text{AU} \leq \log(2)/N$.

</details>


### [128] [GroundAnything: Reconciling Parallel Decoding with Precise Visual Grounding at Flash Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39600)
*Qize Yu,Lianrui Fan,Bowen Ping,Xini Ding,Zetian Song,Junbo Niu,Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Minghua He,Yuran Wang,Jie Huang,Haojun Zhang,Min Chen,Hao Li,Wenxuan Song,Ruihai Wu,Xianming Liu,Shilong Liu,Shuchang Zhou,Ping Luo,Shiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GroundAnything：4B 双向扩散式视觉定位基础模型，用块级并行去噪替代自回归序列化输出，30 项定位基准达 72.42%（AR 变体）/ 61.75%（熵引导解码），并支持自推测解码实现 4.51× 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归定位模型将空间预测序列化，带来顺序延迟，并给输出 token 强加因果左到右顺序；但视觉定位本质是"视觉证据抽取"，物体、位置与空间关系的依赖关系并不蕴含固有生成顺序，因此并行假设+联合精化更自然。

Method: 以扩散替代 AR 解码：块级（blockwise）去噪调和并行解码与精确定位；训练流程为公开数据+数据引擎的定位预训练 → AR 到扩散的直接转换（联合 AR 与扩散目标）→ SFT → GRPO 强化后训练；推理侧探索熵引导解码与可选自推测（self-speculative）模式。

Result: 30 个定位基准上，GroundAnything-VLM（AR 变体）整体 72.42%，同级模型 SOTA，略低于 GPT-6 Astra（71.35% 为其值，表述为保持竞争力）；熵引导解码下 GroundAnything 平均 61.75%，超过同规模快速 MTP 模型 LocateAnything 的 53.32%；自推测模式相对 AR 对应版本达 4.51× 加速，COCO F1mIoU 仅降 0.74 个百分点。

Conclusion: 双向扩散式、块级并行的定位建模范式可行且高效，配合渐进式推理优化可将并行解码转化为实际延迟收益，适用于延迟敏感的视觉定位系统。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) grounding models serialize spatial predictions, introducing sequential latency and imposing a causal order on output tokens. We view grounding as visual evidence extraction: objects, locations, and spatial relations are jointly constrained by the image and query, yet their dependencies do not imply an intrinsic left-to-right generation order. This distinction makes bidirectional diffusion a natural fit, allowing spatial hypotheses to emerge in parallel and be jointly refined through iterative denoising. We introduce GroundAnything, a 4B-parameter grounding foundation model that reconciles fast parallel decoding with precise localization through blockwise denoising. Training combines grounding pretraining from public datasets and dedicated data engines, direct AR-to-diffusion conversion with joint AR and diffusion objectives, supervised fine-tuning, and GRPO-based reinforcement post-training. Across 30 grounding benchmarks, our autoregressive variant, GroundAnything-VLM, establishes a new overall state of the art among similarly sized models at 72.42%, remaining competitive with GPT-6 Astra (71.35%). With entropy-guided decoding, GroundAnything also surpasses the prior state of the art at this scale, averaging 61.75% versus 53.32% for the fast MTP-based LocateAnything model. We further explore decoding strategies, showing that an optional self-speculative mode achieves a $4.51\times$ speedup over the AR counterpart with a 0.74 percentage-point drop in COCO F1mIoU. Infrastructure experiments show that progressive inference optimizations translate parallel decoding into practical speedups. These support efficient visual grounding in latency-sensitive real-world systems.

</details>


### [129] [GroundingPI: A Grounding Foundation Model towards Physical Intelligence with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39601)
*Qize Yu,Lianrui Fan,Boyu Chen,Jiaqi Liang,Xini Ding,Yue Chen,Zetian Song,Yuran Wang,Yi Zou,Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Wenxuan Song,Bohan Zhou,Mingleyang Li,Siqiao Huang,Yuqi Ye,Caigao Jiang,Wei Wei,Ruihai Wu,Hang Zhang,Yixiao Ge,Shuchang Zhou,Shilong Liu,Xianming Liu,Ping Luo,Shiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GroundingPI，一个 4B 参数的可落地基础模型：以共享词表把点与框表示为量化坐标，经多模态与空间预训练、SFT 和 GRPO 强化学习训练，在 34 个定位基准、11 类感知能力上取得 SOTA（均值 73.68%），并作为视觉骨干提升机器人操作与自动驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 与世界动作模型的感知来自通用视觉-语言或视频生成骨干，在杂乱场景与微小目标下定位精度不足、速度不够快，难以满足闭环控制所需的精确 grounding。作者主张把"专用定位感知预训练"作为物理智能基础模型的独立方向。

Method: GroundingPI（4B）将点和框统一生成为共享词表中的量化坐标 token。训练分三阶段：多模态与空间预训练 → 监督微调 → 基于 GRPO 的强化学习；监督来自公开数据集与专用数据引擎（data engine）。数据配方覆盖 11 类感知能力，并在规模与数据组成上做系统消融/缩放分析。

Result: 对 44 个基线、34 个定位基准，平均 73.68%，超过更大的 GPT-6 Astra（71.54%）。作为下游视觉骨干：RoboTwin 2.0 上在全部四个 OOD 设置中优于所评测的每个主流骨干，相对最强骨干最高提升 24.8%；RoboCasa-GR1 上用 50% 演示训练的 GroundingPI 超过用 75% 演示训练的基线；nuScenes 上开环平均 L2 误差 0.296 m。下游性能随预训练规模扩大而提升；稠密定位数据对操作与驾驶均有显著收益，OCR 数据表现出作为感知学习催化剂的潜力。

Conclusion: 结果支持"定位/grounding 作为感知基础"的定位，并表明专用感知预训练是通向物理智能基础模型的有前景方向；预训练规模与数据配方（稠密定位、OCR 助力）是下游具身与驾驶任务的关键杠杆。

Abstract: Precise grounding matters. It specifies which object is the target and where that object is, even in clutter and for tiny objects, and it has to be fast enough for closed-loop control. Yet vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAMs) take perception from general-purpose vision-language and video-generation backbones, which still fail in these settings. We introduce GroundingPI, a 4B grounding foundation model that generates points and boxes as quantized coordinates in a shared vocabulary. Training combines multimodal and spatial pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning with GRPO, using supervision from public datasets and dedicated data engines. Against 44 baselines across 34 grounding benchmarks spanning 11 perceptual capabilities, GroundingPI establishes a new state of the art, averaging 73.68%, above the larger GPT-6 Astra (71.54%). As a downstream visual backbone, GroundingPI improves performance on robotic manipulation and autonomous driving. On RoboTwin 2.0, it outperforms every mainstream backbone we evaluate in all four out-of-distribution settings, by up to 24.8% relative to the strongest backbone. On RoboCasa-GR1, GroundingPI trained with 50% of the demonstrations outperforms those baselines trained with 75%. On nuScenes, used as the visual backbone, GroundingPI attains an average open-loop L2 error of 0.296 m. We systematically analyze GroundingPI's pretraining in scale and data composition. Downstream autonomous driving and robotic manipulation improve as the pretraining is scaled. Analyzing the data recipe across these 11 perceptual capabilities shows dense grounding's substantial benefits for both, and OCR's potential as a catalyst for perceptual learning. These results support grounding as a perceptual foundation, and dedicated perceptual pretraining as a promising direction for foundation models of physical intelligence.

</details>


### [130] [FOMO: Forget the Concept, Don't Miss Out on the Scene in Selective Video Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39605)
*Łukasz Rudnik,Agnieszka Polowczyk,Alicja Polowczyk,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOMO是首个基于训练的"选择性视频概念遗忘"方法，同时处理"改什么"与"保什么"，可在不改变背景/构图/动态的前提下移除不安全内容、物体与运动概念，并将生成重定向到安全替代概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频模型依赖大规模网络数据训练，不可避免学到有害或有问题的概念，需要"概念遗忘"来缓解。但现有方法主要针对静态视觉概念（物体、身份、不安全外观），几乎忽略"运动"层面的遗忘；同时这些方法很少关注保护周围场景，导致概念移除时连带改变背景、构图或整体视频动态。作者主张有效的遗忘应只改变目标概念，最小化对剩余场景的改动。

Method: 提出FOMO，首个以"保护原场景"为优先的训练式选择性视频遗忘方法。将遗忘建模为两个互补目标：需要改变的内容（what to change）与需要保留的内容（what to preserve）。方法先定位与概念相关的表征并对其进行修改；保留机制在不需要辅助数据的前提下维持非目标场景信息。除擦除不良概念外，FOMO还将生成显式重定向到指定的安全替代概念。作者进一步把该形式化扩展到运动遗忘，此时概念由时序行为而非固定空间区域定义。

Result: 在不安全内容、物体与运动三类概念上均实现有效遗忘，并在"概念移除 vs 场景保持"之间取得最佳权衡（原文未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: FOMO把"场景保持"提升为一等目标，并首次系统性处理视频运动概念的遗忘问题，为视频生成模型的负责任使用提供了一种选择性、低副作用的遗忘范式。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative video models has enabled the synthesis of increasingly realistic and temporally coherent videos, while also raising concerns about the generation of harmful content. The reliance on large-scale web datasets during training inevitably exposes these models to undesirable material, making concept unlearning an essential mitigation. Existing methods mainly target static visual concepts, such as objects, identities, or unsafe appearance, largely overlooking motion unlearning. Furthermore, these approaches often pay little attention to preserving the surrounding scene. As a result, successful concept removal may unintentionally alter the background, composition, or overall video dynamics. We argue that effective unlearning should ideally change only what is targeted, while minimizing unnecessary changes to the remaining scene. In this work, we introduce FOMO, to the best of our knowledge the first training-based selective video unlearning method that directly treats preservation of the original scene as a priority. We formulate unlearning around two complementary objectives: what to change and what to preserve. Our method localizes concept-related representations and modifies them, while the preservation mechanism maintains non-target scene information without requiring auxiliary data. Beyond simply erasing unwanted concepts, FOMO explicitly redirects the generation toward a specified safe alternative. We further extend this formulation to motion unlearning, where the concept is defined by temporal behavior rather than a fixed spatial region. Our solution achieves effective unlearning across unsafe content, object, and motion concepts, while achieving the best trade-off between concept removal and scene preservation. Code: this https URL Project Page this https URL

</details>


### [131] [Semantic Watermarking for Malicious Image Manipulation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39623)
*Yoonseo Kim,Seungwoo Baek,Junyoung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义水印框架 CLIP-VAE（β-VAE 二值水印 + 信道感知训练），将水印视为可恢复的语义参考，并用轻量模块 SDA-Net 判断图像被篡改的语义漂移方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量生成编辑模型可保持视觉逼真地注入暴力或色情内容，威胁公共讨论与弱势群体；现有二值哈希水印在真实噪声信道下退化严重，且只能做不透明标识、无法说明篡改方向。

Method: 以 β-VAE 构建二值语义水印 CLIP-VAE，将水印嵌入 CLIP 语义嵌入空间；训练时注入随机比特翻转噪声实现信道感知训练，使解码器在噪声信道下优雅退化；下游 SDA-Net 利用恢复的语义嵌入检测图像是否被改及其语义漂移方向。

Result: 在 InstructPix2Pix 真实比特错误率下，与 SimHash、ITQ、HashNet 及其 robust-MLP 变体共 5 路对比中，CLIP-VAE 取得最高的 CLIP 嵌入重建余弦相似度，并独有支持漂移方向检测能力。

Conclusion: 语义水印可同时作为鲁棒内容溯源与方向性篡改取证手段，作为现有内容审核流程的补充模块。

Abstract: The proliferation of high-fidelity generative editing models has made it possible to inject violent or sexual content into otherwise ordinary images while preserving visual plausibility, with concrete consequences for public discourse and vulnerable populations. We propose a robust semantic watermarking framework that reframes the watermark as a recoverable semantic reference rather than an opaque identifier. Our framework combines a $\beta$-VAE-based binary watermark (CLIP-VAE) with explicit channel-aware training---random bit-flip noise is injected during training so that the decoder learns graceful degradation under the noisy watermarking channel. As a downstream application, a lightweight module SDA-Net uses the recovered semantic embedding to expose not only whether but in which semantic direction an image has been altered. In a 5-way comparison against representative binary hashing baselines (SimHash, ITQ, HashNet, and their robust-MLP variants), CLIP-VAE achieves the highest reconstruction cosine similarity to the original CLIP embedding under realistic InstructPix2Pix bit-error rates, and uniquely supports direction-of-drift detection---a forensic complement to existing content-moderation pipelines.

</details>


### [132] [ExpandDiff: Dynamic Range Expanding Diffusion for Single-Image HDR Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39624)
*Mehmet Emre andıran,Zhuoqian Yang,Liying Lu,Mathieu Salzmann,Sabine Süsstrunk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ExpandDiff，一种条件扩散式单图 HDR 重建方法，通过动态裁剪合成与空间自适应归一化联合重建过曝高光与被压暗的阴影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDR 图像因传感器动态范围与曝光差异，在阴影和高光区域丢失不同程度的信息，且不同图像丢失量变化很大；现有单图 HDR 方法往往难以同时兼顾两侧裁剪区域。

Method: Dynamic Clipping Synthesis（DCS）：从 HDR 目标构造训练输入时随机采样阴影/高光裁剪百分位，模拟多样化的裁剪分布；采用像素空间扩散模型，以空间自适应归一化（SPADE 类）为条件引导，并通过有界输出头预测感知编码（PU21 相关）的 HDR，在单条采样轨迹中同时重建两个裁剪方向。

Result: 在 SI-HDR 基准上，PU21-PSNR 相比最强对比方法提升 3.43 dB；在双侧同时裁剪的设定下提升 7.34 dB。

Conclusion: 随机裁剪合成加上条件扩散的联合建模能有效应对 LDR 信息丢失的变化性，显著提升单图 HDR 重建精度，尤其擅长双侧裁剪场景。

Abstract: Single-image HDR reconstruction requires inferring missing detail while preserving the visible content of an LDR image. Differences in sensor dynamic range and exposure cause LDR images to lose varying amounts of information in shadows and highlights. We present ExpandDiff, a conditional diffusion pipeline that jointly reconstructs clipped shadows and highlights. To account for this variation, we introduce Dynamic Clipping Synthesis (DCS), which randomly samples shadow and highlight clipping percentiles when constructing training inputs from HDR targets. A pixel-space diffusion model guided by spatially-adaptive normalization then predicts perceptually encoded HDR through a bounded output head, reconstructing both clipping directions in one sampling trajectory. On the SI-HDR benchmark, ExpandDiff variants improve HDR reconstruction accuracy by 3.43 dB in PU21-PSNR over the strongest evaluated competing method, and by 7.34 dB under two-sided clipping. The code and supplementary material are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [133] [D-Scope: Decomposing and Steering Diffusion Transformers with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39625)
*Xinyue Xu,Jiahao Zhang,Lijie Hu,Peter Hase,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D-Scope 提出将扩散 Transformer 中稀疏自编码器特征的"可解释性"与其"可控性"通过共享视觉证据联系起来的框架，用 SigLIP-2 视觉质心实现无逐特征文本标注的特征检索，并用空间掩码干预检验解码方向对生成的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE 能揭示 DiT 中的视觉结构，但"特征可解释"并不等于"特征可用于控制生成"。现有方法缺乏将特征解释与生成控制统一评估的手段，且逐特征文本标注成本高、证据可追溯性差。

Method: 聚合高激活图像块的 SigLIP-2 嵌入形成视觉质心；在共享图像-文本嵌入空间中将目标文本描述与视觉质心匹配，从而无需逐特征文本标注即可检索特征。底层图像块为选择提供证据；在固定生成条件下以不同强度对相应解码方向施加空间掩码干预。评估覆盖两个模型族、五个层级共 150 个 SAE，并构建含 100 个目标概念、每个 10 种上下文的基准（涵盖欠指定与显式冲突条件）。

Result: 高重建保真度可与低字典利用率及有限的视觉证据覆盖共存；在逐案例最优扫掠强度选择下，对比式检索在测试的引导配置中比直接检索带来更大的区域平均 SigLIP-2 增益，但未持续改善区域外保持。

Conclusion: D-Scope 提供了一个可检视的框架，通过视觉证据及其解码方向对生成的影响来评估稀疏 DiT 特征，并揭示重构保真度与特征可用性之间的脱节。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) reveal visual structure in diffusion transformers (DiTs), but interpreting a feature does not establish whether it can be used to control generation. We introduce D-Scope (Diffusion Scope), a framework that connects feature interpretation to generation control through shared visual evidence. D-Scope aggregates SigLIP~2 embeddings of highly activating image patches into visual centroids. Matching target text descriptions against these visual centroids in the shared image-text embedding space then enables retrieval of individual features without per-feature text annotations. The underlying patches provide evidence for inspecting each selection, while spatially masked interventions test the corresponding decoder direction at varying strengths under fixed generation conditions. We characterize 150 SAEs across two model families and five layers, and introduce a benchmark of 100 target concepts with ten contexts each spanning under-specified and explicit-conflict conditions. Our empirical results show that high reconstruction fidelity can coexist with low dictionary utilization and limited visual-evidence coverage. Under per-case best-of-sweep strength selection, contrastive retrieval yields larger mean regional SigLIP~2 gains than direct retrieval across the tested steering configurations, without consistently improving outside-region preservation. D-Scope provides an inspectable framework for evaluating sparse DiT features through their visual evidence and the effects of their decoder directions on generation. The demo is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [134] [Introduction to Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39627)
*Stan Birchfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该书以代码优先方式系统讲解计算机视觉，涵盖经典2D图像处理、经典3D视觉与深度学习三部分共44章，所有算法用Python/NumPy或PyTorch实现并与OpenCV/PyTorch库函数数值对照验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向希望同时理解计算机视觉算法数学原理及其Python实现的学生与从业者，提供自包含的参考教材，弥合理论推导与可复现代码之间的鸿沟。

Method: 按"第一性原理"组织44个短章节：图像算术与形态学、卷积/金字塔/频域滤波、特征检测/光流/立体匹配、投影几何/相机标定/运动恢复结构，以及从单神经元到卷积网络、反向传播、经典架构、迁移学习、目标检测、语义与实例分割的完整深度学习脉络，并含混合精度与并行训练等工程议题。每个技术均以Python与NumPy/PyTorch直接实现，并与OpenCV或PyTorch对应库函数数值校验；内容由AI辅助从公开在线课程笔记蒸馏而成，将大量工作代码压缩为简洁数学阐述并保持结果可复现。

Result: 产出一部三部分44章、覆盖经典与深度学习计算机视觉、理论与代码并列且经数值验证的自包含教材式参考书。

Conclusion: 该书适合作为计算机视觉算法与Python实现结合的学习与查阅资料，强调可复现性与工程实践，但属于通识教材而非特定前沿方法的研究贡献。

Abstract: This book presents a code-first introduction to computer vision, spanning classical 2D image processing, classical 3D vision, and deep learning. Organized as 44 short chapters across three parts, the book builds each topic from first principles: image arithmetic and morphology; convolution, pyramids, and frequency-domain filtering; feature detection, optical flow, and stereo; projective geometry, camera calibration, and structure from motion; and the full arc of modern deep learning, from a single neuron through convolutional networks, backpropagation, classic architectures, transfer learning, object detection, and semantic and instance segmentation, concluding with engineering considerations like mixed-precision and parallel training. Every technique is implemented directly in Python and NumPy or PyTorch and checked numerically against the corresponding OpenCV or PyTorch library function, so readers see not just the mathematics but its concrete behavior on real and synthetic data. The material was distilled with AI assistance from freely available online course notes, condensing extensive working code into concise mathematical exposition while preserving verified, reproducible results throughout. It is intended as a self-contained reference for students and practitioners who want to understand computer vision algorithms and their Python implementations.

</details>


### [135] [SAGE: Salient Factor Discovery and Generation with Visual Foundation Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39635)
*Shuang Liang,Lejun Liao,Shiyuan Zhang,Max C. Zhang,Xiaolong Luo,Han Wang,Stefano Anzellotti,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE 在冻结自编码器的空间潜空间中以对比分析分离目标-显著与共有因子，并用显著表征条件化 DiT 扩散模型，实现高保真重建、无监督子类型发现与可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比分析需分离目标数据集（如戴眼镜人脸）的显著因子与背景数据集共有内容，但已有方法多在低维或预定义属性空间中做因子分解，难以捕捉每张图中目标特异的高维细节（眼镜形状、颜色、位置），也无法在无子类型标签条件下发现子类型并无名生成新样本。

Method: 1) 在冻结表示自编码器的高维空间潜（spatial latent）中直接学习显著/共有两类因子，避免低维瓶颈；2) 对参考图提取显著表征，作为条件注入扩散 Transformer（DiT）以生成新样本；3) 显著空间可用于无监督子类型发现与属性探查。

Result: Digits-ImageNet 与 FFHQ 眼镜数据上：rFID<2 的高保真重建；无监督子类型发现优于基线（探针准确率 0.950 vs 最高 0.281）；揭示眼镜类型、更细的太阳镜风格与错误标注样本；显著条件生成使 Digits-ImageNet 子类型准确率由 27.7% 提升至 90.5%，两数据集多样性均提升；视网膜 OCT 上仅用正常/疾病标签即可将三种疾病在显著空间分离。

Conclusion: SAGE 表明在高维空间潜中做对比因子分离，可同时支撑重建、无监督子类型发现与显著条件生成，并在医学影像上展现跨域泛化潜力。

Abstract: Given a target dataset, such as faces with eyeglasses, and a background dataset, such as faces without, contrastive analysis separates \textit{salient} factors specific to the target from \textit{common} content shared by both. We aim for salient representations that capture target-specific detail in each image, such as the shape, color, and position of the glasses, so that they reveal subtypes without subtype labels and guide the generation of new examples of a discovered subtype, even one with no name or text description. We introduce SAGE, which learns both factors directly in the high-dimensional spatial latent of a frozen representation autoencoder and conditions a diffusion transformer on the learned salient representation of a reference image. On Digits-ImageNet and FFHQ eyeglasses, SAGE combines high-fidelity \textit{reconstruction} (rFID below $2$) with unsupervised \textit{subtype discovery}, recovering the digits better than baselines (probe accuracy $0.950$ vs.\ at most $0.281$) and revealing eyewear types, finer sunglasses styles, and mislabeled images; salient-conditioned \textit{generation} raises Digits-ImageNet subtype accuracy over the unfactorized latent ($90.5\%$ vs.\ $27.7\%$) and diversity on both datasets. On retinal OCT, SAGE's salient space separates three diseases using only normal/disease labels.

</details>


### [136] [FANVIDv2: Evaluating Video Super-Resolution by Face and Licence-Plate Recognition Under Compound Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39649)
*Kavitha Viswanathan,Vrinda Goel,Shlesh Gholap,Devayan Ghosh,Madhav Gupta,Dhruvi Ganatra,Sanket Potdar,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FANVIDv2 视频超分基准，用识别管线（人脸/车牌）在检测框内评测 VSR 输出，而非仅 PSNR/SSIM，并提供复合退化模型、两种识别度量（FaceRecBox、TextRecBox）及基线（RCDM、RCDM-RMGF）与全套数据/评测代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 VSR 以双三次下采样片段上的 PSNR/SSIM 作为评价标准，但监控场景的真实目标不是像素保真，而是让车牌与人脸可被识别；现有基准与实际任务目标存在错配，且双三次下采样无法反映真实成像退化（模糊、噪声、压缩等）。

Method: 构建 FANVIDv2：提供 320×180 低分辨率片段及对应高分辨率参考，含 48 位公众人物（各一张 HR 图库照）与 375 段车牌片段（360 个不同车牌字符串）；LR 由随机复合退化生成（模糊、尺寸抖动、传感器噪声、JPEG 压缩、最终下采样）而非单一双三次；提出在检测框内评分的两个度量——FaceRecBox（需同时定位准确与身份识别正确才给分）与 TextRecBox（按定位质量加权的归一化编辑距离评分车牌转写）；并给出 2.3M 参数的 VSR 基线 RCDM 与残差图门控变体 RCDM-RMGF，公开退化模型、基线架构、评分器细节及标注、元数据、下载与退化脚本、评测代码。

Result: 以 RCDM（2.3M 参数）为基线，FaceRecBox 从 0.6864 提升至 0.7222，匹配人脸上的身份准确率从 84.35% 提升到 86.93%，TextRecBox 从 0.3088 提升到 0.3667；残差图门控变体 RCDM-RMGF 在车牌上达到 0.3801。

Conclusion: 论文主张以“识别管线能从 VSR 输出中得到什么”来评价视频超分，指出识别导向指标与像素指标存在差异，并给出可复现的基准与度量体系，推动 VSR 评测从视觉保真转向任务可用性。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) is normally judged by PSNR and SSIM on clips that were downsampled bicubically, although in surveillance its purpose is to make faces and licence plates \emph{recognisable}. We present FANVIDv2, a benchmark that scores VSR by what a recognition pipeline can do with its output. FANVIDv2 provides $320\times180$ low-resolution (LR) clips with high-resolution (HR) references for 48 public figures (with one HR gallery image each) and 375 licence-plate clips covering 360 distinct plate strings. LR clips are generated with a randomised compound degradation (blur, resize jitter, sensor noise, JPEG compression, final downsampling) rather than bicubic downsampling alone. Two metrics score recognition \emph{inside} detections: FaceRecBox rewards a face only if it is localised and correctly identified, and TextRecBox scores plate transcriptions by normalised edit distance weighted by localisation quality. With a 2.3\,M-parameter VSR baseline (RCDM), FaceRecBox rises from 0.6864 to 0.7222, identity accuracy on matched faces from 84.35\% to 86.93\%, and TextRecBox from 0.3088 to 0.3667; a residual-map gated variant (RCDM-RMGF) reaches 0.3801 on plates. We describe the degradation model, the baseline architectures and the scorers in detail, and release annotations, metadata, download and degradation scripts and evaluation code.

</details>


### [137] [Diffusable Latents from Structure-Agnostic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39657)
*Adrien Ramanana Rahary,Nicolas Dufour,Patrick Pérez,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究表明：将图像自动编码器瓶颈特征与教师模型做"池化图像级"蒸馏（而非逐位置对齐）即可获得相当或更好的潜空间扩散质量，且该一阶匹配可推广到1D token序列潜变量与跨模态（文本编码器→图像自编码器）场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型的采样质量与收敛速度依赖自编码器潜空间的"可扩散性"（diffusability），而现有标准做法是通过逐位置（position-wise）对齐蒸馏预训练基础模型到瓶颈层。这种密集对齐把潜变量布局强行绑定到教师特征的空间结构上，作者认为这一约束并非必要，希望用更简单、更灵活的蒸馏目标替代它。

Method: 系统比较两类池化目标：(1) 一阶图像级描述子匹配——把整张图池化后的单一描述子对齐到教师的池化特征；(2) 关系型目标——仅基于每张图最近邻样本的相对关系进行对齐。实验覆盖不同潜变量形状（2D特征图 vs 1D token序列）与不同教师模态（图像编码器、文本编码器）。

Result: 图像级池化蒸馏在扩散质量上与逐位置密集蒸馏持平甚至略优；一阶匹配自然扩展到1D token序列潜变量，并可跨模态成立——即使把文本编码器蒸馏进图像自编码器，仍能提升可扩散性；仅依赖最近邻关系的关系型目标同样带来增益。

Conclusion: 密集位置对齐并非提升潜扩散性的必要条件，图像级/关系型池化目标已足够，且更为通用、可跨潜变量形状与教师模态迁移；这为设计更简洁的潜空间蒸馏目标提供了依据。代码与博客公开。

Abstract: Distilling pretrained foundation models into an autoencoder bottleneck improves latent diffusability, enabling diffusion models to converge faster and reach higher sample quality. Standard distillation aligns the latent at each position to a co-located teacher feature, tying the latent layout to the teacher's. We show this constraint is unnecessary: aligning a single pooled image-level descriptor to the teacher's performs as well as or slightly better than dense position-wise distillation. We compare first-order and relational pooled objectives across latent shapes and teacher modalities. First-order matching extends naturally to 1D token-sequence latents and across modalities, where distilling a text encoder into an image autoencoder still improves diffusability; a relational objective based only on each image's nearest neighbours improves it as well. Code and blog post are available at this https URL and this https URL .

</details>


### [138] [BAM! Bayesian Anything Model: a foundation model for generative computational imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39660)
*Alessio Spagnoletti,Charlesquin Kemajou Mbakam,Jonathan Spence,Andrés Almansa,Marcelo Pereyra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BAM（Bayesian Anything Model）：将 RAM 的算子条件化骨干升级为条件流映射，构建 36M 参数的物理感知贝叶斯计算成像基础模型，实现少步、免似然近似、免引导权重调参的后验采样，在多个线性逆问题上三步骤即超越专用模型与零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型正在改变贝叶斯计算成像，但领域缺少物理感知的基础模型。现有做法分两类：大型基础图像模型作为即插即用先验，需零样本近似似然引导，带来显著偏差与计算开销；物理感知生成模型虽避免偏差，却各自绑定特定数据集、任务与成像仪器，泛化性差。作者希望兼得泛化性、物理一致性与低推理成本。

Method: 以 Terris 等的算子条件化 Reconstruct Anything Model（RAM）骨干为基础，升级为条件流映射（conditional flow map），使仪器物理特性在推理时指定而非训练时固定；模型仅 36M 参数，在大规模图像语料与前向算子库上联合预训练。推理时单一网络在数步内直接抽取后验样本，无需似然近似，也无需调引导权重。

Result: 在 FFHQ、AFHQ、LSUN、DIV2K 以及 Kohler 相机抖动基准的线性逆问题上，BAM 仅用 3 步采样即在样本质量上超越专用模型和主流零样本方法，且计算成本仅为后者的一小部分。

Conclusion: BAM 以轻量基础模型形式提供物理感知的少步后验采样能力，可零样本或极少微调泛化到未见数据与任务，为社区提供低门槛的生成式计算成像入口，降低训练成像模型的经济与环境成本，并开辟物理感知贝叶斯计算成像的研究新路径。

Abstract: Generative models are transforming Bayesian computational imaging, yet the field still lacks physics-aware foundation models. Current practice falls into two camps. Large foundation image models are deployed as plug-and-play priors with zero-shot approximate likelihood guidance, which introduces significant bias and computational cost. Physics-aware generative models avoid this bias, but each is tied to a specific dataset, task and instrument. We introduce BAM (Bayesian Anything Model), a lightweight foundation model for few-step, physics-aware posterior sampling that generalises robustly to unseen data and tasks, zero-shot or with minimal finetuning. BAM upgrades the operator-conditioned Reconstruct Anything Model (RAM) backbone (Terris et al.) into a conditional flow map, so instrument physics is specified at inference time rather than fixed during training. BAM has just 36M parameters and is pre-trained jointly on large image corpora and libraries of forward operators. A single network then draws posterior samples in a few steps, with no likelihood approximation and no guidance weights to tune. Across linear inverse problems on FFHQ, AFHQ, LSUN, DIV2K and the Kohler camera-shake benchmark, BAM outperforms in just 3 steps both specialised models and leading zero-shot methods in sample quality, at a fraction of their computational cost.
