We study how vision and touch should be combined at the level of primitive skills, asking what each skill needs from each sensor, and propose a skill-conditioned representation in which the queried skill conditions fusion over modality-specific short-term observation tokens while attending to a sparse event memory that retains terminal observations from the last $K$ executed skills. Evaluated by skill progress estimation on three contact-rich tasks, it reduces slip-detection delay by 87% against fine-tuned SOTA progress models, twist-completion delay by 67.5% against a vision-only ablation, and progress error on a blind search task by 92% through sparse event memory. Gains concentrate exactly where completion is defined by contact or task history. More broadly, our results suggest that observation formation not only policy architecture is a central challenge in multi-modal representation. Project Website: this http URL

</details>


### [206] [Onboard Vision and MPC Navigation for Underwater Robots: An Open BlueROV2 Platform for Multi-Robot Experiments & Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38511)
*Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Wiktor Kowalczyk,Cezary Banaszek,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源BlueROV2平台，集成Jetson Orin NX与RealSense D435i立体相机，结合水下标定立体深度、实时目标检测、四元数状态估计与六自由度非线性MPC，实现受限水域自主导航与对接，并提供PX4 SITL/Gazebo多机仿真与低成本对接站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限水下环境（如狭窄空间、对接作业）对自主水下机器人的感知、估计与控制提出高鲁棒性要求；现有平台多缺乏开源、模块化的软硬件一体化方案及可复现的仿真验证环境，难以支撑多机协同与对接研究。

Method: 硬件采用模块化耐压舱集成Jetson Orin NX与Intel RealSense D435i；算法上用水下标定的立体深度与实时目标检测提供邻近BlueROV2在相机系与体系下的相对位姿测量，四元数估计器融合外部位姿与惯性测量，基于非线性六自由度模型的NMPC跟踪规划出的导航与对接轨迹；同时开源物理仿真栈（PX4 SITL + Gazebo）、多机器人仿真工具与低成本物理对接站。

Result: 实验评估涵盖水下感知性能、板上计算性能、状态估计精度、轨迹跟踪与自主对接，验证了该一体化平台在受限环境下的可行性（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了软硬件开源、可复现的水下自主导航与对接平台，具备模块化与多机仿真扩展能力，可作为水下机器人感知—估计—控制一体化研究的工程基线与工具支撑。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots require robust perception, estimation and control to operate in confined environments. This paper presents an open-source BlueROV2 platform combining onboard vision with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) for autonomous navigation and docking. The platform integrates an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX and an Intel RealSense D435i stereo camera in a modular pressure housing. Underwater-calibrated stereo depth and realtime object detection provide relative position measurements of nearby BlueROV2 vehicles in the camera and body frames. A quaternion-based estimator fuses external pose and inertial measurements, while an NMPC controller based on a nonlinear six-degree-of-freedom model tracks planned navigation and docking trajectories. To support reproducible development, we also provide open-source physics-based PX4 SITL and Gazebo environments, multi-robot simulation tools and a lowcost physical docking station. Experiments evaluate underwater perception, onboard computational performance, state estimation, trajectory tracking and autonomous docking.

</details>


### [207] [Behavioral Persistence and Incomplete Functional Transfer of Co-evolved Communication in Evolutionary Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38527)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文评估将2D仿真中协同演化的通信协议直接迁移到3D物理环境（e-puck机器人），不重训网络权重；需三项修正，迁移仅部分且不对称，双向成功未达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验涌现通信协议能否脱离其演化环境，从仿真直接部署到真实物理机器人；并探究迁移失败究竟源于信号翻译问题，还是物理导航约束下的能力不足，从而厘清"生态与导航条件"在通信协议可迁移性中的作用。

Method: 两个e-puck型机器人，由带残差连接的GRU网络控制，执行含社交信号的觅食任务。感知—运动翻译层做了三项修正，其中"饥饿项"校准依据是训练后残差权重中可测量的不对称性。附加实验在社交信道中加入显式方向信息。评测指标为两机器人均到达食物区域即成功。

Result: 迁移是部分且不对称的：在30个测试种子中，一个智能体仅1次到达食物源，另一个从未到达。加入显式方向信息后，接收方智能体轨迹出现可观测变化，若干具体情形有所改善，但仍不足以使第二个智能体到达食物源。

Conclusion: 涌现通信的成功迁移不仅取决于保留信号过程本身，还取决于保留其演化所依赖的生态环境与导航条件；仅靠信号翻译层的改进无法解释和弥补迁移失败。

Abstract: This work evaluates the direct transfer of a co-evolved communication protocol from a 2D simulation to a 3D physical environment, without retraining the network weights. Two e-puck-type robots, controlled by a GRU network with residual connection, were evaluated in a food-seeking task with social signaling. The sensory and motor translation layer required three corrections for stable physical operation, including the calibration of a hunger term based on a measurable asymmetry in the trained residual weights. Even with these corrections, the transfer was partial and asymmetric: one agent reached the food source in one of thirty tested seeds, while the other did not reach it in any. Task success was measured by both agents reaching the food area. An additional experiment incorporating explicit directional information in the social channel produced observable changes in the trajectory of the receiving agent and improvements in several specific cases. However, these improvements were not enough to allow the second agent to reach the food source, suggesting that the limitation may not be explained solely by signal translation, but also by the ability to navigate under the new physical constraints. The results suggest that successful transfer of emergent communication may depend not only on preserving the signaling process itself, but also on preserving the ecological and navigational conditions under which the protocol evolved.

</details>


### [208] [Systematically Exploring the Capabilities of GPT-6 Astra as Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38537)
*Galbot Team,Xuchuan Chen,Xiaoqian Cheng,Yu Deng,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Xiangjun Gao,Haozhe Jia,Zekai Li,Zhoujian Li,Yunrui Lian,Sikai Liang,Chenghuai Lin,Dairu Liu,Jiahang Liu,Qingtao Liu,Yuxuan Ma,Zekun Qi,Jiayi Su,He Wang,Ruochen Xu,Tianyu Xu,Xudong Xu,Zhe Xu,Mi Yan,Siming Yan,Li Yi,Ruixi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Yintianrun Zhang,Zhikai Zhang,Zhizheng Zhang,Yixin Zheng,Weiyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文系统评估GPT-6 Astra作为通用具身策略的能力，在六类任务（夹爪/灵巧/移动操作、导航、运动、人形loco-manipulation）中检验直接控制、混合控制与反馈适应，发现高层任务决策可用但底层物理控制与推理延迟是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大模型从高层规划延伸到直接生成数值机器人动作的可行性，判断其能否作为通用具身策略，并识别其能力边界与部署约束。

Method: 在六个领域开展评测：包括与学习策略的混合控制（如与传统策略π0.5协作）、直接末端/手内控制、经验引导与反馈适应；覆盖RoboDojo、DexJoCo、RoboCasa365、RxR、HM3D、HumanoidBench等基准，并统计token消耗与推理时延。

Result: 夹爪混合控制48%（RoboDojo子集）、灵巧混合控制50%（DexJoCo 10次）但直接手内控制难以协调手指接触、移动操作38.7%（RoboCasa365）、导航92%（RxR）与82%（HM3D）但绕路严重、运动控制五次尝试均未到达终点、人形任务30项中13项超越基线；推理成本高（624.8M vs 1.132B tokens；30秒运动需250次调用，平均39.86秒/次）。

Conclusion: 大模型在任务级决策与动作建议上有价值，但与可靠物理控制之间仍存在显著差距；推理延迟和计算成本构成实际部署约束，混合控制是当前更可行的折中方案。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra exhibits a remarkable ability to generate numerical robot actions, extending its role beyond high-level planning. To assess Astra's capabilities as general-purpose embodied policies, we conduct comprehensive evaluations across six domains, examining direct control, cooperation with learned policies, and feedback-driven adaptation. In gripper manipulation, Astra can correct task targets and prepare contact conditions for subsequent policy execution; hybrid control with {\pi}0.5 achieves 48% success on the evaluated RoboDojo subset. In dexterous manipulation, hybrid control achieves 50% success in ten experience-guided DexJoCo trials, while direct in-hand control struggles to coordinate finger contacts. In mobile manipulation, hybrid control reaches 38.7% success on the evaluated RoboCasa365. In navigation, Astra leads our local comparisons, reaching 92% success on RxR instruction following and 82% on HM3D object search, although search incurs substantial detours. In locomotion, dense motion-reference generation remains unreliable: none of five sequential attempts on a single obstacle course reaches the goal, despite improvements in stability and forward progress. In humanoid loco-manipulation, Astra exceeds baseline methods on 13 of 30 HumanoidBench tasks with pretrained whole-body controllers. These findings reveal a gap between useful task decisions and reliable physical control. Inference latency further constrains practical control: across 50 RoboDojo instances per condition, policy-assisted and direct control consume 624.8 million and 1.132 billion tokens. A 30-second locomotion run requires 250 model calls averaging 39.86 seconds each, with physics paused during inference.

</details>


### [209] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of a Driving VLA to Class 8 Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38570)
*Satyajeet Das,Aaron Buxbaum,Niels Joubert,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将乘用车VLA迁移到Class 8重型卡车：先用数百个真实施工/事故高速场景对动作生成栈做定向微调（adapt），再用流时间条件化的残差模块FVS修正动作空间流速度（steer），开环ADE/FDE减半以上并进一步降低13.9%/16.5%，数据效率显著优于通用卡车监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Class 8卡车在几何、动力学与操纵需求上与乘用车差异大，乘用车VLA难以迁移到事故现场、施工区等非结构化场景；从零训练卡车VLA代价高，故探索数据高效的"先适配再引导"迁移路径。

Method: 以NVIDIA Alpamayo 1.5为基座，仅微调其动作生成栈（数百个真实施工/事故高速场景）；再冻结适配后模型，引入Flow Velocity Steering（FVS）——一个紧凑的、以流时间为条件的残差模块，对每个生成步用于更新动作序列的动作空间流速度加学习式修正。

Result: 场景不相交留出集开环评测中，定向微调使6.4 s全时长单候选ADE与FDE相对基座降低一半以上；FVS进一步使全时长ADE、FDE分别降低13.9%、16.5%；相同数据预算下，定向监督比通用卡车驾驶监督全时长ADE低19–26%，且与用约65倍通用场景微调的模型性能相当。

Conclusion: adapt-then-steer可在少量目标域数据下实现卡车域VLA迁移，为数据高效的跨车辆域适配提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Class 8 trucks differ from passenger cars in geometry, dynamics, and maneuvering requirements. As a result, vision-language-action (VLA) models trained for passenger vehicles do not readily transfer to Class 8 trucks, particularly in unstructured scenarios such as accident scenes and construction zones. Rather than training a truck-driving VLA from scratch, we propose an adapt-then-steer strategy that adapts an off-the-shelf VLA to generate trajectories for Class-8 trucks in these challenging scenarios. In the adapt stage, we use NVIDIA's Alpamayo 1.5 as the base model, fine-tuning only its action-generation stack on a few hundred real-world construction and accident-related highway scenarios. In the steer stage, we introduce Flow Velocity Steering (FVS) to further refine the model's predictions while holding the adapted VLA fixed. FVS is a compact, flow-time-conditioned residual module that adds learned corrections to the action-space flow velocity used to update the action sequence at each generation step. In open-loop evaluation on a scenario-disjoint held-out set, targeted fine-tuning more than halves single-candidate average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) over the entire 6.4 s horizon compared to the base model. Using the same targeted demonstrations, FVS further reduces the fine-tuned model's full-horizon ADE and FDE by 13.9% and 16.5%, respectively. At matched data budgets, targeted supervision yields 19-26% lower full-horizon ADE than general truck-driving supervision, while the targeted model remains competitive with a model fine-tuned on approximately 65 times as many general truck-driving scenarios. These results support adapt-then-steer for data-efficient vehicle-domain transfer to Class 8 trucks. Our project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [210] [Drone Soccer: Learning to Manipulate with Multicopter Downwash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38588)
*Neelay Joglekar,Bavin Saravanan,Yutong Wang,Varun Kandiyappan,Junyi Geng,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用多旋翼无人机螺旋桨产生的下洗气流（downwash）作为主动操作工具，构建"无人机足球"运球任务，设计简化下洗动力学模型并训练RL策略实现带球，策略成功迁移至真实无人机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAM（空中机械臂）在执行移动操作任务时通常忽略下洗气流，或仅将其视为扰动量。作者提出反向思路：能否主动利用下洗气流作为操作工具，从而拓展多旋翼无人机的操作能力边界。

Method: 以"无人机足球带球"作为探索性任务。建立简化的下洗气流动力学模型，基于该模型在仿真中训练强化学习策略来完成带球动作，并将该策略迁移到真实无人机平台上验证。

Result: 基于简化下洗模型训练的RL带球策略可以成功迁移到真实世界部署，验证了下洗气流驱动操作（downwash-based manipulation）的可行性。

Conclusion: 下洗气流可作为主动操作手段而非干扰，本工作为多旋翼无人机的新型操作能力提供了关键洞见；但目前限于简化模型与单一任务，任务复杂度与通用性仍有待扩展。

Abstract: Although multicopter drones are traditionally designed for "perception-only" tasks, like mapping and exploration, recent work has sought to develop Unmanned Aerial Manipulators (UAMs) to solve mobile manipulation tasks. Aerial manipulation performance can be impacted by "downwash," the airflow produced by propellers, but current state-of-the-art UAMs either ignore downwash or treat it as a disturbance. Instead, is it possible to actively use downwash as a tool during manipulation? We design a drone soccer task to explore the feasibility of downwash-based manipulation. Specifically, we develop a simplified downwash dynamics model which we use to train an RL policy to dribble a soccer ball. We further demonstrate that our policy transfers to real world deployment. This work provides key insights into novel manipulation capabilities for multicopters.

</details>


### [211] [FAST-Sync: Fast Group Synchronization for any Matrix Lie Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38594)
*Shane Holmes,Yiran Luo,Firat Taxpulat,David M. Rosen,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Fast-Sync：一种面向任意矩阵李群的快速线性同步（GS）初始化方法，通过泛化 chordal 初始化并利用 Kronecker 积结构与同步图拓扑加速，为局部流形优化器或可认证全局方法提供高质量初值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群同步（GS）是机器人状态估计与 3D 视觉的核心子问题，但问题高维且非凸，直接求解困难；现有局部求解器依赖良好初始化，可认证全局方法也常需初值以加速收敛。作者希望提供一种快速、可扩展且精度足够的初始化手段。

Method: 给出 Fast-Sync 线性近似方法：将 chordal initialization 推广到任意矩阵李群；提出两项算法增强——(1) 利用问题数据矩阵中的 Kronecker 积结构，(2) 利用同步图的拓扑（稀疏性/连通结构）以提升速度、可扩展性与精度。

Result: 在多个 GS 任务上的实验表明，Fast-Sync 产生的高质量初始化可使局部优化器高效收敛到全局最优 GS 解，在相当大测量噪声下仍保持高成功率。

Conclusion: Fast-Sync 是通用、快速的 GS 初始化方法，可作为局部流形优化与全局可认证方法的前端，兼顾速度、可扩展性与精度。

Abstract: Group synchronization (GS) is the problem of estimating a set of $N$ unknown elements $g_1,\ldots, g_N \in \mathcal{G}$ in a group $\mathcal{G}$, given noisy measurements of a subset of their pairwise ratios $g_i^{-1} g_j$. GS problems lie at the core of many state estimation tasks in robotics and computer vision, including 3D vision, robotic mapping, inertial navigation, and molecular reconstruction. Unfortunately, GS problems are typically both high-dimensional and non-convex, and therefore hard to solve in general. In this paper, we present Fast-Sync, a fast linear approximation method for GS that is suitable for initializing local manifold-based optimizers or certifiable global methods. Our approach generalizes chordal initialization to arbitrary matrix Lie groups, and additionally proposes two new key algorithmic enhancements: we show how to exploit both the Kronecker-product structure in the problem data matrix and the topology of the synchronization graph to improve speed, scalability, and accuracy. Experimental evaluation across several GS tasks demonstrates that Fast-Sync provides high-quality initializations that enable local optimizers to efficiently recover globally optimal GS solutions, achieving high success rates even with considerable measurement noise.

</details>


### [212] [Correcting WHERE, Preserving HOW: Compositional Generalization for Vision-Language-Action Models via Referential Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38616)
*Yanyan Zhang,Disheng Liu,Xinpeng Li,Chaoda Song,Mohsen Hariri,Debargha Ganguly,Wang Yang,Kai Ye,Bryce Grant,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练包装模块 ReGuide，用语义+几何重绑定把机械臂末端引导到示教支持构型，让冻结 VLA 策略恢复执行，从而提升组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 在机器人数据多样性不足时依赖视觉捷径，把动作绑定到任务无关的视觉特征，导致在已见元素的重组（组合泛化）下失效；现有方法缺乏对未见重组的显式机制，且需针对骨干重训练。

Method: 观察到组合偏移下 VLA 全局接地失败但保留局部操作技能。ReGuide 作为 training-free 包装器：利用 grounding 模块提供的物体位姿，结合语义重绑定与几何重绑定，将末端执行器引导至指令指涉物在示教分布内支持的构型，随后由冻结策略接管执行。

Result: 在多个 VLA 骨干的仿真实验和真机上，组合偏移场景成功率分别最高提升 56.8 与 75.0 个百分点，同时保持标准任务性能。

Conclusion: 免训练、骨干无关的参照引导可有效缓解视觉捷径导致的组合泛化失败，且不牺牲原有任务表现，为现有 VLA 提供即插即用的泛化增强。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable flexible action generation, their generalization across diverse environmental elements, including manipulated objects, destinations, and backgrounds, is limited by the lack of diversity in robotic training data. Trained end-to-end on such data, VLAs tend to exploit visual shortcuts, associating actions with task-irrelevant visual features rather than the intended task semantics. These shortcuts block recomposition of elements already seen by the policy, that is, compositional generalization. Existing approaches mitigate such entanglement through task-relevant perception or targeted data diversification, but offer no explicit mechanism for unseen recomposition and require backbone-specific modifications with retraining. We observe that under such recomposition, VLAs often fail at global grounding while retaining local manipulation skills that recover near the correct target in familiar configurations. Therefore, we propose Referential Guidance (ReGuide), a training-free wrapper that, given object poses from a grounding module, combines semantic and geometric rebinding to guide the end-effector into demonstration-supported configurations of the instructed referent, where the frozen policy can resume execution. Experiments in simulation across multiple VLA backbones as well as on a real robot show that ReGuide improves success rates under compositional shifts by up to 56.8 and 75.0 percentage points, respectively, while preserving standard-task performance.

</details>


### [213] [Dense Temporal Motion Retargeting for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38617)
*Jaeryeong Kim,Taerim Yoon,Jin Cheng,Sungjoon Choi,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出密集时间运动重定向（DTMR），在单一优化中联合优化时序与控制（每个控制步都调整时序），用GPU并行采样MPC求解，在4个类人机器人、2小时人体运动数据上优于基线，尤其动态动作，并已迁移到真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人与动物的运动与机器人存在形态差距，运动需按机器人动力学特性裁剪；跳跃等动态运动因时序与控制相互耦合，必须有精细调整，而现有方法难以只改动真正需要变时序的部分。

Method: DTMR将时序与控制放在同一个优化问题中联合求解，时序在每一个控制步上都被调整（dense），从而只对需要改变时序的片段进行变形；问题用基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）在GPU上并行求解。基线与评测涉及人类运动数据与多种类人机器人。

Result: 在2小时人体运动、4个类人机器人上与基线对比，DTMR整体更优，动态运动上优势明显；允许更多时间变形可带来更精确的重定向；与优化时间维度的基线相比，在相同变形预算下DTMR重定向更精确且约快19倍；在DTMR参考轨迹上训练的策略可迁移到真实类人机器人。

Conclusion: 将时序与控制联合密集优化是提升运动重定向精度与效率的有效途径，尤其适合动态运动，且产物可直接用于真机策略学习。

Abstract: Legged robots can learn expressive whole-body skills from the motions of humans and animals. Due to the morphology gap between the source and the robot, however, the motion must be tailored to the dynamic properties of the robot. In particular, dynamic motions such as a jump require careful adjustment, since their timing and control are interdependent. We propose dense temporal motion retargeting (DTMR), which jointly optimizes timing and control within a single optimization, where dense means that the timing is adjusted for every control step. This dense formulation enables DTMR to deform only the parts of the motion that need a change in timing. The problem is solved with sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) in parallel on a GPU. We evaluate DTMR against baselines on two hours of human motion with four humanoid robots, where the results show that DTMR outperforms baseline methods, particularly on dynamic motions. We also show that allowing more temporal deformation yields more precise retargeting. We further compare DTMR with a baseline that optimizes the temporal dimension, where the result shows that DTMR retargets more precisely under the same deformation budget while being ~19x faster. Lastly, policies trained on our references transfer to a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [214] [Yggdrasil: a Layer-First 3D Scene Graph for Real-Time Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38640)
*Arshia Akhavan,Ermanno Bartoli,Afnan Algharbi,Alireza Hoseinpur,Iolanda Leite,Bryan Donyanavard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Yggdrasil 提出首个兼顾生成与消费效率的 3D 场景图：以“层优先”的通用节点-边-层层次结构表达现有平面/层次化、室内/室外表示，并原生支持下游任务的位置与语义查询。查询比 DSG 基线快至 121 倍（2–127 微秒），远低于 200 微秒关键帧预算；集成进三个人类轨迹预测、物体目标导航、人感知运动规划流水线，场景图耗时最多减少 99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DSG 研究几乎都聚焦于生成（感知管线），忽视图的消费效率，导致下游任务需绕过自身场景图结构并付出无法满足实时感知循环的延迟代价。作者希望设计一种同时为生成与消费优化的场景图。

Method: 提出 Yggdrasil：层优先（layer-first）的层次化图结构，由通用节点、边、层构成，可表达已有流水线产出的表示（室内/室外、平面/层次），并原生回答下游任务的位置与语义查询；配套基准测试框架（benchmark harness）。

Result: 相较已发表的 DSG 基线，查询最高快 121 倍，单次查询耗时 2–127 微秒，在工作站与嵌入式设备上均位于 200 微秒关键帧预算内 3–5 个数量级。集成到三个人类轨迹预测、物体目标导航、人感知运动规划流水线，最多消除其 99% 的场景图耗时。

Conclusion: Yggdrasil 证明场景图设计应同时面向生成与消费效率，可无缝嵌入现有感知-规划流水线并大幅降低延迟；实现、基准与三个集成均已开源。

Abstract: Robotic agents use 3D scene graphs (3DSG) to perform tasks ranging from scene understanding to scene interaction. Although an extensive body of work addresses scene graph generation, little attention has been paid to optimizing the graph for consumption, which leaves state-of-the-art perception pipelines to work around their own scene graph and to pay a latency cost that does not fit the real-time budget a perception loop runs on. We present Yggdrasil, the first 3D scene graph designed to be efficient for both generation and consumption: a layer-first hierarchical graph built from generic nodes, edges, and layers, which expresses the representations existing pipelines already produce, indoor or outdoor, flat or hierarchical, while natively answering the positional and semantic queries downstream tasks issue. Against a published DSG baseline, Yggdrasil answers queries up to $121\times$ faster, and every query we measure falls between 2 and 127 microseconds, three to five orders of magnitude inside the 200 microsecond keyframe budget a 3DSG consumer lives in, on both a workstation and embedded class device. We integrate Yggdrasil into three published pipelines spanning human trajectory prediction, object-goal navigation, and human-aware motion planning, where it removes up to 99% of the time each spends on its scene graph. The implementation, benchmark harness, and all three integrations are available online.

</details>


### [215] [TERRA: Terrain-Aware Reconstruction, Retargeting and Control for Musculoskeletal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38653)
*Merkourios Simos,Chengkun Li,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TERRA提出端到端流水线，从无场景的运动学轨迹中恢复任务相关支撑地形，并在解剖、肌腱连续性、接触约束下完成地形感知重定向，进而用5个数据集生成的运动-地形配对训练单一肌肉驱动控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉驱动的肌肉骨骼智能体已能复现复杂人体运动，但能力基本局限于平地：运动数据集普遍缺少与运动对齐的地形几何，且将地形交互重定向到复杂肌肉骨骼模型上十分困难。作者希望打通从"无场景运动数据"到"非平地肌肉驱动运动"的实用路径。

Method: TERRA为端到端流水线：(1) 仅从运动学轨迹出发，融合地形先验、估计的接触点与负自由空间证据，恢复与任务相关的支撑几何；(2) 重定向阶段同时施加解剖约束、肌腱连续性约束与接触约束；(3) 用5个数据集生成的运动-地形配对，训练单一肌肉驱动控制策略（9.4小时多样化运动数据）。

Result: 在重建、重定向与留出跟踪三类基准上，TERRA均提升地形精度，显著减少解剖与交互违规，并在各类支撑地形族上取得最高的完成率；成功用单一策略驱动9.4小时多样化运动。

Conclusion: TERRA提供了从无场景运动数据到多样化非平地肌肉驱动运动的可行路线，把地形恢复与受约束重定向统一起来，为肌肉骨骼模型在复杂地形上的运动生成与控制奠定基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in musculoskeletal modeling and reinforcement learning have enabled muscle-actuated agents to reproduce increasingly complex human motions. Yet these capabilities remain largely confined to flat ground, in part because motion datasets rarely include aligned terrain geometry and because retargeting terrain interactions to complex musculoskeletal bodies is challenging. We present TERRA, an end-to-end pipeline for terrain-aware retargeting and control of musculoskeletal locomotion. From kinematic trajectories alone, TERRA combines terrain priors, estimated contacts, and negative free-space evidence to recover task-relevant support geometry. TERRA further considers anatomical, tendon-continuity, and contact constraints during retargeting. Using the resulting motion-terrain pairs from five datasets, we successfully train a single muscle-actuated control policy on 9.4 hours of diverse locomotion. Across reconstruction, retargeting, and held-out tracking benchmarks, TERRA improves terrain accuracy, sharply reduces anatomical and interaction violations, and achieves the highest observed completion rate over supported terrain families. Overall, TERRA provides a practical route from scene-less motion data to muscle-actuated locomotion over diverse non-flat terrain. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [216] [Residual Wrench Certification and Margin-Aware Control Synthesis for Aerial Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38707)
*Abhimanyu Khadga,Abhinav Sinha,Shashi Ranjan Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务相关的残差力矩权限认证框架：用凸几何推导有符号裕度、可计算储备证书、指令投影与增益综合，并证明局部指数收敛与鲁棒最终有界性；仿真对比八旋翼与可变倾转四旋翼的接触权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼在执行悬停与接触作业时，执行器有界会限制可用力矩权限，缺乏能按任务量化"剩余控制权限"的可认证方法，且不同形貌（八旋翼 vs 可变倾转四旋翼）在等推力下的权限差异缺乏理论比较。

Method: 以凸几何为基础建立任务相关框架：为欧氏球与加权椭球等凸储备定义有符号裕度；由执行器内点界导出可计算的储备证书以支持松弛最大化分配；通过把指令投影到收紧的可行集来保持所需储备；将可用储备接入结构化增益综合，认证满足执行器限制的局部跟踪区域，并在该区域内证明标称指数收敛与鲁棒最终有界性。

Result: 理论上证明在给定任务与形貌族下，等总推力时优化后的八旋翼裕度大于优化后的六旋翼；仿真中刚性壁持续接触下，等安装推力时可变倾转四旋翼的局部证书给出更高的归一化推压极限；越界测试中观察到预测的权限丧失分别源于八旋翼的旋翼推力上限与四旋翼的倾转舵机上限。

Conclusion: 该框架将接触工况下的执行器权限量化为可计算、可认证的储备裕度，并可用于形貌/任务级设计比较与控制器综合；权限瓶颈可明确归因到具体执行器饱和类型，为接触作业多旋翼的能力边界提供了定理化的分析工具。

Abstract: We develop a task-relative framework for certifying residual wrench authority after hover and contact loading in multirotors with bounded actuators. Using convex geometry, we derive signed margins for prescribed convex reserves, including Euclidean balls and weighted ellipsoids. We obtain computable reserve certificates from actuator-interiority bounds to support slack-maximizing allocation. To preserve the required reserve, we propose command projection onto a tightened feasible set. We then connect the available reserve to structured gain synthesis and certify a local tracking region that respects actuator limits. Within this region, we establish nominal exponential convergence and robust ultimate boundedness. For the prescribed task and morphology family, we show that the optimized octarotor attains a larger margin than the optimized hexarotor at equal total thrust. We evaluate the proposed framework in closed-loop simulations of sustained rigid-wall contact using a fixed-geometry octarotor and a variable-tilt quadrotor with matched installed thrust. The quadrotor's local certificate admits a higher normalized push limit for the prescribed task family. In tests beyond the realizability boundaries, we observe the predicted loss of authority through rotor-thrust limits for the octarotor and tilt-servo limits for the quadrotor.

</details>


### [217] [CEER2: Directional and Tunable End-Effector and Root Compliance for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38709)
*Xinyuan Luo,Chunyuan Yang,Boyuan Chen,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向人形机器人全身运动操作的柔顺框架，结合方向可调末端柔顺与可选根部柔顺（外力抑制或跟随），用分层强化学习通过高层指令调制固定全身跟踪策略，并从本体感觉历史估计交互力，仿真与真机验证了刚度方向控制、在线调节、协作搬运等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人已能跟踪复杂全身运动，但物理接触带来新挑战：接触人/环境时需在响应外力的同时保持任务所需运动，且这种响应在不同末端方向与身体部位上需求不同（有的方向需要顺应接触力、有的方向需保持运动精度；躯干可能需抵抗外力或随外力运动）。现有全身跟踪策略缺乏这种可跨方向配置的柔顺性。

Method: 分层强化学习控制器：底层为固定的全身运动跟踪策略，高层输出末端执行器（EE）柔顺命令与根部（root）柔顺命令，用以在线调制该跟踪策略；EE支持方向性刚度与在线刚度调节，root支持在"外力抑制/外力跟随"间切换；交互力由本体感觉历史估计（无需力传感器）。

Result: 在仿真与真机上验证：方向性刚度控制、在线刚度调整、不同的根部柔顺模式、柔顺操作以及人-机器人协作搬运。

Conclusion: 该框架使人形机器人在全身运动操作中获得可配置、可在线调整的方向性柔顺，兼顾接触力响应与任务运动精度；真机演示表明其在柔顺操作与人机协作搬运上具备实用性。

Abstract: Humanoids are increasingly capable of tracking complex whole-body motions, but physical interaction introduces a different challenge. When a robot makes contact with a person or the environment, it needs to respond to external forces while preserving the motion needed for the task. This response can vary across directions in the end-effectors and on the body. For example, an end effector may need to accommodate contact force in one direction while maintaining motion accuracy in another, while the robot body may resist an external force or move with it. We present a compliance framework for humanoid loco-manipulation that combines directional and tunable end-effector (EE) compliance with selectable root compliance for external force rejection or force following. A hierarchical reinforcement learning controller modulates a fixed whole-body tracking policy through high-level EE and root commands, while interaction forces are estimated from proprioceptive history. Our simulation and real-world experiments on a humanoid demonstrate directional stiffness control, online stiffness adjustment, distinct root compliance, compliant manipulation, and collaborative carrying.

</details>


### [218] [Path-Following Control and Terramechanics Analysis for Planetary Rovers Under Wheel-to-Wheel Traction Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38715)
*Ryuya Matsuoka,Keisuke Takehana,Kentaro Uno,Toshinori Kuwahara,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无模型、仅靠减速的滑移转向星球车路径跟踪控制策略，通过限制指令速度上限并仅对外侧车轮减速使其进入负滑转状态作为机械锚点，抑制可变形松软地形上的滑移下陷与横向漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动学的控制器在路径纠偏时会加速车轮，在松软可变形地形及持续牵引力不对称条件下易引发滑转-下陷（slip-sinkage）导致车辆被困。作者希望找到一种不依赖模型、且不通过加速纠偏的控制方式，从根本上避免这一失效模式。

Method: 无模型控制策略：设定最大指令速度上限以避免轮子加速；航向纠正仅通过选择性减速转弯外侧车轮，使其进入负滑转区充当机械锚；在四轮独立驱动样车EX1上进行验证，采用左右轮齿高不同的非对称轮配置以诱发显著路径偏差；借助车载多轴力传感器直接测量轮-土作用力。

Result: 在最高0.7 m/s的多种速度工况下成功抑制累积横向漂移，且未产生下陷。多轴力测量提供了地形力学经验证据：定向减速使底盘载荷动态均衡，并完全恢复了反向驱动轮的原生推力能力。

Conclusion: 仅靠减速的路径跟踪控制可在牵引力不对称的松软地形上实现无下陷的鲁棒路径跟随，其有效性得到了实车实验与直接力测量的双重支撑，为星球车及滑移转向平台的松软地形运动控制提供了一条低风险、无需模型的实现路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a control strategy for path following that is model-free and relies solely on deceleration for skid-steering planetary rovers navigating deformable loose terrain under continuously imposed traction asymmetry. While conventional controllers that are based on kinematics frequently cause slip-sinkage entrapment by accelerating the wheels during path correction, the proposed approach prevents this failure by setting an upper limit on the maximum commanded velocity. Heading correction is achieved solely through the selective deceleration of the outer wheels, which are located on the outside of the turn, driving them into a negative slip regime to act as a mechanical anchor. The system was evaluated using the four-wheel independent-drive rover EX1 under an asymmetric wheel configuration with different left and right grouser heights that induces significant deviations from the path. Experimental results demonstrate that this deceleration-only control successfully suppresses accumulated lateral drift across various velocity regimes up to 0.7 m/s without causing sinkage. Crucially, direct force measurements from onboard multi-axis sensors provide important empirical evidence of the underlying terramechanics, proving that the targeted deceleration establishes dynamic load equalization across the chassis and completely restores the native thrust capability of the opposite driving wheel.

</details>


### [219] [FlapKAD: A Simulation Dataset of Coupled Wing Kinematics and Aerodynamic Dynamics for Flapping-Wing Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38719)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlapKAD：包含2000条刚翼飞行episode、720152个有效时间步的扑翼飞行器仿真数据集，同步记录双侧翼运动学、气动响应与飞行状态，并构建正/逆双向序列预测基准，评测8种时序模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器研究受限于缺乏大规模、时间对齐的数据：现有数据规模小、噪声大，高频扑动下快速变化的翼运动与气动/飞行状态响应之间存在时间错位，阻碍了运动学—气动—飞行状态的耦合建模。

Method: 构建FlapKAD情节式（episode-structured）仿真数据集，在统一时钟下同步采集双侧翼扑动角/扭转角、气动力系数与飞行状态；基于同一对齐情节设计统一的双向序列预测基准：正向任务由历史扑动与扭转角预测未来垂直力系数与机身垂直速度，逆向任务由响应历史重建未来扑动与扭转角轨迹；在多个预测时域上评测8种代表性时序架构。

Result: 基准结果显示：模型行为依赖于预测方向与时域；扭转角的重建误差系统性高于扑动角；架构复杂度提升并未带来一致优势。

Conclusion: FlapKAD为研究扑翼飞行器中翼运动学、气动与飞行状态的耦合动力学提供了可复现的数据集与基准。

Abstract: Experimental investigation and modeling of flapping-wing aerial vehicles are limited by the scarcity of large-scale records that temporally align wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states. Existing datasets are often limited in scale and affected by measurement noise and temporal misalignment between rapidly varying wing motion and the associated dynamic response, particularly during high-frequency flapping. We introduce FlapKAD, an episode-structured simulation dataset comprising 2,000 rigid-wing flight episodes and 720,152 valid time steps, with bilateral wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states recorded synchronously within a common clock. FlapKAD supports a unified bidirectional sequence-prediction benchmark constructed from the same temporally aligned episodes. The forward task predicts future vertical force coefficients and body vertical velocity from histories of realized flap and twist angles, whereas the inverse task reconstructs future flap- and twist-angle trajectories from the corresponding response histories. A benchmark of eight representative time-series architectures across multiple prediction horizons reveals direction- and horizon-dependent model behavior, systematically higher reconstruction errors for twist angle than for flap angle, and no consistent advantage from increased architectural complexity. FlapKAD provides a reproducible dataset and benchmark for studying coupled wing-kinematic, aerodynamic, and flight-state dynamics in flapping-wing aerial vehicles.

</details>


### [220] [Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Learning for Forward-Consistent Inverse Flapping-Wing Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38730)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BCS-GRU模型，以循环时序预测为主、受限谱域残差为输出特定修正，并用冻结气动代理模型做前向一致性微调，改善扑翼气动逆建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器需通过stroke、deviation、pitch协同调节气动力与力矩，载荷同时依赖当前翼面构型与历史运动，因此由目标气动轨迹反解运动学是困难逆问题。现有序列模型能建模时序依赖、谱方法可利用周期结构，但无约束的频域增强会干扰时序表征，且在各运动学变量与预测时域间产生不一致修正。

Method: 提出Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Residual Gated Recurrent Unit（BCS-GRU）：以GRU循环时序预测为主表征，将谱信息限定为受约束的、输出通道自适应的残差修正；并引入因果对齐的前向一致性目标，把预测运动学输入单独训练且冻结的气动代理模型进行评价。

Result: 在统一episode级协议下，BCS-GRU相较循环基线（GRU）与自适应谱基线提升逆预测精度，且在更长预测时域上增益更明显；前向一致性微调进一步改善基于代理模型的气动一致性，同时保持平均运动学精度。

Conclusion: 受控谱修正与前向一致性学习为扑翼气动的历史感知逆建模提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Flapping-wing vehicles regulate aerodynamic forces and moments through coordinated variations in stroke, deviation, and pitch motion. Because the resulting loads depend on both the instantaneous wing configuration and its preceding motion history, recovering suitable wing kinematics from a desired aerodynamic trajectory is a challenging inverse problem. Existing sequence models capture temporal dependencies, while spectral methods can exploit the periodic structure of flapping motion. However, unrestricted frequency-domain augmentation may interfere with temporal representations and produce inconsistent corrections across kinematic variables and prediction horizons. We propose the Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Residual Gated Recurrent Unit (BCS-GRU), which retains recurrent temporal prediction as its primary representation and restricts spectral information to a controlled output-specific correction. We further introduce a causally aligned forward-consistency objective that evaluates predicted kinematics through a separately trained and frozen aerodynamic surrogate. Experiments under a unified episode-level protocol show that BCS-GRU improves inverse prediction over recurrent and adaptive-spectral baselines, with greater benefits at longer prediction horizons. Forward-consistent fine-tuning further improves surrogate-based aerodynamic consistency while maintaining mean kinematic accuracy. These results demonstrate that controlled spectral correction and forward-consistent learning provide an effective framework for history-aware inverse modelling of flapping-wing aerodynamics.

</details>


### [221] [AIfred: Augmented Learning through Functional Robotic Embodiment at the Desk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38737)
*Gregorio Orlando,Milan Groshev,Eduardo Castelló Ferrer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 设计桌面机械臂+末端投影器系统 AIfred，将 AI 生成指导直接投射到手写作业旁，实现空间共置的辅助；用户研究(n=36)显示在帮助撤除后短期学习迁移提升 60%，绘画质量评分 33/36 优于 ChatGPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式 AI 工具（如 ChatGPT）通过独立屏幕提供指导，与用户实际书写、思考的桌面空间分离，形成"认知位置"与"辅助位置"的错位，需要用户频繁在纸面与屏幕间切换，打断手写任务流。

Method: 构建 AIfred：桌面机械臂 + 末端执行器搭载投影仪，融合工作空间感知（手写内容与纸面状态识别）、上下文感知内容生成（AI 生成指导）与机器人介导的空间投影三个模块，支持数学作业、图像生成、绘画三类任务；开展 n=36 的用户研究，与笔记本上运行的 ChatGPT (GPT-5.6 Luna) 对照。

Result: 有辅助时数学任务表现相当（6.7 vs 7.3/10，p=.41）；撤除辅助后 AIfred 组短期学习迁移提升 60%（7.0 vs 4.4/10，p=.003）；独立艺术与设计教授在 36 组中 33 组认为 AIfred 辅助完成的绘画更好。

Conclusion: 空间共置的 AI 辅助对"指导与作业共享同一空间框架"的任务具有显著优势，尤其在辅助撤除后的知识保留与迁移方面；为物理化 AI 辅助界面提供了设计范式与实证依据。

Abstract: Desk-based learning and creative activities benefit from handwritten engagement. However, current generative AI tools deliver guidance through a separate screen, creating a gap between where users think and where assistance appears. To address this, in this work we design AIfred, a desk-based robotic arm with a projector mounted at the end-effector that places AI-generated guidance alongside handwritten work. AIfred combines workspace perception, context-aware content generation, and robot-mediated projection to support math assignments, image generation, and drawing tasks. In a user study (n = 36), we compared AIfred against ChatGPT (GPT-5.6 Luna) running on a laptop. Both tools performed comparably while assistance was available during the math assignment (6.7 vs. 7.3/10, p = .41), but AIfred improved short-term learning transfer by 60% once assistance was withdrawn (7.0 vs. 4.4/10, p = .003). In addition, independent art and design professors ranked drawings produced with AIfred better in 33 of 36 cases. Our findings indicate that spatially co-located AI assistance benefits tasks whose guidance shares a spatial frame with the work.

</details>


### [222] [HALO: Heterogeneous Allocation Via Localized Observations for the Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38760)
*Andrew Meighan,Hyungsub Kim,Or Dantsker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO 提出一种混合式机器人车队控制方法，将车辆路径问题（VRP）分解为分配与路由两阶段，利用异构图神经网络在部分可观测、通信受限的在线环境中实现车载实时求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车队控制算法多在受控环境下求解集中式路径问题（可达约1000任务），但忽略了去中心化车队中常见的有限观测与通信范围等现实约束，导致难以落地。因此需要一个能在动态、部分可观测环境下给出在线解的可扩展方法。

Method: HALO 将 VRP 拆分为分配（allocation）与路由（routing）两部分，以实现车载实时求解。分配阶段采用异构图神经网络框架，通过专门的多种消息传递层显式分离两类学习：空间分布学习与任务-机器人兼容性学习。方法对观测空间不设严格上界，因而可直接迁移到传统静态单仓库 VRP 场景。

Result: 在部分可观测的在线 VRP 变体上，HALO 显著优于启发式基线，且解质量接近全知离线版本。在静态单仓库 VRP 上，HALO 相较严格针对静态变体优化的先进架构最高提升 14.06%。所有测试中，该方法始终保持最快的执行时间。

Conclusion: HALO 通过局域观测下的异构图学习兼顾分布式约束与解质量，并具备跨问题变体的通用性与最快执行速度，展现大规模实时车队部署的潜力。

Abstract: Scalable robotic fleets have become increasingly popular for various applications such as package delivery, warehouse management, and military operations. Prior fleet control algorithms solve centralized routing problems with up to $1{,}000$ tasks in controlled environments, yet they fail to consider realistic constraints such as limited observation and communication ranges typical of decentralized fleets. Thus, deploying existing fleet control algorithms into real-world settings is currently infeasible. To tackle this, we propose Heterogeneous Allocation via Localized Observations (HALO) to solve the Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP). HALO is a hybrid method that splits the VRP into allocation and routing portions to provide onboard, real-time solutions to robots in dynamic environments. During the allocation phase, HALO utilizes a heterogeneous graph neural network framework with unique message passing layers to explicitly separate the learning of spatial distributions and task-to-robot compatibility. Evaluation results on a partially observable, online variant of the VRP show HALO significantly outperforms the heuristic baseline while maintaining similar solution quality to an all-knowing offline variant of HALO. While HALO is explicitly designed for partially observable environments, it imposes no strict upper bound on the observation space allowing us to test HALO on the traditional static, single-depot VRP. Here, HALO outperforms state-of-the-art architectures strictly optimized for the static variant of the VRP by up to $14.06\%$. Throughout all testing, this framework maintains the quickest execution times which emphasizes its potential for large-scale, real-time deployment.

</details>


### [223] [PhaseSync-Exo: Human Clock Anchored Reference Adaptation for Dynamic Gait Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38824)
*Kaijie Qi,Yuehan Wang,Kaiming Xu,Chong Li,Jiakuo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhaseSync-Exo，结合双IMU CNN-Transformer步态重建、因子化幅值-步频重定向与课程学习循环控制，以及人类时钟锚定适配器（HCA），实现人-外骨骼时序同步的仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类感知外骨骼行走需重建步态、在动态约束下跟踪多样运动并保持人类自身时序节律，现有方法难以在无需重训练的情况下持续对齐独立的人类时钟。

Method: 三部分：(1) 双IMU输入的CNN-Transformer步态重建；(2) 幅值与步频解耦的因子化重定向 + 课程训练循环控制策略；(3) 人类时钟锚定适配器（HCA），将人类时钟吸引力与机器人相对反馈结合，调节参考速率同时保持前进性与连续性。

Result: 重建模块在留出录制上MAE为3.24°；冻结跟踪策略完成357次幅频试验中的356次。相对机器人纠正，HCA将人类时钟相位MAE从95.55°降至10.99°；初始相位失配时相较固定时钟从72.02°降至13.90°；相较立即相位重置，瞬态参考跟踪髋部RMSE降低22.7%且无参考跳变。420次时序rollout全部无跌倒完成。

Conclusion: 仿真结果表明该框架可实现连续相位获取并持续对齐独立人类时钟，为感知人类节律的外骨骼控制提供可行路径，但尚缺真实硬件验证。

Abstract: Human-aware exoskeleton walking requires reconstructing gait, tracking diverse motions under dynamic constraints, and preserving human timing. We present PhaseSync-Exo, which combines two-IMU CNN-Transformer reconstruction, factorized amplitude-cadence retargeting with curriculum-trained recurrent control, and a human-clock-anchored adapter (HCA). HCA combines human-clock attraction with robot-relative feedback to adjust reference rate while preserving forward progression and continuity. The reconstruction module achieves a mean absolute error (MAE) of 3.24 degrees on a held-out recording, and the frozen tracking policy completes 356 of 357 amplitude-frequency trials. Two complementary dynamic comparisons isolate HCA's timing benefit without retraining. Against robot-relative correction, HCA reduces human-clock phase MAE from 95.55 degrees to 10.99 degrees, limiting reference drift. When human and delivered phases initially differ, it reduces phase MAE from 72.02 degrees to 13.90 degrees versus fixed-clock continuation. Compared with immediate phase reset, HCA reduces transient reference-tracking hip RMSE by 22.7% without reference jumps. All 420 timing rollouts complete without falls. These simulation results support continuous phase acquisition and sustained alignment to an independent human clock.

</details>


### [224] [Coral Grow-out Robotic Assessment System (CGRAS): Scaling Coral Recruit Monitoring Through Robotics and Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38846)
*Dorian Tsai,Scarlett Raine,Emilio Olivastri,Riki Lamont,Andrew Lui,Timothy Morris,Joshua Esplin,Christopher A. Brunner,F. Mikaela Nordborg,Reginald Wardleworth,Garima Samvedi,Karen Jackel,Matthew Dunbabin,Tobias Fischer,Andrea Severati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CGRAS结合机器人成像与计算机视觉，自动检测和计数珊瑚幼苗并评估其健康状况，将监测时间与人力成本降低9.6倍，多物种计数与专家一致率达96.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化威胁珊瑚礁，规模化修复依赖珊瑚幼苗的批量养殖；而数万个毫米级幼苗的高频人工监测成本过高，成为生产瓶颈。

Method: 提出珊瑚培育机器人评估系统CGRAS，集成机械臂/自动化成像与视觉算法，在标准化沉降板上自动采集图像，实现多物种检测、计数、健康评估，并提取生长、存活与空间分布指标。

Result: 在大型水产养殖设施中验证：相比人工监测，时间与人力成本降低9.6倍；对Acropora kenti与专家计数的相符率达96.4%。

Conclusion: 自动化机器人视觉监测可有效缓解珊瑚养殖的监测瓶颈，为放流与部署流程提供实时反馈，支撑可扩展的珊瑚礁修复。

Abstract: Climate change is the largest threat to coral reefs, with increasing global impacts accelerating the need for scalable reef restoration technologies. Large-scale reef restoration depends on the mass production of corals, such as through coral aquaculture. Coral seeding with recruits grown in aquaculture facilities is a feasible restoration approach, but effective production requires consistent, high-frequency monitoring of tens of thousands of macroscopic (0.5-2mm diameter) recruits, making conventional manual assessment prohibitively labor-intensive. To address this monitoring bottleneck, we introduce the Coral Grow-out Robotic Assessment System (CGRAS) which combines robotic imaging and computer vision to automate data acquisition, perform multi-species detection and counting of corals, and evaluate coral health. CGRAS automatically extracts coral growth, survival and spatial distribution metrics, with the aim of providing timely feedback to operators for optimizing production, grow-out and deployment workflow processes. We demonstrate CGRAS in a large aquaculture facility on standardized coral settlement tiles, reducing the time and labor costs by a factor of 9.6 as compared to manual monitoring, whilst achieving 96.4% agreement for Acropora kenti corals relative to expert counts.

</details>


### [225] [Locomotion-Grounded Humanoid Soccer: Task-Gated Reinforcement Learning of a Multi-Directional Kicking Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38852)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Jaehyun Jang,Hwanhee Kim,Kyuwon Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以通用指令条件化运动策略为底层、N个任务门控踢球技能为顶层的倒置分层框架，在Unitree G1上实现7种踢球技能（覆盖259.5°目标方向），并报告指令跟踪、地形与抗推恢复结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人足球系统把运动跟踪作为基底，通过将运动参考锚点导向球来派生移动，导致运动能力仅在狭窄、确定性的接近球指令分布上训练，从未被作为独立能力评估。作者希望让可达步态空间由运动课程而非参考片段决定，并使运动成为技能组合的枢纽。

Method: 倒置分层：先训练一个通用的、指令条件化的运动策略作为基底；再在其上叠加N个运动引导的踢球技能作为任务门控层。每个技能从同一可指令状态出发并返回该状态，使技能间转移复杂度由O(N²)降为O(N)；击球后稳定交由已训练控制器完成而非逐片段脚本化。在29自由度Unitree G1上实例化7种重定向踢球技能。

Result: 技能库覆盖259.5°的标称瞄准方向，包含单一前向参考无法表达的侧向、后向及弱势脚踢击；报告了射门精度，以及附带完整技能库时的指令跟踪、地形通过和推力恢复结果；在硬件上验证了前向、侧向、后向及受指令接近场景。

Conclusion: 以指令条件化运动为基底的倒置设计使运动成为可组合的技能枢纽，扩展了单一参考片段所不能表达的踢击方向空间，并在真机上得到验证；该类别的能力评估维度（指令跟踪、地形、抗扰）此前在类人足球系统中未见报道。

Abstract: Recent humanoid soccer systems make motion tracking the substrate and derive locomotion from it, typically by steering a motion-reference anchor toward the ball. This yields strong shooting results, but locomotion is trained only on the narrow, deterministic command distribution ball approach induces, never evaluated as a capability in its own right. We invert the stack: a general, command-conditioned locomotion policy is trained first as the substrate, and N motion-guided kicking skills are added on top as task-gated layers, so the reachable gait space is set by the locomotion curriculum rather than any reference clip. Because every skill starts from and returns to this same commandable state, locomotion also becomes a composition hub (O(N) transitions rather than O(N^2)), and post-strike stabilisation is handed back to the trained controller rather than scripted per clip. We instantiate this on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 with seven retargeted kicking skills spanning 259.5 degrees of nominal aim direction, including lateral, rearward and weak-foot strikes a single forward-facing reference cannot express, and report shooting accuracy alongside command-tracking, terrain and push-recovery results with the full skill library attached, an axis prior humanoid soccer systems do not report. The library is validated on hardware across forward, lateral, rearward and commanded approaches.

</details>


### [226] [Online Evolution Strategy for Flow-Matching VLA Policies via Self-Supervised Trajectory Distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38855)
*Gongxin Yao,Yongsheng Zhao,Jiayin Deng,Deng Liang,Han Gao,Lei Zhao,Baoping Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Online-ES，用进化策略在动作轨迹空间在线微调 Flow Matching VLA 的条件动作分布，将轨迹扰动+执行反馈转化为自监督 MSE 目标并映射回参数空间，避免学习价值模型或优势函数，仿真与真机均取得接近强化学习微调的策略提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching 类 VLA 建模的是条件动作轨迹分布，但该分布往往"形态不良"：成功与失败行为共存，大量概率质量落在不利区域。已有做法多是裁剪/约束潜噪声空间，间接且次优，作者希望在动作轨迹空间直接进行在线自适应，并在不引入价值模型与优势估计的前提下提升策略。

Method: Online-ES 用进化策略在动作轨迹空间做在线探索：Flow Matching 采样出的多样轨迹作为候选解，对采样轨迹施加扰动后执行并评估结果，由此构造自监督 MSE 目标，把轨迹空间中的进化方向迁移到模型参数空间；论文证明该目标是最优进化方向的无偏估计，并把失败经验作为负反馈正则项，使策略远离已探索的失败区域。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，Online-ES 取得了与强化学习微调相当（comparable）的策略改进，且无需学习价值模型、无需计算优势函数。

Conclusion: 用进化策略在线精炼生成式 VLA 的动作分布是一条有效且低开销的路线：无偏估计算法保证 + 失败负反馈正则，可在真实机器人上稳定带来策略提升，为生成式策略的在线自适应提供了替代 RL 微调的方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models based on generative frameworks, such as Flow Matching, have recently achieved impressive performance in robotic manipulation. Unlike deterministic policies, Flow Matching enables VLA models to learn conditional action trajectory distributions, where latent noise vectors induce different actions under the same task scenario. However, we observe that these distributions are often ill-formed, with successful and failed behaviors coexisting while considerable probability mass remains in unfavorable regions. To this end, we propose Online-ES, an online adaptation framework for Flow Matching VLAs based on Evolution Strategy (ES), which refines the learned action trajectory distribution through interaction feedback. Instead of pruning the latent noise space, our method performs evolutionary exploration directly in the action trajectory space, where diverse trajectories generated by Flow Matching provide candidate solutions for adaptation. By perturbing sampled trajectories and evaluating their execution outcomes, we derive a self-supervised MSE objective that transfers the evolution direction from trajectory space into model parameter space. Mathematically, we prove that the proposed objective provides an unbiased estimator of the optimal evolution direction. Moreover, we also incorporate failure experiences as negative feedback to regularize the evolution direction, steering the policy away from previously explored failure regions. Experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that Online-ES achieves policy improvement comparable to reinforcement fine-tuning, without learning a value model or computing advantages.

</details>


### [227] [Plan-Conditioned Imitation for Robust Object Retrieval under Self-Occlusion in Dense Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38857)
*Kowndinya Boyalakuntla,Ajinkya Pawar,Abdeslam Boularias,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE：一种"计划条件"模仿学习框架，用单帧无遮挡观测初始化数字孪生并生成固定标称 rollout，递归学生网络结合局部上下文、部分物体观测与本体感知纠偏，在自遮挡抓取场景中无需在线教师查询或额外仿真即达 90.7% 仿真/90.0% 真机成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集杂乱环境中检索物体需先重排，机械臂在移动物体时会遮挡目标，为恢复可见性而反复撤回手臂会打断执行、拖慢任务。作者希望在"自遮挡"下实现连续、不停顿的检索，避免依赖在线特权教师或反复仿真调用。

Method: 单次无遮挡观测初始化数字孪生，特权教师在孪生中生成一条固定的标称 rollout（全程不变）。递归学生策略输入局部 rollout 上下文、部分物体观测和本体感知，输出可纠正偏离预测的动作。先用行为克隆初始化，再用 DAgger 在学生访问状态上以教师标签精修。部署时无需在线教师查询，也无需额外仿真 rollout。

Result: 511 个仿真测试场景：TRACE 成功率 90.7%，标称回放 43.4%，特权闭环教师 96.7%。同等 26,373 标签预算下，学生状态监督 87.8% vs 仅专家克隆 66.7%，说明收益来自状态分布而非标签量。UR5e 真机：TRACE 90.0% vs 闭环教师 95.0%，执行总时间由 192.7 s 降至 67.3 s，且避免了教师在推挤过程中平均每回合 16.8 次的感知相关手臂回撤，仅在最后保留一次用于可抓取性评估的撤回。

Conclusion: 以固定标称 rollout + 递归纠偏学生的组合，可在自遮挡检索中兼顾成功率与执行效率，去除了对在线教师与额外仿真回滚的依赖；真机实验显示显著的时间收益与更少的冗余回撤。代码与数据将开源。局限未在摘要中明确说明，如对初始单帧观测质量与孪生保真度的敏感性。

Abstract: Retrieving objects from dense clutter requires rearrangement during which the manipulator can occlude objects while moving them. Repeated arm withdrawals to restore visibility interrupt execution. We introduce TRACE, a plan-conditioned imitation framework for retrieval under self-occlusion. A single unoccluded observation initializes a digital twin, where a privileged teacher generates a fixed nominal rollout. A recurrent student combines local rollout context, partial object observations, and proprioception to select actions that can correct deviations from the prediction. Behavior cloning initializes the student; DAgger refines it with teacher labels on student-visited states. The rollout remains fixed throughout execution, so the deployed student needs neither online teacher queries nor additional simulator rollouts during pushing. On 511 simulation test scenes, TRACE achieves 90.7% success versus 43.4% for nominal replay and 96.7% for the privileged closed-loop teacher. At a matched 26,373-label budget, student-state supervision achieves 87.8% versus 66.7% for expert-only cloning, demonstrating gains beyond additional labels. On a UR5e, TRACE achieves 90.0% success versus 95.0% for the closed-loop teacher, while reducing total execution time from 192.7 s to 67.3 s. It avoids the teacher's 16.8 sensing-related arm retractions per trial during pushing, retaining a final withdrawal for graspability evaluation. Code and data will be released at: this https URL .

</details>


### [228] [Efficient Multi-Modal Planning with Reward-Guided Preference Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38862)
*Chenglin Chen,Lujia Wang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMPlan：面向自动驾驶的高效多模态轨迹规划方法，采用稀疏锚点+偏移精修架构，并用规则奖励信号与无配对偏好监督进行奖励引导微调，在NAVSIM上兼顾精度与实时效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶轨迹规划存在三重矛盾：模仿学习受因果混淆影响、规则打分法计算开销大且目标错位、偏好类方法依赖严格成对标注导致数据利用率低。作者希望在低延迟下同时提升规划安全性与精度。

Method: 混合架构：稀疏锚点提供低延迟粗轨迹候选，偏移精修模块提升预测精度；两阶段训练范式——预训练 + 奖励引导微调，微调阶段利用规则奖励信号与无配对偏好监督，将策略朝更安全的轨迹选择方向优化，且不增加推理成本。

Result: 在非交互式 NAVSIM 基准上评测，规划精度与效率取得良好平衡，在实时约束下表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过奖励引导微调将安全规则知识注入轻量多模态规划器，可在不牺牲推理效率的前提下改善轨迹选择的安全性与精度，为自动驾驶规划提供兼顾效率与安全的方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient trajectory planning is essential in autonomous driving. However, existing end-to-end approaches often fall short in both computational efficiency and safety guarantees. Methods based on imitation learning suffer from causal confusion, while rule-based scoring approaches often incur heavy computational overhead and suffer from objective misalignment. Additionally, preference-based methods rely on strict pairwise annotations, limiting data utilization. To overcome these limitations, we propose EMPlan, an efficient multi-modal trajectory planning method powered by reward-guided fine-tuning. We design a hybrid architecture that combines sparse anchors with an offset refinement module for efficient multi-modal trajectory prediction. Sparse anchors provide coarse trajectory candidates with low latency, which are subsequently refined by the offset module for higher prediction accuracy. To enhance safety without incurring additional inference costs, we adopt a two-stage training paradigm consisting of pretraining and reward-guided fine-tuning. During fine-tuning, we leverage rule-based reward signals and unpaired preference supervision to refine the pretrained policy toward safer trajectory selection. We evaluate EMPlan on the non-reactive NAVSIM benchmark, where it strikes a favorable balance between planning accuracy and efficiency, demonstrating superior performance under real-time constraints.

</details>


### [229] [DODGER: Safety-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Robot Navigation Among Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38873)
*Sanghyuk Park,Kwanwoo Lee,Taekyung Kim,Seohyeon Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DODGER 安全引导强化学习框架：训练时直接执行策略动作以保留探索，利用 CBF 滤波参考与约束违反信号塑造避障策略；在 Dubins 车、全阶人形仿真与真实人形（LiDAR 感知）中实现多动态障碍导航，无需运行时安全滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF 安全滤波常用于安全强化学习，但训练中只执行滤波后的动作会限制策略探索，在安全依赖于机器人与障碍物相对运动的动态场景中尤为致命，因此需要一种既能保留探索又能学习避障的框架。

Method: DODGER：训练 rollout 直接执行策略输出动作（不禁用探索），将 CBF 滤波后的参考动作与约束违反量作为引导/塑形信号来更新策略，而非在运行时施加安全滤波；理论上以 Dubins 车模型进行安全性与约束分析，感知采用 LiDAR。

Result: Dubins 车分析给出安全保证/边界；在全阶人形仿真与真实人形机器人上，利用 LiDAR 感知实现多动态障碍环境中的目标导向导航，且推理/部署时不使用运行时安全滤波器。

Conclusion: 以 CBF 作为训练期引导信号而非执行期滤波器，可在保留探索的同时学到安全避障策略，并在真实人形平台上验证了无需运行时滤波器即可在多动态障碍中导航。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-centered environments must safely navigate among multiple dynamic obstacles to avoid collisions with people and surrounding infrastructure. Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide an effective mechanism for safety filtering, and recent CBF-based reinforcement learning (RL) methods embed such safety information into learned policies. However, executing only safety-filtered actions during training can restrict policy exploration, a limitation that becomes particularly consequential in dynamic scenes where safety depends on relative robot-obstacle motion. We propose DODGER, a safety-guided RL framework that directly executes policy-generated actions to drive training rollouts while using CBF-filtered references and constraint violations to shape the policy toward collision-avoidance behavior. We evaluate DODGER through a Dubins-car safety analysis and demonstrate goal-directed navigation among multiple dynamic obstacles in full-order humanoid simulation and real-world humanoid experiments using LiDAR-based perception, without a runtime safety filter.

</details>


### [230] [Benchmarking and Enhancing Skill-Level Memory for Partially Observable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38886)
*Yansong Shi,Jiange Yang,Xijie Yang,Shaowei Zhang,Yuhan Zhu,Tao Lu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 HIDE 基准与 SEEK 记忆框架，用于在部分可观测条件下评估和提升操作策略的历史依赖能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略的可靠执行常依赖无法从当前观测推断的隐藏任务状态，交互历史因此变得关键，但现有基准缺乏对"操作记忆"的系统评估。

Method: HIDE 包含 15 个任务，覆盖重复计数、历史状态回忆与执行进度追踪，并随机化初始配置与决策点；SEEK 框架组合三种互补记忆机制，用于保留历史证据并跟踪执行状态。

Result: 现有策略在 HIDE 上表现显著不足；加入记忆机制后在仿真与真实世界中任务成功率均有提升。单一机制对部分任务有益但对其他任务可能有害，三种机制组合在评估配置中取得最高平均成功率。

Conclusion: 维持隐藏任务状态的内部表征至关重要，记忆设计需与任务特定的信息需求相匹配。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have enabled manipulation policies to perform increasingly diverse tasks and generalize across environments. However, reliable execution often depends on hidden task states that cannot be determined from current observations alone, making interaction history essential. We introduce $HIDE$, a benchmark for evaluating manipulation memory under partial observability. HIDE comprises 15 tasks covering repetition counting, historical-state recall, and execution-progress tracking, with randomized initial configurations and decision points where similar observations require different actions depending on prior events. We further propose $SEEK$, a framework combining three complementary memory mechanisms to retain historical evidence and track execution state. Evaluations reveal substantial limitations in existing policies on HIDE, while memory augmentation improves task success in both simulation and real-world experiments. Individual mechanisms benefit some tasks but can degrade others; their combination achieves the highest average success rate on HIDE among the evaluated configurations. These findings highlight the importance of maintaining internal representations of hidden task states and matching memory design to task-specific information requirements.

</details>


### [231] [PRICE the Action Chunks: Physical Relational Credit Assignment for Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38890)
*Yangang Zou,Jiajun Lu,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu,Bozhou Zhang,Jiawei Wang,Honglong Tian,Minglei Li,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PRICE，用物理关系图汇聚跨轨迹相同物理状态处的终端结果，估计动作块的成功势能，实现无需额外评估器的细粒度信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结果型强化学习只用终端成功信号后训练 VLA 策略，却把轨迹级优势平均分配给每个动作块，导致失败回合会错误惩罚早期有用动作；现有细粒度反馈方法需要任务特定监督或额外模型训练。

Method: 核心洞察是到达对应物理情境的 rollout 可互为参照，其终端结果提供局部进度证据。PRICE 含两部分：(i) 物理关系图，在当前与历史轨迹的对应块边界处汇聚结果以估计成功势能；(ii) 置信度门控信用分配，用势能变化精炼轨迹级监督。理论分析将 oracle 势能变化与终端成功目标关联，并给出与结果无关证据池的有限样本方向界。

Result: 独立续接测试显示保留的信用与局部进度一致；在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 和真机上相比结果型基线任务成功率更高、学习更快。

Conclusion: 跨轨迹物理关系可在仅有终端结果的具身强化学习中提供动作块信用，无需辅助评估器，为 VLA 后训练提供更细粒度且可验证的监督信号。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning (RL) post-trains vision--language--action policies using terminal success signals, but assigns the same trajectory-level advantage to every action chunk. A failed episode can thus penalize useful early actions as if they caused the failure. Existing approaches seek finer-grained feedback through learned evaluators, adding task-specific supervision or additional model training. We explore, for the first time to our knowledge, whether physical relations across trajectories can provide action-chunk credit in embodied RL from terminal outcomes alone, without an auxiliary evaluator. The key insight is that rollouts reaching corresponding physical situations can serve as references for one another: their terminal outcomes provide evidence for assessing local progress. We introduce Physical Relations for Inferring Credit from Episodes(PRICE), with two components: (i) a physical relational graph that pools current and historical outcomes at corresponding chunk boundaries to estimate success potentials; and (ii) confidence-gated credit assignment that uses changes in these potentials to refine trajectory-level supervision. Our analysis connects oracle potential changes to the terminal-success objective and provides a finite-sample directional bound for outcome-independent evidence pools. Independent continuation tests show that PRICE's retained credits align with local progress, while experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real robots demonstrate improved task success over outcome-based baselines and faster learning.

</details>


### [232] [EmbodiRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Data-Efficient Robot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38905)
*Haoran Lang,Haotao Lu,Shiyu Sang,Haoyang Luo,Guo Chen,Qun Li,Jingyi Yu,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiRSI 提出 real-to-sim-to-real 的智能体式递归自改进系统：从部署场景构建任务级仿真，利用策略执行反馈驱动"协作纠错+自适应数据采集"闭环，在三个桌面环境14个子任务上把仿真自主成功率从50.4%提升到83.5%，仅用400条自适应仿真轨迹+每子任务10条真机轨迹即达83.1%真机成功率，超过每子任务200条真机示教的75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略适配新任务/新环境数据代价高昂，且"下一步该采什么数据"取决于当前策略的能力边界与失败模式，静态数据集与固定示教难以针对性补足短板。作者希望用部署场景定制的仿真作为低成本迭代环境，让策略能力提升与数据获取互相驱动，实现数据高效的递归自改进。

Method: 1) real-to-sim-to-real 流程：由目标部署场景构建任务特定仿真，作为可复用工作区（策略预热、可重复评测、失败诊断、定向数据生成）；2) 以策略执行反馈闭环驱动后续经验获取与策略更新；3) 协作纠错（Collaborative Error Correction）：从策略实际到达状态出发，由智能体辅助生成纠错轨迹；4) 自适应数据采集（Adaptive Data Collection）：将专家示教生成方向对准当前策略的薄弱环节；5) 多轮 RSI 更新后把改进策略迁移回真机。

Result: 三个桌面环境、14个子任务：两轮 RSI 更新后场景均衡仿真自主成功率 50.4%→83.5%；仅使用400条自适应仿真轨迹与每子任务10条真机精调轨迹，达到83.1%场景均衡真机自主成功率，而每子任务200条真机示教的适配方案为75.0%。

Conclusion: 在部署专属仿真中做反馈驱动的递归自改进，能够以显著更低的数据成本把具身策略适配到真实物理环境，仿真—真机数据配比效率优势明显（约1/20真机数据量下仍提升约8个百分点）。

Abstract: Adapting robot manipulation policies to new tasks and environments remains highly data-intensive, while the data needed for further improvement depends on the policy's current capabilities and failure modes. We introduce EmbodiRSI, an agentic system for recursive self-improvement (RSI) in a real-to-sim-to-real setting, where task-specific simulations are constructed from target deployment scenarios and used as low-cost environments for iterative policy improvement before transfer back to the physical world. EmbodiRSI uses policy execution feedback to guide subsequent experience acquisition and policy updates. Two complementary mechanisms close this loop: Collaborative Error Correction generates agent-assisted corrective trajectories from policy-reached states, while Adaptive Data Collection directs expert demonstration generation toward the current policy's weaknesses. The task-specific simulation serves as a reusable workspace for policy warm-up, repeatable evaluation, failure diagnosis, and targeted data generation across successive RSI rounds. Across three tabletop environments and 14 subtasks, EmbodiRSI increases scene-balanced autonomous simulation success from 50.4% to 83.5% over two RSI updates. With 400 adaptive simulated trajectories and only ten real-world refinement trajectories per subtask, EmbodiRSI achieves 83.1% scene-balanced autonomous real-world success, compared with 75.0% for adaptation using 200 real-world demonstrations per subtask. These results demonstrate that feedback-driven recursive improvement in deployment-specific simulations can enable data-efficient adaptation of embodied policies to physical environments.

</details>


### [233] [Local-Minimum Escaper: Programmatic Subgoal Generation for Robust Navigation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38928)
*Yin Gu,Xinming Zhang,Shanze Wang,Siwei Cheng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LME——一个程序化分层框架，通过可解释启发式显式生成并推理子目标，仅用局部观测帮助移动机器人逃出局部极小值，无需训练且可搭配不同局部规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知与部分可观测环境下的无地图导航仍具挑战性，局部极小值会阻碍机器人前进。现有局部导航方法缺乏显式的逃逸机制，而DRL方法只能通过奖励设计与策略优化隐式学习恢复行为，难以保证可解释性与泛化性。

Method: LME为程序化分层框架：仅依赖局部观测，用可解释的启发式准则（兼顾周围障碍几何与候选位置安全性）选取候选子目标，再由局部规划器生成朝子目标的底层运动指令；统一处理有/无局部极小值场景，不依赖特定局部规划器、无需额外训练。

Result: 仿真与真实环境实验显示LME导航性能稳健，可泛化到未见过的挑战场景；生成的子目标可用于引导不同局部规划器，显著提升其逃逸局部极小值的能力；在差速驱动机器人与四足机器人上均成功部署。

Conclusion: 显式子目标生成与推理是解决无地图导航局部极小值问题的有效且可解释的途径，框架通用性强、无需训练、易于与现有规划器集成，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Mapless navigation in unknown and partially observable environments remains challenging for mobile robots, particularly when local minima prevent the robot from making progress toward its goal. Existing local navigation methods often lack an explicit mechanism for escaping such situations, while deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approaches typically learn recovery behaviors implicitly through reward design and policy optimization. In this work, we propose \textbf{LME} (Local-Minimum Escaper), a programmatic hierarchical framework that explicitly generates and reasons subgoals to guide robots out of local-minimum regions. LME operates solely on local observations and selects candidate subgoals using interpretable heuristic criteria that account for both surrounding obstacle geometry and candidate-location safety. A local planner then generates low-level motion commands toward the selected subgoal. This design enables LME to handle environments both with and without local minima within a unified framework, while remaining independent of the underlying local planner and requiring no additional training. Extensive experiments in simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that LME provides robust navigation performance and generalizes to challenging unseen scenarios. Furthermore, the generated subgoals can be used to guide different local planners, substantially improving their ability to escape local minima. Successful deployments on both differential-drive and quadruped robots further demonstrate the practical applicability and generality of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [234] [DrivingBench: Can Vision-Language Models Drive a Toyota Corolla?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38948)
*Aditya Ramabadran,Simon Mahns,Tobias Gessler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DrivingBench：首个让通用视觉语言模型直接驾驶真车（Toyota Corolla，停车场锥桶赛道）的基准，用三个工具读取相机帧并输出转向/速度指令，考验延迟约束下的长时程闭环驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型在数字基准上表现出色，但其驱动真车这一日常人类技能基本未被检验；需要真实世界、闭环、含推理延迟的评测来暴露 VLM 在具身长时程任务上的短板。

Method: 在低俗锥桶赛道用真车搭建基准。模型通过三个工具获取相机帧，直接给出转向与速度命令；车辆在模型推理期间持续运动，新命令覆盖旧命令，因此推理延迟成为任务约束的一部分。在 Codex、Claude Code、Cursor 等厂商原生 harness 中评测 GPT-6 Astra、Claude Fable 5.1、GPT-5.6 Sol、Grok 4.6，每个模型单轮对话最多三次尝试。同时分析动作接口设计原则、工具输出格式与任务表述对"是否愿意驾驶/拒绝执行"的影响，并公开 harness、提示词、赛道地图与含视频和遥测的 trace。

Result: 仅 GPT-6 Astra 完成赛道（第二次尝试成功），其余尝试均未通过赛道的 50%。四个模型中两个在保留上下文的多次尝试间有实质提升。工具输出格式与任务表述共同决定模型是否会真的开动或直接拒绝。

Conclusion: 通用 VLM 在真实闭环驾驶上能力仍很有限，延迟与接口设计是关键变量；该基准与公开 trace 为具身长时程智能体评测提供可复现的真实世界平台。

Abstract: Frontier models excel at many digital benchmarks, yet their ability to drive a real car, an everyday human skill, remains largely untested. We present DrivingBench, to our knowledge the first benchmark where general-purpose vision-language models must drive a real car. Through three tools, the models see camera frames from a Toyota Corolla and directly command its steering and velocity around a parking lot cone course at low speeds. The car may continue moving while the model thinks and new commands replace the currently running one, so inference latency is part of the task, testing the models' abilities to observe, act, monitor, recover, and complete a long-horizon objective under such constraints. We benchmark GPT-6 Astra, Claude Fable 5.1, GPT-5.6 Sol, and Grok 4.6 in vendor-native harnesses (Codex, Claude Code, Cursor) with up to three attempts each in one conversation; Astra is the only model to finish the course, on its second attempt, with no other attempt passing 50% of the course. Two of the four models improved materially across attempts with retained context. We also detail the design principles behind our action interface, and show how the tool output format and the framing of the task combined to determine whether models would drive at all or refuse. We release our harness, prompts, course map, and traces with video and telemetry for reproducibility.

</details>


### [235] [Video2SwimFish: An Automated Pipeline for Reconstructing Controllable Fish Models and Biological Locomotion from Real Fish Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38966)
*Hangong Chen,Linfeng Cheng,Tahsin Zaman Jilan,Ian Fuller,Lee Caesar,Jiaye Wu,Yantian Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Video2SwimFish 提出从真实鱼类多视角视频自动构建可控鱼体资产（度量尺度形变网格 + VLM 驱动的内部关节 + 生物运动流形 BLM 动作空间），并发布 120 条鱼/6 物种的配对数据集与个体游泳策略基准，用于水下具身 AI。实验发现任务成功率与运动保真度并不正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下具身 AI 缺乏与真实个体生物绑定的可控资产生成流程与保真度评估基准；现有仿真鱼通常为手工建模、通用形态，无法体现个体形态与真实运动统计，也难以衡量"像不像这条鱼"。

Method: 给定同步多视角视频：1) 从 VLM 选取的规范帧重建度量尺度形变网格；2) 用 VLM actor-critic 循环生成适配个体形态的内部关节结构；3) 从观测的中线曲率提取生物运动流形 BLM，作为受真实鱼运动约束的低维动作空间；4) 基于 BLM 为每条重建鱼学习个体游泳策略。同时发布数据与基准，含三条评测任务。

Result: 发布 120 条个体鱼（6 物种）的顶视/前视同步配对录制，以及派生可控资产与个体游泳策略；基准覆盖轨迹跟随、无奖励视频行为迁移，以及 BlueROV 水下机器人捕获该资产的案例研究。关键发现：任务成功率最高的方法与运动保真度最高的方法并不一致，两者不一定协同提升。

Conclusion: 个体动物运动的高保真复现仍是开放挑战；该工作将"个体绑定"的保真度指标引入具身运动学习评测，提供了可复用的数据、资产与基准，但缺乏真实水下机器人实机闭环验证。

Abstract: We present Video2SwimFish, an automated pipeline and benchmark for building controllable fish assets from real-fish videos for underwater embodied AI. Given synchronized multi-view videos of an individual fish, the pipeline reconstructs a metrically scaled deformable mesh from a VLM-selected canonical frame, generates internal articulation adapted to that individual's morphology through a VLM actor-critic loop, and extracts a Biological Locomotion Manifold (BLM) from the fish's observed midline curvature. The BLM provides a low-dimensional action space bounded by real-fish motion, enabling an individual swimming policy to be learned for each reconstructed fish. We release two paired datasets: synchronized top- and front-view recordings of 120 individual fish across 6 species, and the controllable assets and individual swimming policies derived from them. Because every asset is tied to the animal it came from, the dataset supports a benchmark that evaluates locomotion learning not only on task success but on fidelity to that individual in trajectory shape, body curvature, and tail-beat frequency, across trajectory following, reward-free swimming behavior transfer from video, and a downstream case study in which a simulated BlueROV underwater robot captures one of the assets. We find that task success and locomotion fidelity do not necessarily improve together: the method achieving the highest task completion is not the method achieving the highest locomotion fidelity, and we identify faithful reproduction of individual animal locomotion as an open challenge for the community. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [236] [SimEX: Simulation-Integrated Robotics AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38982)
*Jiaheng Hu,Roberto Martin-Martin,Peter Stone,Rocky Duan,Zhenyu Jiang,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimEX是一个将仿真实验紧密集成到编码智能体自动研究流程中的框架，两阶段实现仿真到真实机器人的技能获取，无需演示、仅需10分钟真实机器人交互即可完成毛巾折叠、条码扫描、盘子操作等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的编码智能体在数字世界表现优异，但迁移到物理世界面临两大障碍：直接生成方法（如Code as Policies）对机器人和物理环境理解不足；在物理世界中进行迭代试错调优（physical autoresearch）代价高且存在安全隐患。因此需要一种既高效又安全的机制来让编码智能体获得真实机器人操控能力。

Method: 提出SimEX（Simulation-Integrated Robotics AutoResearch）框架，分两阶段：(1) 智能体在仿真中进行开放式"探测-优化"迭代，构建具备鲁棒性和泛化性的机器人工具箱；(2) 智能体联合适配工具箱与仿真器，仅通过少量真实试验完成迁移——每次真实试验用于校正仿真器，校正后的仿真器用于诊断失败并筛选候选修复方案。

Result: 在sim-to-sim设置与真实机器人上进行了大量评估。在具有挑战性的真实操作任务（毛巾折叠、条码扫描、盘子操作）上，SimEX使编码智能体在无任何演示、仅10分钟真实机器人交互的条件下高效获得机器人技能。

Conclusion: 仿真可以成为实现物理智能的关键组件：它不仅是必须高度复刻真实的训练数据来源，更可作为"大致正确"的实验室，供编码智能体在其中发展出作用于机器人所需的知识与流程。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable abilities to autonomously reason about and achieve goals in the digital world. However, bringing this success to the physical world remains challenging. On the one hand, direct generation methods (e.g., Code as Policies) often suffer from the LLMs' insufficient understanding of robots and physical environments. On the other hand, iterative trial-and-error tuning in the physical world (e.g., physical autoresearch) induces significant experimental cost and safety concerns. We introduce SimEX: Simulation-Integrated Robotics AutoResearch, an autoresearch framework that tightly integrates simulated experimentation, enabling coding agents to efficiently acquire physical capabilities for controlling real robots. SimEX operates in two stages. First, the agent conducts open-ended probe-and-optimize iterations in simulation, developing a robot toolbox with robust and generalizable capabilities. Second, the agent adapts the toolbox and the simulator together through only a few physical trials: each trial corrects the simulator, and the corrected simulator is used to diagnose failures and screen candidate repairs. We evaluate SimEX extensively in sim-to-sim settings and on physical robots. On challenging real-world manipulation tasks including towel folding, barcode scanning, and plate manipulation, SimEX enables coding agents to efficiently acquire robot skills without any demonstration and with only 10 minutes of real-robot interaction. These results suggest that simulation can be a critical component in achieving physical intelligence, not only as a source of training data that must closely replicate the real world, but also as a roughly correct laboratory where a coding agent develops the knowledge and procedures needed to act on the robot. More details and robot videos at this https URL

</details>


### [237] [Sparse-WAM: Accelerating World Action Models via Action-Guided Sparse Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38984)
*Xinling Xie,Haodong Wang,Jiazhi Mi,Zhiming Liu,Zicong Hong,Xiaoyi Pang,Qianli Liu,Yangjia Hu,Ying Chen,Zhengyang Yan,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Sparse-WAM 框架，用动作引导的稀疏想象加速 world-action model 推理：基于动作到未来帧注意力在相邻去噪步间空间分布高度重叠的观察，保留动作相关区域与跨帧上下文，并设计 Pilot 引擎降低打分与打包开销，在 LIBERO 与 RoboLab-120 上分别取得约 2.0× 与 1.8× 加速且基本保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 联合预测未来视觉状态与动作以提升机器人控制泛化，但密集未来帧 token 在去噪中被反复处理，推理代价高昂。已有 token 剪枝方法面向视频扩散模型、以视觉保真度为优先，未利用动作相关性来决定联合去噪中应保留哪些未来帧 token。

Method: 观察到相邻去噪步之间动作 token 对未来帧 token 的注意力空间分布存在显著重叠，尽管未来表示持续更新；据此提出 Action-Guided Token Selection，保留帧内动作相关区域并兼顾跨帧上下文；针对朴素实现中打分与 token packing 的额外开销，提出 Pilot 引擎，通过轻量打分与跨步复用 token 选择来降低稀疏推理开销。整体为训练无关（training-free）框架。

Result: 在 LIBERO 搭配 FastWAM-Joint、RoboLab-120 搭配 Cosmos 3 Edge 上，于 NVIDIA RTX 4090 相比密集 eager 推理分别获得约 2.0× 与 1.8× 加速，且大体保持任务性能。

Conclusion: 动作相关性可作为 WAM 联合去噪中未来帧 token 取舍的有效准则，且注意力分布的跨步稳定性使选择可被复用，从而在免训练前提下显著降低 WAM 推理成本；但结论主要基于仿真基准，尚缺真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video models to improve generalization in robot control by jointly predicting future visual states and actions. This capability comes at a substantial inference cost, as dense future-frame tokens are repeatedly processed during denoising. Prior methods address this by token pruning that prioritizes visual fidelity to reduce denoising costs in video diffusion models. However, these methods do not use action relevance to determine which future-frame tokens to retain during joint denoising in WAMs. In this paper, we propose Sparse-WAM, a training-free framework for action-guided sparse imagination that selectively processes future-frame tokens to accelerate WAM inference. We observe substantial overlap in the spatial distribution of attention from action tokens to future-frame tokens (action-to-future attention) between consecutive denoising steps, despite continued updates to the future representations. Motivated by this, we develop Action-Guided Token Selection to retain frame-specific action-relevant regions together with cross-frame context. However, a naive implementation can incur attention-scoring and token-packing overhead that offsets the computational savings from pruning. We therefore introduce Pilot, an efficient engine that reduces sparse inference overhead through lightweight scoring and cross-step reuse of token selections. On LIBERO with FastWAM-Joint and RoboLab-120 with Cosmos 3 Edge, Sparse-WAM achieves inference speedups of approximately $2.0\times$ and $1.8\times$, respectively, over dense eager inference on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, while largely preserving task performance.

</details>


### [238] [Cue the Flow: Steering Flow-Matching Policies for Open-World Delivery Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38989)
*Haoxuan Wang,Griffin Galimi,Junhua Huang,Selina Song,Wayne Wu,Yan Yan,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以预训练流匹配VLA作为底层控制接口，将高层grounding输出当作空间线索，用轻量adapter预测仿射变换来引导动作块，从而提升开放世界物体操作的指令跟随与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界货物交付要求移动机械臂理解自由形式指令并操作新物体。现有双系统方法用高层grounding模型把语言转成视觉prompt，但底层控制器在噪声感知、动态场景和接触丰富交互下依然脆弱。

Method: 把预训练flow-matching VLA当作底层控制接口，利用其反应性与鲁棒性；将grounding输出视为空间线索用于策略引导。引入轻量cue-conditioned adapter：先用对比目标训练得到显著且空间可区分的线索表示，再监督其预测作用于生成动作块的对角仿射变换，使策略引导对齐线索目标。

Result: 在桌面与移动底座设置下，方法在域内和域外物体上都提升了指令跟随与操作成功率，平均任务成功率提升最高接近2倍，推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 证明了预训练VLA已具备强操作先验，只需补充目标空间信息即可在新颖语言-物体映射下引导动作；轻量adapter方案有效且高效。

Abstract: Open-world goods delivery requires mobile manipulators to follow free-form user instructions and manipulate potentially novel objects. Existing dual-system approaches use high-level grounding models to convert language into grounded visual prompts, but their low-level controllers can remain brittle under noisy perception, dynamic scenes, and contact-rich interactions. We instead use a pretrained flow-matching vision-language-action model as the low-level control interface, leveraging its reactivity and robustness to environmental changes while treating the grounding output as a spatial cue for policy steering. Our key insight is that the pretrained VLA already provides a strong manipulation prior, while the spatial cue supplies the missing target information needed to guide actions under novel language--object mappings. Concretely, we introduce a lightweight cue-conditioned adapter. The adapter is first trained with contrastive objectives to produce salient and spatially discriminative cue representations, and is then supervised to predict a diagonal affine transformation over the generated action chunk, aligning policy steering with the cued target. Across tabletop and mobile-base settings, our method improves instruction following and manipulation success on both in-domain and out-of-domain objects, achieving up to near $2\times$ improvement in average task success rate with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [239] [NEXUS: Perceptive Whole-Body Control for Terrain-Adaptive Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39000)
*Xiangyu Miao,Junsong Wu,Jiyuan Shi,Weiji Xie,Jinrui Han,Xingyi Wang,Weinan Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NEXUS感知式全身控制框架，通过可扩展地形自适应算法生成近1000小时平地-非平地配对运动数据，并用教师-学生学习训练感知全身控制器，实现跨地形全身遥操作并完成真机零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身遥操作要求机器人即使在操作者与机器人所处地形不同时也能复现人的行为。这需要机器人感知局部地形并相应调整姿态与接触，而非逐帧复制人的运动。但配对运动数据稀缺（同一行为在平地与不同地形上的成对数据），限制了地形自适应控制的学习监督。

Method: 1) 可扩展的地形自适应算法：无需针对单个动作或单个地形调参，即可高效生成高质量跨地形配对运动；2) 构建近1000小时的配对运动语料；3) 采用教师-学生学习（teacher-student learning）训练感知式全身控制器，融合人体运动指令与机载传感反馈，实现跨地形行为复现。

Result: 实验表明可高效、可扩展地生成高质量运动数据；NEXUS在行为覆盖广度、地形自适应性和跟踪保真度上兼具优势，在所评估基准上优于现有全身控制器；实现零样本真机部署，可在多种未见地形上实时全身遥操作。

Conclusion: NEXUS通过数据生成+教师-学生学习范式，解决了跨地形配对数据稀缺问题，实现了感知式全身遥操作的跨地形泛化与真机零样本部署，验证了方法的泛化能力。

Abstract: Whole-body teleoperation requires a humanoid robot to reproduce a human operator's behavior even when their terrains differ. This demands that the robot perceive local terrain and adapt its posture and contacts accordingly, rather than copy the operator's motion frame by frame. However, paired motion data linking the same behaviors across flat ground and different terrains remain scarce, limiting supervision for learning terrain-adaptive control. To enable whole-body teleoperation across mismatched terrains, we introduce NEXUS, a perceptive whole-body control framework that combines human motion commands with onboard sensory feedback. We first develop a scalable terrain-aware adaptation algorithm that efficiently generates high-quality motion pairs across motions and terrains without per-motion or per-terrain tuning. Using a paired motion corpus totaling nearly 1,000 hours, we train a perceptive whole-body controller through teacher-student learning to reproduce commanded behaviors across terrains. Experiments demonstrate efficient, scalable generation of high-quality motion data and show that NEXUS combines broad behavioral coverage with terrain adaptability and tracking fidelity, outperforming existing whole-body controllers on the evaluated benchmarks.
