To address this limitation, we propose a Sparse Planner (SP) that improves sampling efficiency by learning the conditional relationship between scene context and effective trajectory parameters using a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE). By modeling the structure of high-quality sampling distributions, SP directly generates cost-effective samples in the parameter space, significantly reducing the required sampling density while preserving solution quality. Experimental results show that SP achieves lower trajectory cost than the state-of-the-art FISS+ planner while using only one-eighth of the sampling density. In addition, SP demonstrates improved distance-keeping capability in obstacle-rich scenarios and maintains reduced and more stable runtime characteristics, indicating enhanced computational efficiency and predictable runtime behavior.

</details>


### [274] [ECHO-G: Embodied Co-speech Humanoid mOtion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39575)
*Yizhao Li,Pusen Gao,Ming Wang,Shaojie Shen,Shuo Yang,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ECHO-G：以语音音频+时间戳文本为联合条件的Speech-Grounded Diffusion Transformer，用整流流匹配在机器人空间直接生成全身共语运动，并给出数据集、基准与真机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人全身共语运动需同时协调韵律、语言内容与具身本体约束。既有做法多先生成人运动再重定向，易引入重定向误差与具身不匹配；且音频帧级与文本词级两种粒度差异明显，单一模态条件信息不足。

Method: 提出ECHO-G框架：SGDiT将帧对齐声学特征与词级语言上下文分别编码并保留各自粒度进行联合条件注入；采用rectified flow matching训练，直接在机器人运动空间建模一对多的话语—运动映射。构建BEAT2衍生的音频-文本-机器人数据集及覆盖共语特性、机器人运动质量与运行时效率的基准。

Result: 对比评估显示直接机器人空间生成优于"人运动生成+重定向"流水线；模态消融证明音频-文本联合条件的增益；在实体人形机器人上完成部署；补充的视频评分实验同样偏好联合条件方案。

Conclusion: 联合音频-文本条件与机器人空间直接生成是可行的共语运动路线，数据、训练、推理与评测代码开源。

Abstract: Generating full-body co-speech motion for humanoid robots requires coordinating speech prosody, linguistic content, and embodiment-specific motion. To this end, we present ECHO-G, a framework jointly conditioned on speech audio and timed transcripts. Its Speech-Grounded Diffusion Transformer (SGDiT) combines frame-aligned acoustic features with token-level linguistic context, preserving their distinct granularities. Trained with rectified flow matching, it models one-to-many utterance-motion relationships directly in robot space. To support training and evaluation, we introduce a BEAT2-derived audio-text-robot dataset and a benchmark covering co-speech characteristics, robot-motion quality, and runtime efficiency. Comparative evaluation supports direct robot-space generation over the evaluated human-motion generation and retargeting pipelines, while modality ablations highlight the benefits of joint audio-text conditioning. We further demonstrate deployment on a physical humanoid robot. A complementary video-rating study also favors joint conditioning over the alternatives. The dataset and training, inference, and evaluation code are available through our project page.

</details>


### [275] [Neuro-Symbolic Predicate Learning for Semantic Safe Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39594)
*Zihan Ye,Jiayi Liu,Puze Liu,Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NEUPRO框架，用可微分推理机从人类给定的符号安全规则中学习可复用的语义安全表征，并发布首个可解释机器人安全规约的真实机器人基准数据集REASON。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人进入日常环境后安全性成为核心挑战，但现有方法把安全约束编码为不透明的数学/逻辑公式或稠密代价函数，难以解释、与任务强耦合、且无法说明为何某动作安全或不安全。作者希望将可解释性与可微性结合，把安全约束从"黑盒代价"转为"可复用、可解释的安全推理"。

Method: NEUPRO让使用者以透明的符号规则表达任务相关安全要求，通过可微分推理机使梯度回传到特征提取器，将原始观测映射为安全相关概念；学习到的特征提取器被（软性）锚定在人类可理解的语义上，支持透明的约束评估并跨任务迁移。

Result: 在自建的真实机器人基准数据集REASON上，NEUPRO学习到可跨任务泛化的安全关键特征，缓解了传统黑盒代价公式的可解释性局限，并能对安全违规给出显式解释；同时公开释放了该数据集。

Conclusion: 将符号规则的透明性与可微学习的表征能力结合，可超越不透明代价设计，迈向可复用、可解释的机器人安全推理，并为可解释安全规约提供首个真实机器人基准。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in everyday environments, ensuring their safety has become a central challenge. Existing methods often encode safety requirements as opaque mathematical/logical formulations or dense cost functions. While effective in specific tasks, they remain difficult to interpret, tightly coupled to individual tasks, and offer limited insight into why a robot action is considered safe or unsafe. To address this limitation, we propose ``Neuro-Symbolic Predicate Learning for Semantic Safe Robot Control'' (NEUPRO), which leverages a differentiable reasoner that can learn reusable safety representations from human-specified safety knowledge. NEUPRO allows practitioners to express task-related safety requirements as transparent symbolic rules, while enabling gradients to propagate through these rules to a feature extractor that maps raw observations to safety-relevant concepts. As a result, the learned feature extractor is (softly) grounded in human-understandable semantics, supports transparent constraint evaluation, and is transferable across tasks. By coupling interpretability with differentiability, NEUPRO moves beyond opaque cost design toward reusable safety reasoning. To evaluate NEUPRO's capability, we collect and release REASON, the first real robot benchmark dataset for interpretable robot safety specification. Experiments on REASON show that NEUPRO learns safety-critical features that generalize across tasks, mitigate the interpretability limitations of conventional black-box cost formulations, and provide explicit explanations of safety violation.

</details>


### [276] [MVP-SLAM: Multi-Camera Visual-Inertial Floorplan-Prior SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39596)
*Asier Bikandi-Noya,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Muhammad Shaheer,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVP-SLAM：面向室内施工现场的在线视觉惯性SLAM，用双鱼眼相机检测墙体并与竣工图纸匹配，通过漂移感知策略在线纠正漂移并实现楼层平面内的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内施工现场光照多变、结构重复且纹理稀少，长轨迹下视觉SLAM易漂移；但墙体等结构元素仍可区分，且施工遵循设计阶段的楼层平面图，图纸虽与实建存在差异，仍可提供度量参考用于定位与纠偏。现有方法多离线纠正轨迹，或在线方法依赖深度传感器。作者的动机是仅用相机实现"在线"纠偏与图纸内定位。

Method: MVP-SLAM是双面对鱼眼相机的在线视觉惯性SLAM。核心是将地图中检测到的墙体与楼层平面图匹配，采用漂移感知（drift-aware）策略决定何时/如何匹配；随后通过多阶段集成把每对匹配逐步转化为持久性校正（persistent correction），使轨迹在构建过程中持续被纠正并保持图纸内的定位，无需深度传感器。

Result: 在Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026多楼层施工现场数据上验证：Localization任务22支队伍中排名第2（平均RMSE 0.29 m）；SLAM任务62支队伍中排名第5（平均RMSE 0.24 m）；在这两个任务中，均为"在线运行+集成楼层平面图+在图纸内定位"方法中的最高排名。

Conclusion: 仅凭双鱼眼相机即可在线完成视觉惯性SLAM的漂移纠正与楼层平面图内定位，墙体-图纸匹配加持久性校正的策略在真实多楼层施工场景中有效，性能对标并超越部分依赖深度的方案。

Abstract: Indoor building construction sites are demanding environments for visual SLAM, where variable lighting and repetitive, low-textured structures make the system drift over long trajectories, though structural elements such as walls remain distinguishable despite these conditions. These buildings are constructed according to their as-planned floor plans, available from the design phase, and although the actual as-built site can differ from this design, floor plans still provide a metric reference, both to localize the system in the building and to correct drift. Existing methods often use the floor plan to correct an already-built trajectory offline, and those that instead correct it online typically rely on depth sensors. We instead present MVP-SLAM, an online visual-inertial SLAM on two opposite-facing fisheye cameras that corrects drift from cameras alone by matching walls detected in its map to the floor plan, through a drift-aware policy. A multi-stage integration then turns each matched pair incrementally into a persistent correction, so the trajectory stays corrected and localized within the floor plan as it is built. MVP-SLAM was validated on the multi-floor construction sites of the Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026, ranking 2nd of 22 teams in the Localization task (0.29 m mean RMSE) and 5th of 62 teams in the SLAM task (0.24 m), the top-ranked one in both tasks among those that operate online, integrate the floor plan, and localize within it.

</details>


### [277] [Text-to-3D Policy: Fine-Grained Language-Behavior Alignment for Unseen Specification Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39599)
*Xinhao Yang,Wenhao Wu,Ning Lv,Yanshen Ding,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: T3DP提出细粒度文本-行为对齐框架，通过token级双向对应替代全局嵌入压缩，显著提升3D视觉运动策略对未见行为规格的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有text-to-3D策略难以遵循演示未覆盖的细粒度行为规格（如目标位置、位移量、关节状态）；预训练语言表征与常规全局行为-语言对齐只能捕捉粗粒度任务语义，邻近但需不同行为的规格在表征空间中容易被混淆。

Method: 不将指令与演示各压缩为单个全局嵌入，而是保留其局部结构，建立语言元素与行为片段之间的双向token级对应，将细微语言变化直接grounding到受影响的行为分量，得到规格敏感的语言表征；以此条件化点云3D扩散策略，无需改动底层策略架构。

Result: 在Meta-World、ManiSkill、RoboTwin上，held-out规格平均成功率较全局对齐提升+11.0-14.2点，15个任务族全部获益；真机任务平均成功率从47.5%提升至65.0%（+17.5点）。表征与动作探针分析表明细粒度对齐更好保留规格几何与动作相关变化。

Conclusion: token级细粒度语言-行为对齐可防止相近规格表征坍缩，将局部行为grounding与下游控制性能建立联系，为未见行为规格泛化提供有效方案。

Abstract: 3D visuomotor policies provide a strong foundation for spatially precise manipulation, yet current text-to-3D policies struggle to follow unseen fine-grained behavioral specifications beyond those covered by demonstrations. We study this challenge as unseen specification generalization, where language specifies behaviorally significant variations, such as target position, displacement, or articulated state, that are absent from policy training. We find that pretrained language representations and conventional global behavior-language alignment capture coarse task semantics but often blur nearby specifications that require distinct behaviors. We introduce T3DP, a Text-to-3D Policy framework for fine-grained language-behavior alignment. Rather than compressing each instruction and demonstration into a single global embedding, T3DP preserves their local structures and establishes bidirectional token-level correspondence between linguistic elements and behavioral segments. This directly grounds subtle linguistic variations in the behavior components they affect, preventing closely related specifications from collapsing in the representation space. The resulting specification-sensitive language representation conditions a point-cloud-based 3D diffusion policy, enabling more precise control over unseen behavioral specifications without modifying the underlying policy architecture. Across Meta-World, ManiSkill, and RoboTwin, T3DP improves average held-out-specification success over global language-behavior alignment by +11.0-14.2 points, with gains on all 15 task families; on real-robot tasks, it further raises average success from 47.5% to 65.0% (+17.5 points). Representation and action-probe analyses show that fine-grained alignment better preserves specification geometry and action-relevant variation, linking local behavior grounding to downstream control.

</details>


### [278] [Towards Agile Vision-Based Multi-UAV Flight: Revisiting State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39611)
*Michal Pliska,Matouš Vrba,Ondřej Víta,Martin Jiroušek,Viktor Walter,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出仅用位置测量的视觉估计会引入固定结构延迟，提出融合倾角（推力方向）测量的位姿感知估计器，在三个数据集上将速度/加速度误差降低约40%/57%，并在NMPC闭环编队实验中实现超2g横向机动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷多无人机飞行需要低延迟的邻机运动状态估计。现有视觉方法多为位置-only测量，只能通过位移间接推断速度与加速度，导致高阶状态估计存在固定结构延迟，限制了可达敏捷性。

Method: 提出融合最新视觉检测器提供的倾斜（tilt）测量以获取共面多旋翼推力方向。基准测试4种位置-only与5种位姿感知估计器，其中包括一种新的线性推力约束卡尔曼滤波器（thrust-constraining KF）公式，在2个真实数据集与1个高保真照片级仿真数据集上覆盖3-21 m/s²的敏捷度。

Result: 位姿感知估计在三个数据集上分别将平均速度、加速度估计误差降低40%与57%，所提KF公式优于其他估计器。位置-only滤波器在加速度阶跃响应上存在约300 ms恒定延迟且与敏捷度无关；倾角约束估计器接近由相机帧率决定的物理响应极限，因为它在位移累积前即观测到推力方向变化。在NMPC闭环leader-follower仿真中，位置-only估计无法支撑跟随者稳定悬停，而所提估计器可实现超过2g加速度的横向机动跟踪。

Conclusion: 利用推力方向/倾角这一物理先验可突破位置-only视觉估计的结构性延迟瓶颈，显著提升敏捷多无人机状态估计与闭环编队控制性能。

Abstract: Agile multi-UAV flight requires accurate and low-latency onboard estimation of the kinematic states of neighboring UAVs for collision avoidance, motion coordination, etc. Most vision-based approaches rely on position-only measurements, inferring velocity and acceleration indirectly from displacement. We show that this introduces a fixed structural delay in the estimation of higher-order states, which limits the achievable agility. To address this, we propose to integrate tilt measurements, provided by a state-of-the-art visual detector, which inform about the thrust direction of co-planar multirotor UAVs. We benchmark four position-only and five pose-aware estimators, including a novel formulation of a linear thrust-constraining Kalman filter, on two real-world and one high-fidelity photorealistic simulated dataset over different levels of agility (3-21 m/s^2). In our setup, pose-aware estimation consistently reduces the average velocity and acceleration estimation errors by 40% and 57% across the three datasets with the proposed KF formulation outperforming the other estimators. Position-only filters exhibit a constant ~300 ms delay in acceleration step response independent of agility, whereas the tilt-constrained estimators operate near the physical response limit given by the camera frame-rate by observing the change in thrust direction before the displacement accumulates. In a closed-loop leader-follower simulated experiment with NMPC control, position-only estimation of the leader's state fails to facilitate stable hovering of the follower, while the proposed estimator enables tracking of lateral maneuvers exceeding 2g of acceleration.

</details>


### [279] [TO-mdiSPAs: Topology Optimization of multi-directional Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39618)
*Swagatam Islam Sarkar,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于拓扑优化的系统化方法，设计多/全向弯曲的软气动执行器（SPA），采用三场TO保证制造鲁棒性，用带排液项的Darcy定律刻画气压载荷的设计依赖性，并以多目标变形为目标构建min-max问题用MMA求解，数值仿真验证优化结构可实现多向运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软气动执行器在气压下通过弯曲变形执行任务，多向/全向SPA需多自由度协同，但传统设计依赖经验与试错，缺乏系统性的几何自动设计方法；同时气压载荷与结构变形耦合，使设计问题具有载荷设计依赖性，需专门处理，且需兼顾可制造性。

Method: 提出基于拓扑优化（TO）的多向SPA设计方法：采用blueprint/dilated/eroded三场TO公式提升制造鲁棒性；用含drainage项的Darcy定律模拟气压加载的设计依赖性；以SPA单元的目标输出变形构造min-max优化问题，并用移动渐近线法（MMA）求解。

Result: 优化得到高性能、非常规的几何构型；数值仿真表明优化后的SPA能够实现多样化的多向运动。

Conclusion: 研究表明拓扑优化可系统化地生成多向软气动执行器构型，三场公式与设计相关气压载荷建模是保证可制造性与优化有效性的关键；但验证仅限于数值仿真，缺少样机制作与实验标定，其实际制造精度、疲劳与响应性能仍待验证。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators (SPAs) are highly promising and have been extensively explored within the field of soft robotics. Under pneumatic pressure loads, an SPA undergoes bending deformation to perform specific tasks. A multi- or omni-directional SPA can bend and move in any direction within a 3D space, leveraging its multiple degrees of freedom to realize various mechanical functions. This work presents a systematic methodology using topology optimization (TO) to achieve an optimized design for a multi-directional SPAs. To ensure manufacturing robustness, a three-field TO formulation considering blueprint, dilated, and eroded designs is implemented. Additionally, Darcy's law, incorporating a drainage term, is used to model the design-dependent nature of the pneumatic loading. A min-max optimization problem is formulated based on the target output deformations of the SPA unit and solved using the Method of Moving Asymptotes. The optimization yields a high-performing, unconventional geometric design. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate that the optimized SPA successfully achieves versatile multi-directional movements.

</details>


### [280] [Prediction is Better than Detection: Traffic Congestion Control using Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39637)
*Samira Hayat,Christian Raffelsberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文用多智能体仿真研究无人机车队规模对交通拥堵检测与预测性能的缩放规律：检测率在车队规模约等于监测路口数时饱和；而基于预测（而非已检测）拥堵进行信号自适应控制，可将拥堵时长削减效果翻倍，说明预测精度而非感知覆盖成为瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署移动机器人车队做持续监测时，核心问题是任务性能如何随车队规模缩放，以及当感知驱动下游行动（而非仅观测）时该缩放规律是否依然成立。作者希望为车队配置提供可推广的规则，并厘清闭环保单中"多派机器人"与"提升机载预测"哪个更值得投入。

Method: 构建多智能体仿真：车辆采用Nagel-Schreckenberg元胞自动机动力学，无人机按轮询（round-robin）策略巡逻路口，无人机报告驱动自适应交通信号控制器形成闭环。对车队规模、交通流量、路网规模做参数扫描，评估检测率、检测延迟与预测率。

Result: 当车队规模接近被监测路口数量时性能出现平台期，据此给出持续监测部署的车队配置规则。更关键的是，基于"预测到的"拥堵调整信号相比基于"已检测到的"拥堵，拥堵时长削减幅度约翻倍，表明感知-行动流水线中机载预测的价值可超过增加机器人的价值。

Conclusion: 预测精度而非感知覆盖率成为继续提升性能的紧约束，未来方向应聚焦机载推理能力而非单纯扩大车队规模。结论基于仿真，缺少真实部署或真实交通数据验证。

Abstract: A central question in deploying teams of mobile robots for persistent monitoring is how task performance scales with fleet size, and whether this scaling holds once sensing drives downstream action rather than mere observation. We study this question for a team of drones performing traffic-jam detection and prediction in a simulated road network, whose reports drive an adaptive traffic-signal controller in closed loop. We build a multi-agent simulation, with vehicles following Nagel-Schreckenberg cellular-automaton dynamics and drones patrolling junctions via a round-robin policy, and sweep fleet size, traffic level, and network size to evaluate detection rate, detection delay, and prediction rate. We show how performance plateaus for fleet size approximating the number of junctions being monitored, and offer a general fleet-provisioning rule for persistent-monitoring deployments. More significantly, adapting the signal on a predicted jam, rather than a detected one, roughly doubles the resulting reduction in jam duration, showing that the value of onboard prediction in a sensing-to-action pipeline can exceed the value of adding more robots. Prediction accuracy, not sensing coverage, is now the binding constraint on further improvement, pointing to onboard inference, not fleet size, as the more promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [281] [Making Waves: A Membrane-Coupled Delta Array for Manipulating Objects Below the Actuator Spacing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39652)
*Bailey Dacre,Andrés Faíña,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将8×8三自由度Delta机器人阵列的末端通过可拉伸织物耦合，把64个离散接触点变成连续表面，从而能操作尺寸小于执行器间距的物体，并用局部位移场原语与DCT低阶系数策略实现多物体并行操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式机械臂阵列依赖多个执行器同时支撑物体，中心间距因此构成可操作物体尺寸的硬下界，小物体无法被操作。作者希望突破这一几何限制，使同一平台能覆盖执行器间距两侧的尺度范围。

Method: 用可拉伸织物连接8×8个三自由度Delta机器人末端，形成连续操作表面；把阵列视为该表面上的位移场，研究局部准静态场与循环场作为操作原语，可全局施加也可围绕被跟踪物体局部约束以并行操作多个物体；随后训练策略作用于低阶离散余弦变换（DCT）系数，采用十九个Delta的局部邻域作为动作空间。

Result: 在相同动作维度下，指挥十九个Delta邻域比指挥整个阵列的放置误差减半；策略无需适配即可迁移到硬件，成功率76%；平台在单一表面上可操作15mm–90mm的物体，达到六倍尺度范围，跨越43.3mm的执行器间距。

Conclusion: 通过弹性织物将离散执行器阵列转化为连续操作表面，并结合位移场原语与低阶DCT系数策略，可在同一表面上突破执行器间距下界，实现跨尺度、多物体并行的操作。

Abstract: Distributed manipulator systems manipulate objects through the coordinated motion of many actuators. However, an object must be supported by several actuators at once, so the centre-to-centre actuator spacing imposes a hard lower bound on manipulable object size. We remove this bound by coupling the end-effectors of an 8 x 8 array of three degrees-of-freedom delta robots with a stretchable fabric, turning 64 discrete contacts into a continuous surface capable of manipulating objects smaller than the actuator spacing. Viewing the array as a displacement field over that surface, we investigate local quasi-static and cyclic fields as manipulation primitives. These primitives can be applied globally across the array or locally confined around each tracked object to independently manipulate several objects in parallel. We then train a policy acting on low-order discrete cosine transform coefficients: at equal action dimension, commanding a nineteen-delta neighbourhood halves the placement error of commanding the whole array. The policy transfers to hardware without adaptation at 76% success. The platform manipulates objects from 15mm-90mm, a six-fold range spanning both sides of the actuator spacing, 43.3mm, on a single surface.

</details>


### [282] [From Local Whole-Body VLA Behaviors to Scene-Scale Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39670)
*Weixiang Guo,Rui Jin,Haotian Jin,Xinhang Xu,Ruiyang Liu,Haoran Zhao,Yi Wang,Weiqi Gai,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向关节式空中机械臂(UAM)场景级操作的统一框架：用可重配置合成管线生成任务条件轨迹与多视角观测以训练VLA，用MPAR对齐异步动作块与执行进度，用关系场景图与拓扑引导迁移实现跨场地行为组合；仿真局部技能成功率65%、多站点任务42%，并在实体UAM上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型支持任务条件交互，但扩展到场景级空中操作面临三大瓶颈：整机示教成本高、时延导致动作-状态失配、跨场地行为难以组合。因此需要无需真机示教的合成策略训练与鲁棒的场景级执行方法。

Method: 1) 场景可重配置合成管线：生成运动动力学可行的任务条件轨迹与时间同步多视角观测，供VLA训练，无需物理平台示教；2) MPAR(Measured-Progress-Aligned Realization)：将异步返回的动作块与实测执行进度对齐，并实现为连续、动力学可行的轨迹；3) 关系场景图：把语言目标落地到物体实例与可行交互区域；4) 拓扑引导迁移：跨场地连接局部行为。

Result: 仿真中局部VLA技能在oracle目标与可行交接条件下60次成功39次(65.0%)；在500 ms附加时延、含/不含瞬时指令更新停滞时，MPAR相较标称时间对齐将接管阶段中位误差降低0.212 s；完整系统在仿真中完成50次多站点任务中的21次(42.0%)；并在实体关节式UAM上进一步验证。

Conclusion: 合成示教+进度对齐可支撑场景级空中操作的VLA训练与执行，MPAR有效缓解时延引起的动作-状态错配，场景图与拓扑迁移支持跨场地组合；系统具备仿真到真机的可行性，但整体多站点成功率仍偏低，规模化可靠性待提升。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable task-conditioned interaction, but extending them to scene-scale aerial manipulation remains challenging due to costly whole-body demonstrations, latency-induced action-state misalignment, and cross-site behavior composition. We present a unified framework for synthetic policy training and scene-scale execution on articulated uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs). A scene-reconfigurable pipeline synthesizes task-conditioned, kinodynamically feasible trajectories and synchronized multiview observations for VLA training without physical-platform demonstrations. Measured-progress-aligned realization (MPAR) aligns asynchronously returned action chunks with measured execution progress and realizes them as continuous, dynamically feasible trajectories. A relational Scene Graph grounds language goals to object instances and feasible interaction regions, while topology-guided transfer connects local behaviors across sites. Local VLA skills achieve 39/60 successes (65.0%) in simulation under oracle target and feasible-handoff conditions. Under 500-ms added latency, with and without a transient command-update stall, MPAR reduces median takeover phase error by 0.212 s over nominal-time alignment. The complete system completes 21/50 simulated multi-site missions (42.0%) and is further validated on a physical articulated UAM.

</details>


### [283] [RoboCoach: World Models as Active Coaches for Compositional Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39685)
*Jiajun Liu,Yifan Chen,Yichao Liu,Jiayi Zhang,Ruoqu Chen,Shaoxuan Xie,Guocai Yao,Mengdi Xu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROBOCOACH世界模型引导的"教练"框架，用想象失败驱动示范采集与专家微调，实现长时序机器人操作的自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序操作依赖可复用技能组合，但用端到端示范提升组合泛化代价高；系统需自行决定"教什么"（请求哪些子任务示范）与"在哪监督"（更新哪个专家），现有方法缺乏主动指导机制。

Method: ROBOCOACH采用RIDI（Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve）循环：在共享的动作条件世界模型COACHWORLD中执行可复用技能专家，用进度评判器记录首个未完成的子任务位置；聚合这些记录选择待采集的子任务示范及待更新的专家适配器，从而将想象失败转化为定向监督。

Result: 在两个仿真套件与两个真机平台、22组任务-策略对上，想象成功率与实机成功率相关性rho=0.840。同数据预算与更新调度下优于匹配基线；仅150条额外子任务示范即把Franka成功率由13.3%提升至75.0%，AgileX由40.0%提升至83.8%；受训专家零样本迁移至4个未见组合，平均成功率35.0%，而均匀采样的共享策略基线为0%。

Conclusion: 世界模型可作为主动教练，把想象失败转化为定向监督，用于模块化策略的持续改进，兼顾数据效率与组合泛化。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation reuses skills across many task compositions, but improving these compositions with additional end-to-end demonstrations is costly. A practical self-improving system must decide both what to teach next and where to apply that supervision. We present ROBOCOACH, a world-model-guided coaching framework that uses imagined failures to guide demonstration requests and expert updates. Its Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve (RIDI) loop executes reusable skill experts inside COACHWORLD, our shared action-conditioned world model, and uses a progress judge to record the first subtask that fails to complete. Aggregated records select which subtask demonstrations to acquire and which expert adapters to update. Across two simulation suites and two real-robot platforms, imagined and deployed success correlate over 22 task-policy pairs (rho = 0.840). Controlled comparisons show that our coaching method outperforms matched baselines under matched data budgets and update schedules. With only 150 additional subtask demonstrations, success rises from 13.3% to 75.0% on Franka and from 40.0% to 83.8% on AgileX. The coached experts also transfer to four held-out compositions, achieving an average success of 35.0%, compared with 0% for a shared-policy baseline updated with uniformly acquired demonstrations. Together, these results show that world models can serve as active coaches, turning imagined failures into targeted supervision for modular policy improvement. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [284] [Beyond Policy Alignment: Closing the Planning-Learning Loop for Robot Control with Learned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39751)
*Kowndinya Boyalakuntla,Yuhan Liu,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PL-MPC 在 TD-MPC 闭环规划-学习框架上改进 critic 监督、MPPI 终端值估计与 actor 蒸馏，在 HumanoidBench 与真实 KUKA 螺栓对齐任务上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划器决定学习经验，而学到的 critic 与 actor 反过来评分并提议未来规划，二者构成闭环；已有 policy-constrained 变体只强化了环中的一环。作者希望同时改进 critic 监督与规划器终端值估计，使闭环的两端更一致、更稳健。

Method: PL-MPC：(1) 混合多步 TD 目标，在自举前让 critic 接触更多真实奖励；(2) 分歧感知（disagreement-aware）终端价值估计，在 MPPI 规划中降低不确定 critic 值的影响；(3) 回报加权 actor 蒸馏，强调来自高回报回合、由规划器实际执行的动作。世界模型结构与 MPPI 优化器保持不变。

Result: HumanoidBench 上最大增益出现在 balance-hard（TAR 由 98±18 升至 387±255）与 hurdle（199±13 升至 466±200），但整体基准表现依任务而异，在 DMControl 上保持竞争力。消融显示两个任务中组件交互不同。并在 7-DoF KUKA IIWA14 的 wrench-nut 对齐上实现零样本 sim-to-real，训练物体尺寸及两个未见尺寸的成功率均高于 TD-M(PC)^2。

Conclusion: 通过在 critic 监督与规划终端值估计两侧同步改进，可强化规划-学习闭环，带来基准性能提升并支持零样本真机迁移；贡献偏增量、方差较大，通用性仍需更多任务验证。

Abstract: Planning with learned world models combines online trajectory optimization with learned value and policy functions for high-dimensional control. Because the planner determines the experience used for learning, while the learned critic and actor in turn score and propose future plans, planning and learning form a closed feedback loop. TD-MPC is a prominent instance of this design. Recent policy-constrained variants strengthen one part of the loop by aligning the learned policy with planner behavior. We introduce PL-MPC (Planning-Learning MPC), which additionally modifies critic supervision and planner terminal-value estimation. Hybrid multi-step TD targets expose critic updates to more realized rewards before bootstrapping; disagreement-aware terminal estimates reduce the influence of uncertain critic values during MPPI planning; and return-weighted actor distillation emphasizes planner-executed actions from high-return episodes. The world-model architecture and MPPI optimizer are otherwise unchanged. On HumanoidBench, the largest gains occur on \texttt{balance-hard}, where Total Average Return (TAR) increases from $98\pm18$ to $387\pm255$, and \texttt{hurdle}, from $199\pm13$ to $466\pm200$; performance across the broader benchmark remains task dependent, and PL-MPC remains competitive on DMControl. Controlled ablations show different component interactions across the two tasks. We further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on wrench-nut alignment with a 7-DoF KUKA IIWA14, obtaining higher observed success than TD-M(PC)^2 on the training object size and two unseen sizes. Code and data will be available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [285] [ChunkTrust: Adapting Execution Horizons for Robot Policies with Action-Expert Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39754)
*Fanding Huang,Jingyan Jiang,Shifeng Bao,Mingkang Pu,Shiwei Li,Jing Xu,Shijia Xu,Guanbo Huang,Chenghao Gu,Yuzhi Huang,Chenxin Li,Faisal Nadeem Khan,Huan Yang,Yan Wang,Cheng Chi,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChunkTrust将机器人基础策略的动作块执行长度视为隐变量，用免训练的动作感知水平选择器（AHS）结合块内谱稳定性与块间连续性证据，并用在线Beta后验与可选轻量适配器（QHA）推断执行水平，提升多基准成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作块策略在重规划前应执行多少动作取决于任务阶段，而现有做法将其当作固定超参数，缺乏自适应机制。

Method: AHS免训练：融合块内生成轨迹的谱稳定性与已执行历史到预测动作的块间连续性；用带核遗忘的在线Beta后验跟踪重规划间的水平偏好；QHA为可选轻量查询式适配器，从互补证据学习上下文条件的稠密先验，并与当前证据和回合内Beta记忆融合，基座策略保持冻结。

Result: RoboTwin2.0与RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop上AHS对各基座配置均提升任务平均成功率，π0.5在全部50个RoboTwin2.0任务上+6.80个百分点，Qwen3GR00T在RoboCasa上+9.67个百分点；AHS+QHA在8任务π0.5评测上把增益提高到+9.44个百分点；四个真实家庭任务归一化过程分从50.4%升至57.5%；消融验证两项证据、时序记忆与学习先验的作用。

Conclusion: 执行水平可作为可推断的隐变量，以证据驱动方式自适应选择，多数设置下优于固定水平；免训练方案即有稳健增益，学习先验可进一步提升，且无需微调基座策略。

Abstract: Robot foundation policies predict action chunks, but how many actions to execute before replanning depends on the current task phase. We introduce ChunkTrust, which treats the execution horizon as a latent variable inferred from action-expert evidence rather than a fixed hyperparameter. Its training-free Action-aware Horizon Selector (AHS) combines intra-chunk spectral stability of generation traces with inter-chunk continuity between executed history and predicted actions. An online Beta posterior with kernel forgetting tracks horizon preferences across replans. A lightweight Query-based Horizon Adapter (QHA) optionally learns a context-conditioned dense prior from complementary evidence, fused with current evidence and episode-local Beta memory while the base policy remains frozen. Across RoboTwin2.0 and RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop, AHS improves overall task-averaged success for each evaluated base-policy configuration, including gains of +6.80 percentage points on $\pi_{0.5}$ over all 50 RoboTwin2.0 tasks and +9.67 percentage points on Qwen3GR00T in RoboCasa. AHS+QHA raises the gain over Base to +9.44 percentage points on the eight-task $\pi_{0.5}$ evaluation. On four real-world household tasks, AHS improves the equal-task mean normalized process score from 50.4% to 57.5%. Ablations examine the contributions of both evidence terms, temporal memory, and the learned prior. Project page is this https URL

</details>


### [286] [Experience-Driven Continual Learning of Terrain Traversability for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39755)
*Luca Bricarello,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Alberto Sanchez-Delgado,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用冻结DINOv3的预接触图像描述子，通过证据回归预测五种足地交互指标并带不确定性，结合有界经验回放与验证门控实现持续学习，构建可调权重的保守可通行性地图用于Unitree Go2导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在陌生地形上安全高效导航需要在接触前预判地形-机器人交互结果；仅靠几何与视觉外观无法揭示打滑、足部载荷或能量消耗等交互特性，因此需要利用运动经验学习"接触前图像→交互指标"的映射。

Method: 冻结DINOv3骨干生成预接触描述子，映射到5个按测量可靠性加权的本体感觉指标（平面足滑、平均法向地面反作用力、牵引指数、运输代价、触地加载率）；紧凑证据回归器同时预测指标及偶然/认知不确定性；持续适应采用有界经验回放+验证门控（仅在近期留出数据提升且历史留出退化在容差内时替换模型）；预测与认知不确定性投影到局部多层地图，合成可调权重的保守可通行性评分图；ROS2实现支持仿真与Unitree Go2硬件（分域训练）。

Result: 硬件上三个此前未见地形的序列流实验中，门控回放相比无门控回放将最终锚点NLL退化降低23.1%，同时新地形适应性能相当；预测与不确定性地图成功用于下游导航测试，仿真与实机均完成评估。

Conclusion: 验证了"接触前视觉预测交互结果+带门控的持续学习"这一管线的有效性，能在不牺牲新地形适应的前提下缓解灾难性遗忘，并为四足导航提供不确定性感知的保守可通行性地图。

Abstract: Safe and efficient quadruped navigation over unfamiliar terrain requires predicting terrain-robot interaction before contact: geometry and visual appearance alone cannot reveal how the robot will slip, load its feet, or expend energy. This paper presents a continual learning pipeline that uses locomotion experience to learn these interaction outcomes from pre-contact images and continually updates the predictions as new contacts are observed. Pre-contact descriptors, produced by a DINOv3 backbone model frozen during training, are mapped to five proprioceptive indicators weighted according to measurement reliability: planar foot slip, mean normal ground-reaction force, traction index, cost of transport, and touchdown loading rate. A compact evidential regressor allows us to predict these indicators together with aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty from the visual descriptors. Continual adaptation combines bounded experience replay with a validation gate: candidate models replace the deployed predictor only when they improve performance on recent held-out data while keeping degradation on historical held-out data within a prescribed tolerance. Predictions and epistemic uncertainty are projected into a local multilayer map and combined into a conservative traversability score map whose property weights can be adjusted without retraining. The resulting map is used for downstream navigation tests. The ROS2 implementation supports evaluation on a Unitree Go2 in simulation and on hardware, with models trained separately in each domain. On a sequential hardware stream over three previously unseen terrains, gated replay reduces final anchor negative log-likelihood (NLL) degradation by 23.1% relative to replay without the gate while attaining similar new-terrain adaptation.

</details>


### [287] [DiffWAM: A Fast and Efficient Navigation World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39763)
*Mo Zhu,Yuze Wu,Xijie Huang,Xiao Cui,Fei Gao,Xin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffWAM 直接从冻结视频基础模型的预测表征中回归无人机相机轨迹，用离线几何监督替代部署时的未来视频生成与重建，并配 FastDreamer 异步执行框架实现在线飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频基础模型蕴含丰富的语义与时空先验，但用于具身导航时通常需昂贵的未来视频合成与几何重建（generate-then-reconstruct），推理代价高、难以机载实时运行。作者希望验证：未来视觉预测中隐含的运动信息能否直接从冻结模型的预测表征中解耦出来，从而绕开视频解码与多帧重建。

Method: 提出 DiffWAM 几何条件化的导航 world-action model，将多层次预测特征直接映射为连续相机轨迹：Grid-Motion 模块保持时空运动关联，Latent2Pose 用首帧几何将隐运动锚定为度量有意义的 3D 运动；完整视频 rollout 与几何重建仅用于离线监督。另提出 FastDreamer，将预测与几何计算与飞行过程重叠，并进行时间戳感知的异步轨迹交接，实现连续 UAV 执行。还有机载 DiffWAM-Flash 实现。

Result: 在 1000 样本 DiffWAM-1000 基准上轨迹 RMSE 0.3492 m、终点成功率 74.40%；真实世界实验完成受限穿越、环绕、S 形飞行与多阶段导航等复杂行为；DiffWAM-Flash 在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor 上模型管线延迟 1.08 s。

Conclusion: 预测式视频表征可被高效地锚定（grounding）为连续 3D 运动，为 UAV 导航提供了一条无需未来视频生成与多帧重建的直接替代路径，兼顾精度与机载实时性。

Abstract: Pretrained video foundation models encode rich semantic and spatiotemporal priors for embodied navigation, yet converting these priors into UAV motion typically requires expensive future-video synthesis and geometric reconstruction. We investigate whether the motion implicit in future visual prediction can instead be recovered directly from the predictive representations of a frozen video model. To this end, we present DiffWAM, a geometry-conditioned navigation world-action model that directly transforms multi-level predictive features into continuous camera trajectories. Its Grid-Motion module preserves spatial-temporal motion associations, while Latent2Pose grounds them with first-frame geometry to recover metrically meaningful 3D motion. Complete video rollouts and geometric reconstruction are required only for offline supervision, eliminating future-video decoding and multi-frame reconstruction during deployment. We further introduce FastDreamer, which overlaps predictive and geometric computation with ongoing flight and performs timestamp-aware asynchronous trajectory handoff for continuous UAV execution. DiffWAM achieves a trajectory RMSE of 0.3492 m and an endpoint success rate of 74.40% on the 1,000-sample DiffWAM-1000 benchmark, while representative real-world experiments demonstrate complex behaviors including constrained traversal, orbiting, S-shaped flight, and multi-stage navigation. An onboard DiffWAM-Flash implementation further reaches 1.08 s model-pipeline latency on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor. These results demonstrate that predictive video representations can be efficiently grounded into continuous 3D motion, providing a direct alternative to generate-then-reconstruct navigation pipelines. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [288] [Tool-Policy Co-Design for Powder Weighing in Laboratory Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39797)
*Nikola Radulov,Xin Yang,Kevin S. Luck,Gabriella Pizzuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出工具-策略协同设计框架，用双层优化同时优化粉末分装工具几何形态与控制策略，机器人称量误差较标准工具降低45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室自动化中自主粉末称量受异质材料非线性动力学困扰；现有机器人化学家沿用为人类手部灵巧性设计的固定几何工具，其固定形态决定了控制策略必须去调节的动力学，限制了性能上限。

Method: 将问题建模为双层优化：外层用贝叶斯优化与Hyperband搜索工具设计参数（深度、宽度、边缘尖刺拓扑）；内层为每个候选形态优化控制策略。引入几何相似度度量，用结构相似设计的缓存策略热启动训练。

Result: 在含七种不同物理动力学材料的机器人粉末称量任务、基于流动性的机器人-材料仿真框架中评估；相同算力下多探索28%的构型。真实世界称量误差相对标准工具降低45%，且在未见过的材料上同样有效。

Conclusion: 该方法能同时使控制策略与物理工具适配目标材料动力学，为实验室自动化带来材料操作新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous powder weighing is one of many bottlenecks in laboratory automation due to the complex, non-linear dynamics of heterogeneous materials. Robot chemists performing this task utilise standard tools shaped for the dexterity of human hands, whose fixed geometry sets the dynamics that the control policy needs to regulate. This work introduces a tool-policy co-design framework that concurrently optimises the morphology of a dispensing tool and its control policy for use by robots in chemistry laboratories, formulated as a bi-level optimisation that minimises dispensing error over a target distribution of powder flowabilities. The outer loop varies tool-design parameters such as tool depth, width and rim spike topology using Bayesian optimisation and hyperband, while an inner loop optimises a control policy for each candidate morphology. We also introduce a geometric similarity metric that warm-starts policy training from cached policies of structurally similar designs, exploring 28% more configurations under the same compute budget. The proposed framework is evaluated on a robotic powder weighing task across seven materials with distinct physical dynamics in a flowability-informed robot-material simulation framework. Experimental results demonstrate that our co-designed tool morphology reduces real-world weighing errors by 45% relative to a standard tool, including on previously unseen materials. These results demonstrate our method can adapt both the control policy and the physical tool to the dynamics of the target material, bringing a new paradigm for material manipulation to the field of laboratory automation.

</details>


### [289] [Learning from Runtime Feedback through Failure-Bank Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39820)
*Mingyue Cui,Zheyuan Liu,Yihan Zhu,Zheyuan Zhang,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FailBank是一个四阶段自演化框架，将CBF运行时安全反馈转化为持久的策略改进，在VLA-Arena上同时提升任务成功率并降低累积代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型泛化能力强，但在复杂环境中需在任务成功与避免意外接触之间权衡。运行时shield虽能纠正单步动作，却不改变底层策略，导致策略-shield反复冲突、任务停滞；作者希望把运行时反馈从临时动作约束升级为持久策略监督。

Method: 四阶段自演化框架：采集阶段以固定CBF安全模块作"仅观察"教师，在策略仍掌控执行的情况下生成反事实修正；随后进行结果感知的准入（outcome-aware admission），把有用的修正建议转为纠正目标，同时保留未被纠正的成功动作作为"静默锚点"；最后以受保护的LoRA方式做策略更新，实现策略与shield的持续对齐。

Result: 在VLA-Arena基准、两个难度等级与两个VLA主干上评估：相比基座策略，任务成功率提升8.5与6.9个百分点，策略引发的累积代价降低35.6%与23.8%；相比运行时shielding，成功率提升25.4与9.5个百分点，同时保持相近的累积代价。

Conclusion: 运行时反馈可作为持久策略监督，而非仅为临时动作约束；通过反事实修正+结果感知准入+LoRA更新的闭环，可同时改善成功率与安全性代价的联合工作点。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize broadly across robotic manipulation tasks, but complex environments require balancing task success with unintended contact. Runtime shields can correct individual actions, but they leave the underlying policy unchanged, so repeated disagreements may create a persistent policy-shield mismatch that blocks task progress. To address this challenge, we introduce FailBank, a four-stage self-evolving framework that converts runtime feedback into persistent policy improvement. During collection, a fixed CBF-based safety module serves as an observe-only teacher, producing counterfactual corrections while the policy remains in control. Outcome-aware admission then converts useful proposals into corrective targets and retains successful uncorrected actions as quiet anchors for guarded LoRA updates. We evaluate FailBank on the VLA-Arena benchmark across two difficulty levels and two VLA backbones. Compared with the base policies, FailBank improves the joint success-cost operating point. Across the two backbones, FailBank improves task success rate by 8.5 and 6.9 percentage points, while reducing policy-induced cumulative cost by 35.6\% and 23.8\%, respectively. Compared with runtime shielding, FailBank raises task success rate by 25.4 and 9.5 percentage points, while maintaining comparable policy-induced cumulative cost. These results show that runtime feedback can serve as persistent policy supervision rather than only as a temporary action constraint.

</details>


### [290] [Toward Real-Time VLAs: Stage-Aware Two-Step Flow Denoising and System-Level Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39822)
*Di Wu,Rongtian Shen,Ping Liu,Yan Shen,Zhenhan Yin,Shun Zuo,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Jianglin Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作量化了VLA模型的端到端推理-执行时序鸿沟，提出两阶段非均匀去噪（步数10→2，推理61.6ms→22.0ms）以及分布式实时VLA框架，并在长时程衣物折叠任务上对比六种实时执行方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型低频率推理与机器人高频率执行之间存在时序不匹配，而现有研究多集中于模型能力本身，缺乏对推理延迟与机器人执行链延迟的联合刻画与协同优化。

Method: 对模型推理与机器人执行链做端到端延迟测量与归因；分析Flow Matching速度场的阶段性异质性（早期幅值与方向稳定、末期方向修正增强），据此设计两阶段非均匀去噪；构建支持独立推理/动作发布/控制频率、模块化观测采集与动作溯源日志的分布式实时VLA框架；以π0.5为基线在长时程衣物折叠任务上评测六种实时执行方法。

Result: 去噪步数由10降至2，模型推理时间从61.557 ms降至21.956 ms；Legato在训练类方法中整体最优，Temporal Smoothing在免训练方法中最优，二者在任务成功率、完成时间、动作连续性、加速度平滑性上表现突出；两步去噪结合代表性执行方法可大幅降低推理成本，仅带来小幅任务性能下降。

Conclusion: 推理效率与机器人系统时序应联合优化，非均匀去噪与实时执行策略的结合可在实时性约束下保持可用性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models face a timing gap between low-rate inference and high-rate robot execution. We characterize this gap through end-to-end latency measurements of model inference and the robot execution chain. Repeated Flow Matching denoising contributes substantially to inference cost, while robot-side delays mainly arise from perception acquisition, communication scheduling, and physical response. Analysis of the velocity field shows relatively stable magnitude and direction in early integration, followed by stronger directional correction near the terminal steps. Based on this stage heterogeneity, we propose two-stage non-uniform denoising, reducing the number of steps from 10 to 2 and model-inference time from 61.557 ms to 21.956 ms. We also develop a distributed real-time VLA framework with independent inference, action-publication, and robot-control rates, modular observation acquisition, and action-provenance logging. Using {\pi}0.5 as the baseline, we evaluate six real-time execution methods on a long-horizon physical garment-folding task. Legato performs best overall among training-based methods, while Temporal Smoothing leads among training-free methods; both perform strongly in task success, completion time, action continuity, and acceleration smoothness. Combining two-step denoising with representative execution methods substantially reduces inference cost with a small reduction in task performance. These results motivate joint optimization of model-inference efficiency and robot-system timing.

</details>


### [291] [RL-Guided PAC-NMPC for Probabilistically-Safe Perception-Based Navigation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39854)
*Adam Polevoy,Dillon Capalongo,Katherine Tang,Mark Gonzales,Marin Kobilarov,Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将随机非线性模型预测控制（SNMPC）与强化学习结合的方法：先用RL训练概率型actor-critic与传感器预测模型，再在PAC-NMPC采样框架中以硬约束实现碰撞概率与价值函数改进的有限时间统计保证，从而在满足概率安全约束下逼近RL的长时域性能。仿真验证其可提升感知型RL导航安全性并扩展至高维系统，硬件上实现了固定翼无人机在未知环境中的视觉导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知型RL导航策略在未知环境中缺乏安全性保证，而纯MPC方法又难以达到RL的长时域性能。作者希望融合两者：用SNMPC提供概率安全的硬约束与统计保证，同时借RL的概率模型与价值函数保持长时域任务表现。

Method: 1) 用RL训练概率型actor-critic策略与传感器预测模型（输出概率分布而非确定性预测）；2) 将这些概率模型嵌入基于采样的SNMPC框架PAC-NMPC；3) 利用硬约束在有限时间内对碰撞概率和价值函数改进施加统计保证；4) 通过保证有限时域SNMPC策略在期望意义下降低价值函数，衔接长时域RL性能。

Result: 仿真表明该方法能提升感知型RL导航策略的安全性，并可扩展至高维系统（大传感器输入空间与复杂非线性动力学）。硬件实验中，敏捷固定翼飞行器在未知环境下进行视觉导航取得性能提升。

Conclusion: SNMPC与RL的概率化结合可在保持概率安全约束的同时逼近RL的长时域性能，为感知型导航提供兼顾安全性与任务表现的可行路径，并在真实飞行平台上得到验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an approach for combining stochastic nonlinear model predictive control (SNMPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) to enable probabilistically-safe perception-based navigation in unknown environments. Our method first uses RL to train probabilistic actor-critic and sensor prediction models. We then leverage these probabilistic models in a sampling-based SNMPC framework known as Probably Approximately Correct (PAC)-NMPC, which uses hard constraints to enforce finite-time statistical guarantees on the probability of collision and value function improvement. By ensuring that our finite-horizon SNMPC policies decrease the value function in expectation, we can approach the long-horizon performance of the RL approach while satisfying probabilistic safety constraints. Through simulation experiments, we show that our approach can improve the safety of perception-based RL navigation policies and scale to high dimensional systems with large sensor input spaces and complex nonlinear dynamics. We also demonstrate our approach through hardware experiments, showing improved performance for vision-based navigation with an agile fixed-wing aerial vehicle in unknown environments.

</details>


### [292] [Magic-W0: A Structured World-Action Foundation Model for Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39870)
*Xuhua Chen,Zhenhan Yin,Yuan Zhang,Lingfeng Zhang,He Zheng,Tong Mu,Shun Zuo,Dian Zhou,Di Wu,Xuan Zhou,Shaojie Wan,Rongtian Shen,Qiulong Xu,Yiduo Li,Yinglong Wang,Yanqian Wang,Kun Wang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Magic-W0 是一个世界-动作基础模型,通过"结构化世界转移"(当前状态+转移+未来状态)联合建模物理状态演化与连续动作,并提出层对齐的世界-动作交互架构耦合预测与控制。在 RoboDojo-Sim 上取得最高平均 Score 27.10,并在真实机器人任务上微调后展现良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型(WAM)在增强机器人策略时,主要依赖未来观测重建或通用隐变量预测,缺乏与动作生成紧密耦合的结构化、面向控制的世界表征。作者希望构建一种结构化、控制导向的世界表征,使预测与控制相互促进。

Method: 提出 Magic-W0 世界-动作基础模型,将交互建模为"结构化世界转移"(Structured World Transition),包括:当前状态(视觉-语言上下文+当前3D几何)、转移(由3D运动表示动作引发的三维变化)、未来状态(未来语义描述任务相关结果)。提出层对齐的世界-动作交互架构:演化的动作假设条件化世界转移预测,预测的世界表征反过来持续指导动作生成。在大规模第一人称人类操作、UMI、真实机器人及仿真数据上预训练,使用预训练视觉模型对几何、3D运动、未来语义进行隐监督。

Result: 推理时干预实验表明结构化世界表征对候选动作变化有系统性响应,且动作相关信息通过共享3D表征传播至未来语义预测。在 RoboDojo-Sim 上平均 Score 为 27.10,为对比的 WAM 中最高;在多个真实机器人任务上,用有限下游数据微调后仍表现强劲,支持泛化与快速适应。

Conclusion: Magic-W0 通过结构化的世界-动作耦合表征,实现了预测与控制的双向促进,提升了世界-动作基础模型在仿真与真实机器人任务上的表现与泛化能力。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) augment robot policies with action-conditioned environment dynamics, yet existing approaches largely rely on future observation reconstruction or generic latent prediction and lack structured, control-oriented world representations tightly coupled with action generation. We introduce Magic-W0, a world-action foundation model that jointly models structured physical state evolution and continuous actions. Magic-W0 represents interaction as a Structured World Transition consisting of Current State, Transition, and Future State. Current State combines vision-language context with Current 3D Geometry; Transition is represented by 3D Motion capturing action-induced three-dimensional changes; and Future State is represented by Future Semantics describing task-relevant outcomes. To couple prediction and control, we propose a layer-aligned world-action interaction architecture in which evolving action hypotheses condition world-transition prediction, while predicted world representations continuously inform action generation. Magic-W0 is pre-trained on large-scale egocentric human manipulation, UMI, real-robot, and simulation data, with latent supervision for geometry, 3D motion, and future semantics from pre-trained visual models. Inference-time interventions show that structured world representations respond systematically to changes in candidate actions and that action-related information propagates through shared 3D representations into future semantic predictions. On RoboDojo-Sim, Magic-W0 achieves an average Score of 27.10, the highest among the compared WAMs. Across multiple real-robot tasks, it also demonstrates strong downstream performance after fine-tuning with limited downstream data, supporting generalization and rapid adaptation.

</details>


### [293] [SplineWAM: Adaptive Action Horizons for World Action Models via B-Spline Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39873)
*Jun Guo,Xiaoshen Han,Qiwei Li,Nan Sun,Peiyan Li,Heyun Wang,Hang Lai,Weinan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SplineWAM将动作轨迹自适应压缩为固定规模的三次B样条参数窗口（节点时间按运动特性拟合），使单一参数预算可解码出不同时间分辨率与持续时长的动作块，执行跨度与下次策略调用间隔由预测自身决定；并提出JP-RTC实现异步部署下的块连续性。LIBERO-Plus与RoboCasa成功率提升8.2/4.4点，策略调用减少22%/26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World action models (WAM) 每次推理输出定长动作块，计算预算在时间上均匀分配，无法在自由空间运动时执行更久、也无法在接触密集操作上投入更多推理，限制了云端服务吞吐量。

Method: 1) 用固定尺寸的三次B样条参数窗口自适应压缩动作轨迹，节点时间按运动特征拟合，实现可变时间分辨率与持续时间；2) 将视频监督对齐到示范的拟合节点时间而非均匀网格，使监督帧集中在轨迹复杂处；3) 提出Jacobian-Pullback Real-Time Chunking (JP-RTC)，将块连续性约束施加于解码后的原始动作而非样条参数，并通过解码器反传校正参数以保持已承诺前缀一致。

Result: LIBERO-Plus与RoboCasa上成功率相对动作分块WAM分别提升8.2与4.4个百分点，每回合策略调用减少22%与26%；三个双臂真机异步执行任务中领先或持平基线，每次调用解码的执行动作量提升1.2–1.6倍。

Conclusion: 通过样条参数化动作表示与视频监督重对齐，实现时间自适应的动作分块与调用间隔预测，配合JP-RTC支持异步部署，在仿真与真机上同时提升吞吐与成功率，为WAM的云服务部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are large embodied policies that jointly predict future video and the actions to execute, emitting a fixed-length action chunk per inference call. Such a policy allocates its computational budget uniformly in time, unable to execute for longer over free-space motion or to spend more inference on contact-rich manipulation, which limits the throughput a WAM can reach when served in the cloud. We present SplineWAM, which adaptively compresses the action trajectory into a fixed-size window of cubic B-spline parameters, fitting the knot times to the characteristics of the motion. One parameter budget then decodes into chunks of varying temporal resolution and duration, and both the executed span and the interval until the next policy call follow from the prediction itself. Aligning the video supervision to the fitted knot times of the demonstration rather than to a uniform grid concentrates the supervised frames where the action trajectory is complex. For asynchronous deployment we introduce Jacobian-Pullback Real-Time Chunking (JP-RTC), which imposes chunk continuity on the decoded raw actions the robot executes rather than on the spline parameters, and corrects the parameters through the decoder so that the executed prefix agrees with the actions already committed. On LIBERO-Plus and RoboCasa, SplineWAM improves success rate over an action chunking WAM by $8.2$ and $4.4$ points while cutting policy calls per episode by 22% and 26%. On three bimanual real-robot tasks under asynchronous execution, it leads or matches the baseline while decoding 1.2 to 1.6 times as much executed motion per call.

</details>


### [294] [Active Mapping of Underwater Litter Using Camera-Sonar Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39898)
*David Rete,Patrick Boros,Lucian Busoniu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种主动建图框架，将前视声呐与相机融合进共享贝叶斯占据栅格图，逐步求解最优视角优化问题，在水下仿真中比割草机式覆盖路径更快发现目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋垃圾威胁水下生态，需要在广域内高效定位碎片，但现有调查方法依赖预设路径，且只用单一模态：相机在低能见度下失效，声呐噪声大、分辨率低，缺乏多模态主动感知方案。

Method: 构建共享贝叶斯占据栅格图，融合前视声呐与相机观测；在每个决策步求解优化问题以选取下一最佳视角；候选视角用两项效用函数评分，权衡基于体素熵的不确定区域探索与可能目标的开发；每种传感器的检测概率与虚警概率按距离和方位查表建模，并由数据标定。

Result: 在逼真的水下仿真器中评估，主动建图比割草机覆盖模式更快发现目标，且双传感器融合优于单独使用相机或声呐的任一方案。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的双模态主动建图可提升水下碎片搜寻效率与鲁棒性，验证了探索-开发效用设计和传感器概率表建模的有效性，但验证仅限仿真，缺少真实水下平台实验。

Abstract: Marine litter is a growing threat to the underwater ecosystem, driving demand for autonomous survey methods that can locate debris efficiently over large areas. Existing survey methods typically follow predefined paths or operate with a single sensing modality, typically a camera (with image quality suffering in poor-visibility conditions) or sonar (usually noisy and low-resolution). We present an active mapping framework in which a forward-looking sonar and a camera both feed into a shared Bayesian occupancy map, and an optimization problem is solved at each step to decide on the next best view. Candidate viewpoints are scored by a two-term utility that balances exploration of uncertain regions via voxel entropy against exploitation of likely objects. Each sensor is characterized by range- and bearing-dependent detection and false-alarm probability tables determined from data. We evaluate the approach in a realistic underwater simulator, demonstrating that active mapping finds objects faster than a lawnmower coverage pattern, and that the dual-sensor approach works better than using either of the individual sensors.

</details>


### [295] [Magnetic based In-situ Self 3D Pose Estimation for a Modular Soft Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot via IMU-Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39950)
*Zheng Cao,Guo Ning Sue,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Junzhe Hu,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种嵌入式位姿感知框架，融合IMU与主动磁场参考实现连续体机器人无外部视觉的构型估计，16.7 Hz实时闭环控制验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人固有柔顺性适合柔顺操作，但连续可变形结构使构型估计困难，尤其在外部视觉系统不可用或被遮挡时缺乏可靠反馈手段。

Method: 在机器人本体嵌入IMU与主动磁场发射/传感单元，将IMU角速度/角度测量与磁场提供的全局方位参考进行融合，抑制姿态估计的累积漂移，形成分布式磁-惯位姿感知方案，更新率16.7 Hz，并接入闭环控制器。

Result: 实验通过闭环控制验证：利用估计构型维持末端执行器在目标位置并与物体交互，姿态累积误差得到抑制，感知更新率满足实时反馈需求。

Conclusion: 分布式磁-惯融合感知可为连续体机器人提供无外部视觉的实时构型估计，支撑闭环柔顺操作，具备进一步推广潜力。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for gentle manipulation because of their inherent compliance and ability to adapt to complex environments. However, their continuously deformable structure makes accurate configuration estimation challenging, particularly when external vision systems are unavailable or obstructed. In this work, we present an embedded pose sensing framework that combines inertial measurement units (IMUs) and active magnetic fields to estimate the robot configuration without relying on external cameras. The angular measurements from the IMU and magnetic-field references are fused to improve local orientation estimation and reduce accumulated orientation error during operation. This pose sensing scheme achieves an update rate of 16.7~Hz, allowing real-time feedback. The proposed system is experimentally validated through closed-loop control, where the estimated robot configuration is used to maintain the end-effector at a desired position while interacting with an object. These results demonstrate the potential of distributed magnetic--inertial sensing for real-time pose estimation and closed-loop control of continuum robots.

</details>


### [296] [TACTIC: Temporal and Context-Aware LLM Tactical Planning for Roadside LiDAR Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39969)
*Yiming Gao,Shaocheng Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACTIC 提出一种场景感知的路侧 LiDAR 攻击框架，用多模态大模型（MLLM）融合度量级车辆状态与路侧图像，构建语义场景图并据此选择与配置攻击图元，实现状态自适应的战术级攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理 LiDAR 攻击的现有评测多依赖固定图元与人工选参，忽略了攻击效果对周围交通态势的强依赖，缺乏场景自适应能力。

Method: 灰盒威胁模型：仅使用攻击方自建的路侧感知栈，不接触受害 LiDAR 原生点云与内部处理。局部感知提供度量级车辆状态，MLLM 结合路侧图像推断关系型交通语境并构建语义场景图；据此选择并配置两种互补图元——push-away（偏移前车感知距离）与 phantom-obstacle braking（注入障碍触发紧急制动）。利用实测交通状态与经验标定约束将战术限制在物理可行区间；针对 MLLM 延迟采用异步重叠推理与执行，高频局部感知检测场景变化并触发重规划。

Result: 280 次随机 CARLA 试验中，完整策略碰撞率达 100%，固定规则 35%、随机选择 60%、受限 LLM（默认参数的模式选择）75%。物理+图像联合输入成功率 100%，仅物理量 65%，仅图像 75%；异步 Δ 刷新将场景突变响应时间从 7.4 s 降至 2.0 s。

Conclusion: 场景依赖的战术级规划能暴露固定攻击策略可能遗漏的上下文敏感 LiDAR 失效模式，表明攻击参数与交通语境强耦合，需引入语义推理与状态自适应机制。

Abstract: Physical LiDAR attacks are often evaluated using fixed primitives and manually selected parameters, despite their strong dependence on surrounding traffic. We present TACTIC, a scene-aware framework that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to coordinate state-adaptive roadside LiDAR attacks. Under a gray-box threat model, TACTIC relies only on an attacker-operated roadside perception stack, without accessing the victim LiDAR's native point clouds or internal processing. Local perception provides metric vehicle states, while the MLLM combines these measurements with roadside imagery to infer relational traffic context and construct a semantic scene graph. Based on this representation, TACTIC selects and configures two complementary primitives: \emph{push-away}, which shifts the perceived range of a lead vehicle, and \emph{phantom-obstacle braking}, which triggers emergency braking through obstacle injection. Measured traffic states and empirically calibrated constraints ground the generated tactics in physically feasible operating regions. To accommodate MLLM latency, TACTIC overlaps reasoning and execution asynchronously while high-rate local perception detects scene changes and triggers replanning. Across 280 randomized CARLA trials, the full policy achieves a 100% collision rate, compared with 35% for a fixed rule, 60% for random selection, and 75% for a restricted LLM using mode selection with default parameters. Joint physical-and-image input achieves 100% success, versus 65% with physical measurements alone and 75% with imagery alone, while asynchronous $\Delta$ refresh reduces scene-mutation response from 7.4 s to 2.0 s. These results show that scene-dependent tactical planning can expose context-sensitive LiDAR failure modes that fixed attack policies may miss.

</details>


### [297] [PhasePlan: Ordered Future-Phase Planning for Robot Brain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39970)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Yafei Zhang,Wensheng Li,Qing Zhan,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ordered future-phase planning 方法，让 robot brain 模型在未来每个动作位预测任务阶段并以该规划表征条件化动作生成，保留阶段-动作时间对齐，在传送带操作上相对 π0.5 降低约 22.5% 离线联合动作误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数 robot brain 模型预测固定长度 action chunk，chunk 可能跨越多个任务阶段，导致阶段转换被掩盖、偏向高频动作模式，在动态环境中损害动作时序。

Method: 从当前多模态观测出发，预测未来每个动作位置对应的任务阶段，得到 ordered phase planning 表征并用于条件化对应动作；训练分两阶段，先学 planner，再在 action-model 适配时冻结 planner 以保持阶段表征稳定；实例化于预训练 π0.5 与 AcrossWAM1.0。

Result: 在传送带操作任务上，相对原始 π0.5 模型离线 joint-action error 降低约 22.5%，并改善阶段转换建模与跨阶段动作预测。

Conclusion: ordered future-phase planning 对连续动作生成有价值，可提升阶段转换处的时序对齐与动作质量。

Abstract: Robot brain models integrate vision, language, and robot state to generate actions for complex manipulation tasks. Most predict fixed-length action chunks that may span multiple task phases. This can obscure phase transitions and favor frequent action patterns, compromising action timing in dynamic environments. We propose \method, an ordered future-phase planning method for robot brain models. From current multimodal observations, it predicts the task phase at each future action position. The resulting planning representations condition the corresponding actions, preserving temporal alignment between task progress and action generation. Training first learns the planner, then freezes it during action-model adaptation to maintain stable phase representations. We instantiate \method on pretrained $\pi_{0.5}$ and AcrossWAM1.0 robot brain models. Detailed quantitative evaluation uses the $\pi_{0.5}$ implementation. On conveyor-belt manipulation, \method reduces offline joint-action error by approximately 22.5\% relative to the original $\pi_{0.5}$ model. It also improves phase-transition modeling and cross-phase action prediction. These results demonstrate the value of ordered future-phase planning for continuous action generation.

</details>


### [298] [When Instructions Retrieve Trajectories: Diagnosing and Mitigating Generalization Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39971)
*Hung-Jen Chen,Yu-Hsun Hou,Yan-Hong Chen,Yan-Fu Chen,Binghua Cai,Min Sun,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出"指令-动作绑定"失败模式：VLA策略能同时响应语言与视觉，却未将二者结合以选择任务所需动作，表现为保留源行为或切换到其他已演示任务。作者用行为分析、内部读出与干预刻画该现象，并从模仿目标的角度证明窄条件动作支撑会使指令对齐解与场景对齐解在演示上不可区分。据此提出Equivariant Counterfactual Training (ECT)，在数据层面提供"同一指令、不同动作"的反事实演示，在损失层面将配对演示置于同一次更新中训练。LIBERO-PRO上π0.5位置交换成功率36%→59%，CALVIN上仅用ECT损失即可提升五任务完成率，真实UR5e在固定演示预算下未见位置成功率8%→88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在分布内任务成功率可超90%，也能容忍不改变所需动作的干扰变化，但在需要不同动作的反事实变化下失败；聚合鲁棒性指标掩盖了"策略同时接收语言与视觉却未将其结合以选择动作"这一特定失效，即指令-动作绑定失败。作者希望显式定位并解决该失效。

Method: 行为分析（细调π0.5与GR00T-N1.7的失败rollout归类）、内部状态读出与干预，建立指令诱导轨迹族、视觉反馈调整执行的解释；对模仿目标做条件动作支撑分析，论证演示数据上grounded解与instruction-keyed解不可区分；提出ECT：数据上构造同一指令在不同可区分场景中要求不同动作的有效演示（counterpart配对），损失上将配对演示放入同一次更新联合训练，实现双层等变反事实约束。

Result: LIBERO-PRO受控对比中完整ECT将π0.5平均位置交换成功率从36%提升至59%；CALVIN上（原始数据已含counterpart）仅用ECT损失即提升五任务完成率，无需新演示；真实UR5e在固定演示预算下未见位置成功率从8%提升至88%。

Conclusion: 指令-动作绑定是VLA在反事实场景下的具体失效根源，源自模仿数据中窄条件动作支撑导致的解不可辨识；通过数据与损失层面的配对反事实训练（ECT）可显著提升反事实鲁棒性，并在真实机器人上获得大幅未见位置泛化增益，说明该问题可通过训练目标而非仅数据规模缓解。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can exceed 90% success on in-distribution tasks and withstand nuisance changes that preserve the required action, yet fail under counterfactual changes that demand a different action. Aggregate robustness scores can therefore conceal a more specific failure, in which a policy responds to both language and vision yet does not combine them to select the action the task requires. We call this failure instruction-action binding. Instructions cue familiar trajectory families, and visual feedback adjusts their execution. Behavioral analyses of fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.7 policies reveal that failed rollouts often retain the source behavior or switch to another demonstrated task. These switches show that language is not simply ignored. Readouts and interventions connect these choices to task-conditioned internal states. Our analysis of the imitation objective shows how narrow conditional action support can leave grounded and instruction-keyed solutions indistinguishable on the demonstrations. This motivates Equivariant Counterfactual Training (ECT), which acts at two levels. ECT data supply valid demonstrations in which the same instruction requires different actions in distinguishable scenes, while the ECT loss trains each demonstration with its counterpart in the same update. In a controlled LIBERO-PRO comparison, full ECT raises $\pi_{0.5}$'s mean position-swap success from 36% to 59%. On CALVIN, where counterparts already occur in the original data, the ECT loss improves five-task completion without new demonstrations. On a real UR5e under a fixed demonstration budget, full ECT raises unseen-position success from 8% to 88%.

</details>


### [299] [EWAM: Emergent Depth-Wise Specialization in a Unified Embodied Model -- From Semantic Understanding through Visual Foresight to Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39973)
*Hao Wang,Jiajun Wen,Jingzhi Liu,Shuoshuo Xue,Zhiliang Chen,Min Lin,Yicheng Chang,Xiaoyu Guo,Yukang Zhuo,Zheng Chong,Yunshuang Nie,Jian Zhang,Weijia Liufu,Qingman Wu,Heming Xu,Bingchang Song,Dantong Wu,Zhiyuan Wang,Hang Xu,Jianhua Han,Bokui Chen,Shen Zhao,Rui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EWAM 是一种以动作为中心的统一具身模型，通过非对称联合注意力让动作 token 在每一层读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来与动作信息，并自发涌现出"浅层看语义、中层看预测未来、深层看动作"的深度分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略强调语义理解，WAM 学习环境动态的预测表征，但已有混合系统通常只把动作计算集中在单一专家上，未能让动作生成真正受益于两类信息源的互补性。

Method: 提出 EWAM：一种动作中心的统一具身模型，采用非对称联合注意力机制，使动作 token 在每一层均可读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来帧与动作信息，而感知专家保持各自独立角色；在两种预训练范式下训练（跨具身机器人轨迹、人类第一视角视频），并引入子任务阶段监督。

Result: 无需逐层监督即涌现出稳定的深度方向分工（浅层语义→中层预测未来→深层动作），跨任务可复现且在不同去噪步下稳定；checkpoint 追踪与因果干预证明该分工是习得的且为动作生成所必需。仿真与真机实验中优于现有 VLA、WAM 及混合基线；人类第一视角数据提升跨具身迁移与真机鲁棒性，子任务阶段监督改善长时序完成率。

Conclusion: 统一具身学习能够自发诱导从语义理解、经视觉前瞻、到动作形成的内部有序递进过程，为动作中心的统一建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies emphasize semantic understanding, whereas world-action models (WAMs) learn predictive representations of environment dynamics. Systems that expose a policy to both sources often still concentrate action computation on a single expert. We present EWAM, an action-centric unified embodied model whose asymmetric joint attention lets action tokens read semantic, current-visual, predicted-future, and action information at every layer while the perceptual experts retain their distinct roles. Without layer-wise supervision, EWAM develops an emergent depth-wise specialization: action queries attend mainly to vision-language features in shallow layers, to predicted future frames in intermediate layers, and to action tokens themselves in deep layers. This handoff replicates across tasks and is stable across denoising steps. Checkpoint tracking and causal interventions show that it is learned and that action generation depends on it. EWAM is pretrained in two separate regimes, one on cross-embodiment robot trajectories and one on human egocentric video. In simulation and real-robot experiments, it surpasses existing VLA, WAM, and hybrid baselines. Human egocentric data improve both cross-embodiment transfer and real-robot robustness, and subtask-phase supervision improves long-horizon completion. Together, these results suggest that unified embodied learning can induce an ordered internal progression from semantic understanding, through visual foresight, to action formation.

</details>


### [300] [Multi-Link Safety Filtering for VLA Policies Around Moving Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40007)
*Yatharth Agarwal,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的运行时安全屏障（shield），用五个椭球覆盖夹爪、腕与前臂，基于RGB-D拟合禁入椭球并借稀疏光流在线跟随障碍物，在仿真与实机上降低VLA策略碰撞并提升安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略即使任务成功，也可能撞倒无关物体，任务成功率不足以证明其在杂乱环境中的部署安全性；且需在不重训策略、满足机载算力约束的前提下实现运行时防护。

Method: 免训练 shield：以五个椭球包络夹爪、腕、前臂；对每条指令运动用一个 barrier program 针对 reset 时由 RGB-D 拟合的 keep-out 椭球做过滤；用稀疏光流传播椭球中心以跟随运动障碍，无需重复检测或重拟合；并裁剪 VLM 前缀、减少 flow-matching 步数以压缩策略调用时延。

Result: 六种仿真障碍运动条件下，碰撞率由 65.62% 降至 27.27%，安全成功率由 29.35% 升至 50.43%；消融显示防护臂体优于仅护末端，跟踪可挽回冻结估计的大部分损失；边缘硬件上五椭球屏障 CPU 99 分位 2.2 ms，π0.5 调用由 343 ms 降至 177.3 ms；真机 SO-101 四任务中，受保护 16 回合有 3 次触碰障碍，未保护为 11 次。

Conclusion: 该工作表明无需重训即可在运行时为预训练 VLA 提供有效的臂体级动态安全防护，兼具实时性与真机可行性，但其范围限于障碍物避碰，非接触丰富任务操作。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy can finish a manipulation task while knocking over objects unrelated to it, so task success alone does not show that the policy is safe to deploy in clutter. We study how to keep a pretrained VLA policy clear of such hazards at run time without retraining it, which requires guarding more of the arm than the end effector, following the hazard as it moves, and sharing onboard compute with the policy. Our training-free shield covers the gripper, wrist, and forearm with five ellipsoids and filters every commanded motion through one barrier program against a keep-out ellipsoid fitted from RGB-D perception at reset. Sparse optical flow then carries that ellipsoid's center along with the hazard, with no repeated detection or refitting. Over six simulated hazard-motion conditions, the shield lowers collision from $65.62\%$ to $27.27\%$ and raises safe-success, task completion without collision, from $29.35\%$ to $50.43\%$. Ablations show that guarding the arm links protects beyond end-effector shielding, and that tracking recovers most of the protection lost when the hazard estimate is frozen at reset. On heterogeneous edge hardware, the five-ellipsoid barrier runs on the CPU in $2.2$~ms at the 99th percentile, and trimming the vision--language prefix and taking fewer flow-matching steps shortens each $\pi_{0.5}$ policy call on the integrated GPU from $343$ to $177.3$~ms. On a physical SO-101 arm across four tasks, the arm touched the hazard in 3 of 16 shielded episodes versus 11 of 16 unshielded ones. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [301] [Identifiable Decomposition of Submovements in Human Hand Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40012)
*Adrian Prados,James Hermus,Ramon Barber,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出子运动可辨识分解方法Sub-ID，用时空核相关作为可辨识性准则并嵌入自适应岭正则，实现高相关/冗余基元的显式歧义检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子运动（submovement）作为人类运动行为的描述性模型长期被假设存在，但现有分解方法可扩展性差，且缺乏判断分解是否具有信息量的原理性准则，尤其在基元高度重叠时易给出误导性结果。

Method: 以基元对之间的时空核相关（spatiotemporal kernel correlation）作为可辨识性判据，将其嵌入自适应岭（adaptive-ridge）正则化中，使优化器偏向低相关解；当基元共线时可辨识性丢失，空间上分离时可恢复，从而在分解过程中显式检测不可辨识情形。

Result: 在合成数据上，Sub-ID 能恢复真实参数分布，而现有方法失效；当基元重叠严重无法区分时，方法显式检测出该歧义而非输出误导结果；并从真实三维长时程运动中成功提取子运动，属于此前方法未覆盖的场景。

Conclusion: Sub-ID 为运动基元分解提供了可辨识性准则与可扩展的分解框架，有望为运动控制研究与模仿学习提供具有生理依据的基元。 

Abstract: Voluntary movements have long been hypothesised to be comprised of discrete primitives called submovements, as a descriptive model of human motor behaviour. However, existing methods scale poorly, and no principled method exists to determine whether a decomposition is informative. We propose a spatiotemporal kernel correlation between primitive pairs as an identifiability criterion. Submovement-Identifiable Decomposition (Sub-ID) embeds this criterion in its adaptive-ridge regularisation, biasing the optimiser toward low-correlation solutions. Identifiability is lost when primitives become collinear and recovered when they diverge spatially. On synthetic data, Sub-ID recovers ground-truth parameter distributions where existing methods fail; furthermore, when primitives overlap too heavily to be distinguished, the method explicitly detects this ambiguity rather than outputting misleading results. Sub-ID extracts submovements from real three-dimensional, long-horizon movements, a regime no prior method addresses. This method has the potential to identify physiologically grounded primitives for motor control research and imitation learning.

</details>


### [302] [BatSLAM 2.0: Sequence-Verified Sonar Place Recognition in a Robust Pose Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40085)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BatSLAM 2.0：仅用仿生双耳声呐的 SLAM 系统，由改进声学前端、序列验证器与因子图位姿图组成，在仿真与真实录音中实现鲁棒拓扑建图并抑制地图坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 BatSLAM 依赖声呐信号做地点识别，但声呐地点识别本身具有歧义性：走廊等结构化场景产生的回声序列几乎相同，错误的回环闭合会导致拓扑地图坍塌。因此需要一个能抗歧义、可扩展的声呐 SLAM 方案。

Method: BatSLAM 2.0 由三部分组成：(1) 更新的声学前端；(2) 序列验证器，用于跟踪并验证回环闭合候选，以过滤声呐歧义导致的假回环；(3) 在高效因子图框架上实现的位姿图 (pose graph) 后端。系统在仿真与真实世界录音数据上进行了评估。

Result: 在仿真和真实录音两种条件下，BatSLAM 2.0 均能稳健地创建拓扑地图，有效抵抗回环误匹配造成的地图坍塌，并支持地图规模的良好扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，引入序列级回环验证与因子图后端可显著提升声呐建图的鲁棒性与可扩展性，为纯声呐 SLAM 提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Echolocating bats can navigate dark and cluttered spaces using echolocation. Over a decade ago, BatSLAM showed that a robot with a biomimetic binaural sonar can build a topological map of the environment, by recognizing places from the received acoustic signals. Sonar place recognition, however, is ambiguous by nature: corridors produce nearly identical echo trains, and wrong loop closure can collapse the topological map. In this paper, we introduce BatSLAM 2.0, a novel sonar-only SLAM system built from three elements: an updated acoustic front-end, a sequence verifier that tracks and verifies loop closure candidates and a pose graph implemented on a high performance factor graph framework. The system was thoroughly evaluated both in simulated as well as real world recordings. In both cases, the BatSLAM2.0 algorithm shows the capability of robust topological map creation, countering map collapse, and robust scaling of map size.

</details>


### [303] [Passive Stiffness Shaping in Cable-Suspended Aerial Manipulation via Movable Compliant Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40102)
*Antonio Franchi,Amr Afifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文把多无人机视为可移动的柔顺锚点，建立了重力感知的准静态理论，用于预测并主动塑造悬吊负载的被动笛卡尔刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索悬吊式空中操作具有轻量化、可协同搬运与物理交互的优势，但负载端被动机械响应尚缺乏系统理解与利用。作者希望将空中飞行器的柔顺性作为设计变量，实现对负载刚度的可预测、可调控。

Method: 对任意数量无人机通过绷直、不可伸长的缆索连接单点负载的构型建模；在重力加载平衡点处，将每条支腿视为锚点柔顺与缆索横向几何柔顺的串联，各支腿刚度在负载处并联。在各向同性锚点假设下，证明每条支腿严格等价于虚拟单侧弹性缆索，并给出由平衡张力主导的轴向-横向刚度分解。由此定义"指令锚点构型→被动负载刚度"的非线性映射，并用其微分实现保持约束的局部刚度整形（通过锚点重定位）。最后构建含非线性飞行控制、弹性阻尼缆索与环境接触的刚体动力学验证框架。

Result: 理论推导给出串联-并联刚度组合律、虚拟单侧弹性缆索等价性及轴向-横向分解；微分映射支持约束保持的局部刚度整形；动力学框架用于评估解析模型假设之外刚度的可预测性范围。

Conclusion: 该工作为缆索悬吊空中操作提供了可预测、可塑造负载被动刚度的解析工具，并为后续实验验证给出了明确的动力学测试方案与适用边界。

Abstract: Cable-suspended aerial manipulation offers a lightweight architecture for cooperative transportation and physical interaction, yet the passive mechanical response perceived at the load remains insufficiently understood and systematically exploited. This work interprets aerial vehicles as movable compliant anchors and develops a gravity-aware quasi-static theory for predicting and shaping the passive Cartesian stiffness of a suspended load. The formulation applies to an arbitrary number of aerial vehicles connected to a point load by taut, straight, inextensible cables. At a selected gravity-loaded equilibrium, aerial-anchor compliance and transverse cable geometric compliance combine in series within each leg, while the leg stiffnesses act in parallel on the load. For isotropic aerial-anchor behavior, each leg is exactly equivalent to a virtual unilateral elastic cable, revealing an axial--transverse stiffness decomposition governed by the equilibrium tension. These results define a nonlinear map from commanded-anchor configuration to passive load stiffness, whose differential enables local constraint-preserving shaping through anchor repositioning. A dynamic rigid-body validation framework with nonlinear vehicle control, elastic-damped tendons, and environmental contact is defined to assess when and to what extent the derived stiffness remains predictive beyond the assumptions of the analytical model.

</details>


### [304] [Tactile Curiosity Drives Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40134)
*Klemens Iten,Alexander Proshkin,Bhavya Sukhija,Stelian Coros,Andreas Krause,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacEx 将触觉反馈引入基于认知不确定性的强化学习探索，按感知模态分解模型不确定性并把好奇心导向触觉通道，从而在无任务奖励、无专家演示条件下采集接触密集数据，并用于离线策略学习与 VLA 后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人操作中样本效率低：随机动作采样使大部分训练预算消耗在无接触的自由空间运动上；现有基于模型分歧或认知不确定性的内在动机方法虽优于各向同性噪声，但仍会奖励功能无关的过渡（如自由空间中的杂乱运动）。作者认为触觉反馈是探索的自然信号。

Method: 提出 TacEx 框架：按感知模态（如本体感觉/视觉与触觉）分解模型认知不确定性，将好奇心定向到触觉通道，从而在探索阶段无任务奖励、无专家演示地驱动机器人发现复杂接触动力学。所收集的接触密集数据集支持下游 pick-and-place 策略的离线学习，无需额外环境交互；并进一步用触觉驱动探索对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型进行后训练（VLA 预训练阶段不含触觉反馈）。

Result: 触觉驱动的内在动机使智能体在无奖励、无演示条件下学会操作与抓取物体；由该探索得到的交互密集数据支持离线学习下游 pick-and-place 策略；对初始未使用触觉预训练的 VLA 进行 TacEx 后训练后，下游性能显著提升且样本效率很高。

Conclusion: 将好奇心锚定在触觉上是提升机器人操作强化学习样本效率与接触发现能力的有效手段，并可作为 VLA 后训练的有效数据来源。

Abstract: Mastering robot manipulation skills via reinforcement learning (RL) remains largely sample-inefficient. The most common RL algorithms rely on random action sampling to discover new strategies, resulting in agents that allocate most of their training budget to motions in free space, away from the contacts from which manipulation skills emerge. Existing intrinsic motivation methods based on model disagreement or epistemic uncertainty improve on isotropic noise, but they can also reward uncertainty in functionally irrelevant transitions, such as erratic motions in free space. In this work, we argue that tactile feedback provides a natural signal for exploration, and introduce TacEx, a framework that incorporates touch into epistemic uncertainty-driven exploration by decomposing model uncertainty across sensory modalities and directing curiosity toward the tactile channel. By anchoring curiosity to the sense of touch, TacEx drives the robot to discover complex contact dynamics, learning to manipulate and grasp objects without task rewards or expert demonstrations during exploration. The interaction-dense dataset collected through this tactile-driven curiosity supports offline learning of downstream pick-and-place policies without additional environment interaction. We further use tactile-driven exploration to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models. Although the VLAs are initially pre-trained without tactile feedback, post-training with TacEx substantially improves downstream performance while remaining highly sample-efficient.

</details>


### [305] [Dream4ACT: A Shared Visual Action Interface for Multi-Embodiment Video-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40153)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Jin Xu,Yue Guo,Xin Wu,Yifan Sun,Xiancong Ren,Jianxin Sun,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream4ACT 提出以“动作视图”（action views）为统一的视觉动作接口，用 URDF 正运动学从四个虚拟相机渲染目标关节构型，使观测与动作共享同一视频自编码器与扩散 Transformer，通过掩码流匹配在单模型中支持前向动力学、逆向动力学与联合观测-动作生成，并用无训练的多视图 URDF 约束恢复可执行关节序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型具备强时空先验，可用于具身观测-动作建模，但关节空间动作向量缺乏显式图像空间结构，且不同本体的维度与语义不一致，难以直接复用其先验；末端执行器可视化又无法给出机器人执行所需的完整关节构型。因此需要一个跨本体统一、同时保留本体特定关节几何的动作表示。

Method: 核心设计是共享视觉动作接口 action views：基于 URDF 正运动学，从四个固定虚拟相机渲染目标关节构型，从而把动作映射到图像空间并保留关节几何。观测与动作序列共享视频自编码器和 diffusion transformer。训练采用 masked flow-matching，通过改变被掩码/损坏的未来序列部分，在同一联合训练模型中统一实现前向动力学、逆向动力学和联合观测-动作生成。部署时使用免训练、URDF 约束的多视图恢复机制，从预测的 action views 反解出可执行动作序列，无需学习本体特定的解码器。

Result: Dream4ACT 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上平均成功率达 88.98%，在 TriWorldBench 上总分 65.66；支持有效的闭环操作，并在动作条件下实现有竞争力的多视图预测。

Conclusion: 通过视觉动作接口将关节空间动作统一为多视图渲染表示，Dream4ACT 使视频生成模型的时空先验可跨本体直接用于观测-动作联合建模，并以无训练的 URDF 约束反解保证动作可执行性，验证了世界模型在多本体闭环操作中的可行性。

Abstract: Video generation models (VGMs) offer strong spatiotemporal priors for embodied observation--action modeling. However, joint-space action vectors lack explicit image-space structure and vary in dimensionality and semantics across embodiments, making it challenging to directly leverage the rich spatiotemporal priors of VGMs. End-effector visualizations provide an alternative but do not specify the full articulated configuration needed for robot execution. We present Dream4ACT, a world model built for joint video-action modeling across embodiments. To unify action representations across embodiments, we introduce a shared visual action interface, called action views, which render target joint configurations from four prescribed virtual cameras using URDF-based forward kinematics. This shared visual representation preserves embodiment-specific articulated geometry while allowing observation and action sequences to share a video autoencoder and diffusion transformer. Through masked flow-matching, our model supports forward dynamics, inverse dynamics, and joint observation--action generation within a single jointly trained model by varying which future sequences are corrupted. To recover executable action sequences from predicted action views, we propose a training-free, URDF-constrained multiview recovery mechanism, without a learned embodiment-specific decoder. Dream4ACT achieves an average success rate of 88.98\% on RoboTwin~2.0 and an overall score of 65.66 on TriWorldBench, supporting effective closed-loop manipulation and competitive action-conditioned multiview prediction through the visual action interface.

</details>


### [306] [Rethinking Legibility in Social Robot Hallway Navigation: Impact of Intent Representation and Human Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40158)
*Pranav Goyal,Andrew Stratton,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究社交导航中机器人"可读性运动"生成，通过两项各含45名被试的受控用户实验，在共享MPC框架下比较不同可读性表示与不同行人注意力条件下的导航表现与人类印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有可读性运动生成框架多假设观察者静止且注意力集中，而社交导航中行人是动态的、注意力常被分散。因此需要探究意图表示方式（representation）与人类注意力水平如何共同影响导航性能与主观感受。

Method: 聚焦走廊导航场景，在统一的共享模型预测控制（shared MPC）框架中实现多种可读性公式。研究一（N=45）对比意图表示方式：以"通过侧"（passing-side）为意图 vs 以目的地（destination）为意图 vs 不可读基线，并测试自适应更新的效果。研究二（N=45）操纵行人注意力（分散 vs 集中），检验可读性运动在分心条件下的效果。

Result: 研究一表明，passing-side可读性（尤其自适应更新版本）能带来更平滑的人类运动，并被评价为更有能力、在心理与体力上更省力，优于destination基线和不可读基线。研究二表明，即使行人处于分心状态，可读性运动仍能带来平滑的人类运动，但这一效果并未一致地反映在主观评分中。

Conclusion: 社交机器人导航中的有效可读运动得益于"交互级"（interaction-level）意图表示，此类表示支持人机协调，且部分收益在人类注意力被分散时依然存在。

Abstract: We focus on legible robot motion generation in social navigation settings. Legibility in human-robot interaction (HRI) is often described as the property of robot motion that enables an observer to confidently infer the robot's intent. While mature frameworks exist for generating legible motion in front of static observers, social robot navigation presents a new challenge: the robot must clearly convey its intent while ensuring human safety in dynamic pedestrian environments where human attention is often divided. With the goal of enabling robots to generate legible motion in dynamic and constrained spaces, we investigate how the choice of representation and the level of human attention shape navigation performance and human impressions. Focusing on the ubiquitous and demanding scenario of hallway navigation, we conduct two controlled user studies involving alternative legibility formulations implemented within a shared model predictive control framework. Study 1 (N = 45) investigates the role of intent representation, showing that passing-side legibility, particularly when adaptively updated, leads to smoother human motion and is perceived as more competent and less mentally and physically demanding than destination-based and non-legible baselines. Study 2 (N = 45) examines the effect of pedestrian attention, demonstrating that legible motion allows for smooth human motion even under distraction, even if this is not consistently reflected in subjective ratings.
