Together, these findings suggest that effective legible motion in social robot navigation benefits from interaction-level intent representations that support coordination, with some effects persisting even when human attention is divided. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [307] [PrefPI: Preference-Guided Steering into Out-of-Distribution Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40165)
*Seungeun Rho,Wontaek Kim,Danfei Xu,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrefPI 将偏好学习重塑为"偏好条件生成建模"，利用偏好轨迹定义的隐式条件分布与行为先验的密度比作为偏好信号，并以 CFG 放大后迭代形成偏好引导的策略迭代，使预训练生成式机器人策略在有限反馈下向初始支持集之外的行为迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好学习方法主要在策略已覆盖的模式内做锐化（sharpening），难以获得初始策略几乎不产生的新行为；作者希望突破初始有效支撑集（effective support），在极少偏好标注下实现对预训练生成式策略的有效"转向"。

Method: 把偏好学习形式化为偏好条件生成建模：偏好轨迹定义条件分布，其与更宽的行为先验之间的密度比给出隐式偏好信号，再由 classifier-free guidance (CFG) 放大；反复执行"偏好条件建模 + 引导"得到偏好引导的策略迭代（preference-guided policy iteration）。验证对象包括扩散策略与 PI0.5 flow-matching VLA，覆盖仿真与真机。

Result: 在扩散策略和 PI0.5 flow-matching VLA 上、仿真与真实世界中均产生显著行为偏移，且所需反馈很少；真机上仅用 150 条偏好标注轨迹，将物体搬运高度从 10.7 cm 提升到 19.8 cm。

Conclusion: 偏好可作为一种隐式条件信号嵌入生成式策略的采样过程，配合 CFG 与迭代即可实现超出初始支撑的行为迁移，为预训练生成式机器人策略的低成本、可迭代对齐提供可行路径；真机定量增益为进一步验证提供基础。

Abstract: We present PrefPI (Preference-Guided Policy Iteration), an iterative framework for steering pretrained generative robot policies using only relative preferences over self-generated trajectories. Unlike prior preference-learning methods that primarily sharpen modes already represented by the policy, we study steering beyond the initial effective support, where desired behaviors are rarely or never observed under the initial policy. Our key idea is to formulate preference learning as preference-conditioned generative modeling: preferred trajectories define a conditional distribution, whose density ratio with the broader behavior prior provides an implicit preference signal amplified by classifier-free guidance (CFG). Repeating this preference-conditioned modeling and guidance step yields a form of preference-guided policy iteration, turning incremental improvements toward previously inaccessible behaviors. Across diffusion policies and the PI0.5 flow- matching VLA in simulation and the real world, PrefPI produces substantial behavioral shifts with limited feedback. In particular, PrefPI increases object transport height from 10.7 cm to 19.8 cm on real hardware with only 150 preference-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [308] [Social-WM: Safety-Aware Latent World Models for Robot Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40177)
*Zhihao Zheng,Mooi Choo Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Social-WM：从自我中心RGB视频训练隐空间世界模型，通过"名义动作—可执行动作"差异信号实现安全社交导航规划，在Social-HM3D上达63.77%成功率、人碰撞率降至21.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全社交导航要求机器人不仅预测动作的未来后果，还要判断名义动作在当前物理与社交约束下是否真正可执行。既有方法依赖特权人体状态、显式行人跟踪或在线强化学习，代价高且泛化差；作者观察到社交导航经验中存在"名义动作"与"可实现动作"的系统性偏差（自由空间可全速前进，面对行人或障碍则须受限），可将该偏差转化为执行前的安全信号。

Method: 隐空间世界模型规划框架：1) 以动作条件化的未来预测为训练目标，监督信号是每条指令后实际观测到的未来（而非理想后果），从而直接学到安全相关后果；2) 提出"可实现逆动力学"目标，将观测到的隐空间转移关联到实际被执行的动作而非名义动作；3) 部署时通过世界模型想象候选动作，由逆动力学模型估计其可实现性，名义—可实现差异作为执行前的安全信号；4) 学到的动力学与可实现性模型与目标无关，支持位置目标与图像目标两类导航。

Result: 在Social-HM3D上达到63.77%成功率，同时将人类碰撞降至21.67%；零样本迁移到Social-MP3D仍保持较强性能；无需显式行人跟踪、无需特权人体状态、无需在线强化学习。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从自我中心视频中以自监督方式学习"名义—可实现动作差异"能有效充当执行前的安全先验，使世界模型规划在社交导航中兼顾效率、安全性与跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Safe social navigation requires a robot to anticipate not only the future consequences of its actions, but also whether a nominal action can actually be executed under surrounding physical and social constraints. We present Social-WM, an efficient latent world-model planning framework trained from egocentric RGB video sequences. Our key observation is that social-navigation experience contains a systematic discrepancy between the nominal action and the realizable action: a nominal forward action may be fully executed in free space, but needs to be constrained when heading towards a pedestrian or obstacle. Social-WM learns these safety-relevant consequences directly through action-conditioned future prediction, where the target is the actual observed future following each command. We further introduce a realizable inverse-dynamics objective that associates observed latent transitions with the action actually realized rather than the nominal one. At deployment, candidate actions are imagined through the latent world model, and the inverse dynamics model estimates their realizability; nominal--realizable discrepancy then provides a safety signal before execution. The learned dynamics and realizability model remain goal-independent and support both position- and image-goal navigation. On Social-HM3D, Social-WM achieves 63.77% success while reducing human collisions to 21.67%, and maintains strong performance under zero-shot transfer to Social-MP3D, without explicit pedestrian tracking, privileged human state, or online reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [309] [Non-Invasive Inspection of Water Canals Using Dronar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40178)
*Michael Zielinski,Zhizhan Wang,Benjamin Dymond,Reza Razavian,Zhongwang Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发并验证了一种低成本、非侵入式的运河床检测系统"dronar"，将消费级声呐集成到无人水面艇上，无需排空水即可扫描渠底，通过亚利桑那运河三次现场试验证明了其可重复的测深能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凤凰城地区开放混凝土运河是关键输水基础设施，长期会出现衬砌变形、混凝土开裂和渠底淤积。现有巡检只能在四年一次的干渠期进行，人力成本高且无法让维护团队优先处理受损最严重的渠段，因此需要一种无需排水的可快速部署、非侵入式检测手段。

Method: 提出"dronar"系统：将消费级声呐测深仪集成到无人水面艇（USV）上，使换能器保持在水面稍下方随艇航行采集DownScan深度剖面。在凤凰城亚利桑那运河开展三次现场试验，对比同一淤积渠段清理前后的深度剖面，并对清洁渠段进行重复航测以检验测量重复性。

Result: 淤积渠段的DownScan深度剖面一致地浅于同一渠段清理后的剖面，沿航迹的深度偏差最大约15 cm，说明系统能探测到沉积物引起的渠底变化；在清洁渠段上重复航测得到的深度剖面高度重合，证明测量结果在渠床自然变化范围内具有可重复性。

Conclusion: dronar被验证为一种可行的概念验证工具，可在不排水条件下完成非侵入式渠床检测，为水利基础设施巡检提供了低成本、易部署的新思路；后续需在更大范围与更多场景下验证其通用性与定量精度。

Abstract: Open concrete canals play a vital role in water transportation, serving as primary water infrastructure for millions of people across the Phoenix, Arizona, metro area. Over time, the concrete canals can experience a range of issues, including canal lining deformation, cracked concrete, and sediment buildup on the canal floor. Identifying such critical issues is a resource-intensive process, which currently happens only during four-year dry-up cycles. This prevents the maintenance crew from prioritizing operations on the most affected canal segments. To address this issue, the research team has developed and verified an easily deployable and non-invasive method to inspect canal beds without draining the water. This inspection system integrates affordable, off-the-shelf drone and sonar technology (termed dronar). This dronar system includes a consumer-grade sonar system integrated into an unmanned surface vehicle (USV) that carries the sonar transducer just under the surface of the canal water. This paper presents a proof-of-concept demonstration of the dronar system across three field tests on the Arizona Canal in Phoenix. DownScan depth profiles from the sedimented canal segment were consistently shallower than profiles from the same segment after cleaning, with offsets of up to 15 cm observed along the track. Repeated runs over the clean segment produced closely overlapping DownScan depth profiles, confirming that the dronar yields repeatable measurements across the natural variation of the canal bed. These results establish the dronar as a viable proof-of-concept tool for non-invasive canal bed inspection.

</details>


### [310] [Centralized Multi-UAV Exploration and 3D Reconstruction Using Single-UAV Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40208)
*João Félix Mendes,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出集中式多无人机探索框架，基于共享TSDF地图与集中规划，将单机采样规划器扩展至多机；实验表明分离起始部署比联合起始探索更快、覆盖更全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单机探索方法扩展至多机可提升覆盖速度与鲁棒性，但面临一致建图、安全导航与部署策略等挑战，且现有单机采样规划器难以直接复用。

Method: 以voxblox为基础改造建图管线，实时融合多机深度数据到统一全局TSDF；加入机间避障与自过滤机制，避免将其他无人机重建为静态障碍；保留RH-NBVP、KRH-NBVP、AEP、KAEP四个采样规划器核心逻辑，仅做系统级适配。

Result: 在多环境、两种部署配置（联合起始JS与分离起始SS）下评测，SS配置在所有规划器上均取得更快的探索速度与更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 集中式共享TSDF+集中规划可有效复用单机采样规划器实现多机协同探索，部署策略对多机探索性能影响显著。

Abstract: Extending single Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) exploration methods to multi-UAV teams can improve coverage speed and robustness, but introduces challenges such as consistent mapping, safe navigation, and deployment strategy. In this work, we present a centralized multi-UAV exploration framework that enables the use of existing single-UAV sampling-based planners in a multi-UAV setting. The proposed architecture allows multiple UAVs to collaboratively explore unknown environments using a shared global Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF) map and centralized planning. Building on the voxblox library, we adapt its mapping pipeline to support real-time fusion of depth measurements from multiple UAVs into a common TSDF representation. In addition, inter-UAV collision avoidance and robot self-filtering mechanisms are integrated into the system to ensure safe navigation and prevent reconstruction of other UAVs as static obstacles. The framework is evaluated in simulation using four sampling-based exploration planners - RH-NBVP, KRH-NBVP, AEP, and KAEP - whose core sampling logic is preserved, with only system-level adaptations for multi-UAV operation. Experiments are conducted across multiple environments and under two deployment configurations: Joint Start (JS), where UAVs are initialized in close proximity, and Separated Start (SS), where UAVs are initialized in distinct locations. Results show that SS deployments consistently achieve faster exploration and improved coverage across all planners, highlighting the importance of the deployment strategy in multi-UAV exploration performance.

</details>


### [311] [STARS: From Spatiotemporal Dynamics to Social Representations in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40245)
*Nathan Tsoi,Michael J. Munje,Tejas Oberoi,Rishab Maheshwari,Pengen Zheng,Tanush Chauhan,Peter Stone,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SocialNav-SUB基准（VQA数据集），系统评估视觉语言模型在真实社会机器人导航场景中的空间、时空与社会推理能力，发现最优VLM仍不及规则基线和人类共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会机器人导航需要基于稳健场景理解的符合社会规范的决策。VLM具备物体识别、常识推理与情境理解能力，看似契合该需求，但其能否准确理解复杂社会导航场景（如推断智能体间时空关系与人类意图）尚不明确，且此前无工作系统评估这些必要条件。

Method: 构建SocialNav-SUB：面向真实社会机器人导航场景的VQA数据集与基准，提供统一评估框架，将VLM与人类基线及规则基线在需要空间、时空和社会推理的VQA任务上对比。对多种先进VLM开展实验。

Result: 最优VLM与人类答案一致的概率较为可观，但仍逊于更简单的规则方法与人类共识基线，表明当前VLM在社会场景理解上存在关键缺口。

Conclusion: 该基准为面向社会机器人导航的基础模型研究奠定基础，提供探索VLM如何被定制以满足真实社会导航需求的框架；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Robot navigation in dynamic, human-centered environments requires socially-compliant decisions grounded in robust scene understanding. Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit promising capabilities such as object recognition, common-sense reasoning, and contextual understanding, capabilities that align with the nuanced requirements of social robot navigation. However, it remains unclear whether VLMs can accurately understand complex social navigation scenes (e.g., inferring the spatial-temporal relations among agents and human intentions), which is essential for safe and socially compliant robot navigation. While some recent works have explored the use of VLMs in social robot navigation, no existing work systematically evaluates their ability to meet these necessary conditions. In this paper, we introduce the Social Navigation Scene Understanding Benchmark (SocialNav-SUB), a Visual Question Answering (VQA) dataset and benchmark designed to evaluate VLMs for scene understanding in real-world social robot navigation scenarios. SocialNav-SUB provides a unified framework for evaluating VLMs against human and rule-based baselines across VQA tasks requiring spatial, spatiotemporal, and social reasoning in social robot navigation. Through experiments with state-of-the-art VLMs, we find that while the best-performing VLM achieves an encouraging probability of agreeing with human answers, it still underperforms simpler rule-based approach and human consensus baselines, indicating critical gaps in social scene understanding of current VLMs. Our benchmark sets the stage for further research on foundation models for social robot navigation, offering a framework to explore how VLMs can be tailored to meet real-world social robot navigation needs. An overview of this paper along with the code and data can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [312] [GPU-Accelerated Path-Dependent Marginal Information Gain for Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40297)
*João Félix Mendes,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速的路径相关边际信息增益计算方法，用深度缓冲表示历史观测以剔除重叠区域，可在桌面GPU上加速最高118倍，实机实验将95%覆盖时间缩短30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样式自主探索规划器依赖光线投射估计候选视点的信息增益，因计算开销大而假设视点间相互独立，忽略了同一路径上视点的观测重叠，导致增益估计不准确、路径冗余。

Method: 用深度缓冲替代存储并合并已观测未知体素：将候选光线投影到祖先节点的深度缓冲中识别观测重叠、剔除以观测区域；按深度顺序评估规划树以保持视点与其优化偏航角的依赖关系，每层候选节点与光线在GPU上并行处理。

Result: 与基于体素哈希图的精确边际增益相比误差在5-10%以内；桌面GPU加速最高118倍，Jetson Orin NX上最高28倍。集成到两种采样式探索规划器、三个仿真环境中，六个规划器-环境组合中五个的95%覆盖时间下降；真实实验95%覆盖时间减少30%，探索终止更早。

Conclusion: 深度缓冲近似可高效实现路径相关边际信息增益，精度接近精确解且大幅提速，能在仿真与真实机器人上提升探索效率并提前终止。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration demands that robots continuously evaluate candidate viewpoints based on their expected information gain and execution cost. Sampling-based planners estimate this gain by volumetric raycasting and, due to its computational cost, evaluate candidates under an assumption of mutual independence, ignoring the overlap between viewpoints along the same path. This work presents a GPU-accelerated method for computing path-dependent marginal information gain, where instead of storing and merging the observed unknown voxels along each candidate path, previous observations are represented using depth buffers. Candidate rays are projected into the depth buffers of their ancestors to identify observation overlap and exclude regions expected to be observed. The planning tree is evaluated in depth order to maintain the dependency between viewpoints and their optimized yaws, while candidate nodes and rays at each level are processed in parallel on the GPU. The proposed method stays within 5-10% of the exact marginal gain computed using voxel hash maps, with speed-ups of up to 118x on a desktop GPU and 28x on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. The method was integrated into two sampling-based exploration planners and evaluated in three simulation environments, where marginal gain reduced the time to 95% coverage in five of the six evaluated planner-environment combinations. Real-world experiments also showed a 30% reduction in the time to 95% coverage, as well as earlier exploration termination times.

</details>


### [313] [DynaHarness: A Dynamic Physical Harness for Self-Evolving Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40306)
*Haoyuan Deng,Jiebin Liu,Tengxiao Zhang,Langning Yan,Hongye Cao,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaHarness：用共享执行契约把语义推理（慢脑）与物理执行管控（快脑）耦合，并通过失败归因与配对回归检查实现能力的自演化，在 LIBERO-Pro 上把冻结策略的 17.5% 提升到 75.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略提供了有用的动作先验，但长时程操作仍要求语义推理与物理执行之间的协调。二者的时间尺度不匹配：语义推理在更粗的时间尺度上运行，而物理交互在细粒度上发生；同时，回合级（episode-level）的失败信号过于粗糙，无法指出应当修改系统的哪个组件（能力库、执行机制还是语义层）。因此需要一个既能约束执行、又能把失败证据转化为可复用能力修订的中间管控层。

Method: 提出 DynaHarness，一个动态物理执行框架（physical harness），核心是一个共享执行契约（shared execution contract）。慢脑（slow brain）负责提出能力（capability）及其符号参数；快脑（fast brain）负责对命令进行接地与监控，拒绝未解析的动作、替换能力，并在必要时请求重新规划。执行契约对每条被接受的命令施加边界约束，并在解析技能、恢复技能与冻结的 VLA 三类执行路径上记录执行证据。失败归因模块在这些记录中定位故障来源，并指导对可复用能力或执行机制进行定向修订；配对回归检查（paired regression checks）决定修订被接纳还是拒绝，从而闭合自演化回路。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 上，DynaHarness 在 800 个新采样初始状态下取得 75.2% 成功率，而冻结策略仅 17.5%。在相同能力库条件下，完整动态执行达 74.0%，而名义单步重规划（nominal one-step replanning）为 63.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，DynaHarness 的价值在于作为动态物理管控层，治理已有能力在长时程操作中如何被接地、监控与协调，并能将失败证据转化为经验证的能力修订，形成闭环自演化。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies provide useful action priors, but long-horizon manipulation still requires coordination between semantic reasoning and physical execution. Semantic reasoning operates at a coarser timescale than physical interaction, while episode-level failures provide limited guidance on which system component should be revised. We propose DynaHarness, a dynamic physical harness that couples semantic reasoning with physical governance through a shared execution contract and turns failure evidence into validated capability revisions. To be more specific, the slow brain proposes capabilities and symbolic arguments, while the fast brain grounds and monitors commands, refuses unresolved actions, substitutes capabilities, and requests replans when needed. The physical execution contract bounds each accepted command and records execution evidence across analytic skills, recovery skills, and the frozen VLA. Failure attribution localizes faults in these records and directs targeted revisions of reusable capabilities or execution mechanisms. Paired regression checks govern admission or rejection, closing the self-evolution loop. On LIBERO-Pro, DynaHarness achieves 75.2% on 800 newly sampled initial states, compared with 17.5% for the frozen policy. With the same capability library, full dynamic execution reaches 74.0% versus 63.9% under nominal one-step replanning. This demonstrates the value of DynaHarness as a dynamic physical harness that governs how existing capabilities are grounded, monitored, and coordinated during execution. Our project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [314] [Ego4WAM: What Matters When Scaling Egocentric Human Data for Robot Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40341)
*Zhihao Sun,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Haoyi Jiang,Wei Feng,Huiqiang Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Zhizhong Su,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在统一 world-action model (Ego4WAM) 框架下，系统解耦分析自我中心人类数据的对齐度、时长、任务多样性、动作监督与使用策略对下游机器人策略的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类数据是机器人学习的可扩展经验来源，但其人机对齐、行为覆盖与监督信号差异巨大；已有工作显示随数据量增加有正向 scaling，但不清楚哪些数据属性驱动机器人端增益，也不清楚如何在训练全流程中利用这类数据。

Method: 固定模型骨干，在统一 world-action model 框架下控制变量，分别考察人机对齐、数据时长与任务多样性、动作监督（含仅视频无动作标签）以及数据使用策略的影响；并通过真机与 RoboDojo 闭环策略评测验证。

Result: 对齐的人类演示显著提升分布外泛化并降低目标任务的机器人数据需求；数据时长与任务多样性的作用不同；仅视频监督即便无动作标签仍有效，可作为后续视频-动作训练的基础。

Conclusion: 数据的价值不应仅由时长这一单一 scaling 轴衡量，而由对齐度、任务多样性、可用监督和使用策略共同决定。

Abstract: Egocentric human data provides a scalable source of experience for robot learning, but varies substantially in human-robot alignment, behavioral coverage, and available supervision. Existing work shows favorable scaling with increasing human data, but it remains unclear which data properties drive downstream robot gains and how to use such data throughout the training pipeline. We present a systematic study of egocentric human data with different alignment and supervision under a unified world-action model framework. With the model backbone fixed, we disentangle the effects of human-robot alignment, data duration and task diversity, action supervision, and data usage strategies. We find that aligned human demonstrations substantially improve out-of-distribution generalization and reduce target-task robot data requirements; data duration and task diversity affect downstream capabilities differently; and video-only supervision remains effective without action labels, providing a strong foundation for subsequent video-action training. We validate these findings through closed-loop policy evaluation on both real robots and RoboDojo. Rather than treating data duration as the sole scaling axis, Ego4WAM shows how alignment, task diversity, available supervision, and usage strategy jointly shape the value of egocentric human data for robot learning.

</details>
